Inspired by the current form of Large Language Models, future TSFMs may be offered as commercialized, closed-source API services. However, many existing online adaptation methods still rely on white-box access for parameter fine-tuning or gradient backpropagation. This paradigm mismatch raises a question: In black-box online adaptation for TSFMs, what should we learn? We answer this with an insight: the predictive errors of the base model are conditioned on both the input and output of the base model (i.e., the context of errors). To validate this insight, we propose ORCA (Online Residual Contextual Adaptation). We conduct extensive experiments across 5 state-of-the-art TSFMs and 8 datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Furthermore, through ablation studies, we quantitatively analyze the impact of different adapter learning hypotheses on the final adaptation performance in black-box online adaptation. Code available at https://github.com/Fifthky/ORCA.

</details>


### [180] [Provably Safe, Yet Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14536)
*Kai S. Yun,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PS2-RL框架，通过两阶段架构（先训练备份策略隐式构建控制不变集，再通过可微投影层训练RL策略）在保证严格安全性的同时实现可扩展性，在高达10维的机器人控制任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法存在两难困境：软约束方法缺乏形式化安全保证，而具有严格保证的方法依赖显式证书函数（控制不变集），这随状态维度增加扩展性差且产生过度保守行为。

Method: PS2-RL包含两阶段：第一阶段利用提出的safe-arrival价值函数训练备份策略，通过前向积分系统动力学隐式构建控制不变集；第二阶段通过可微投影层对RL策略进行端到端训练，严格施加由备份策略诱导的安全保证。

Result: 在最高10维的机器人控制任务上进行了评估，验证了框架的有效性，表明在此维度区间内先前具有可证明安全保证的RL方法难以适用或不可行。

Conclusion: PS2-RL在保持可证明安全性的同时提升了可扩展性和性能，且对底层RL算法无限制，可集成到任何现有训练流程中。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn policies that optimize rewards while satisfying constraints. Predominant approaches rely on soft-constrained policy optimization, which has achieved empirical success but does not provide formal safety guarantees for the learned policy. In contrast, methods with strict guarantees typically rely on explicit certificate functions, whose construction requires the direct synthesis and verification of control-invariant sets, a process that scales poorly with state dimension and often yields overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we present the Provably Safe, yet Scalable RL (PS2-RL) framework, a novel two-phase architecture for learning provably safe policies in a scalable manner, designed to overcome the key bottlenecks of prior methods. Rather than explicitly computing invariant sets, PS2-RL leverages a learned backup policy to forward-integrate the system dynamics, generating an implicit control-invariant set online. In the first phase, the backup policy is trained with our proposed safe-arrival value function, which characterizes the optimal backup policy for invariant-set construction. In the second phase, an RL policy is trained end-to-end through a differentiable projection layer that strictly enforces the safety guarantees induced by the learned backup policy. By maximizing the volume of the implicit control-invariant set in the first phase, the resulting PS2 policy from the second phase is performant and scalable, while maintaining provable safety. Crucially, PS2-RL imposes no restrictions on the underlying RL algorithm and can be plugged into any existing training pipeline. We establish theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework and evaluate it on robotic control tasks with state dimensions up to 10, a regime in which prior provably safe RL methods struggle or become impractical.

</details>


### [181] [Implicit Variational Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14235)
*Jian Xu,Shigui Li,Wei Chen,Jiacheng Li,Zhiqi Lin,Delu Zeng,Xinghao Ding,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出隐式变分拒绝采样(IVRS)，将隐式分布与拒绝采样结合，通过判别器网络估计密度比来改进后验近似，并引入IR-ELBO作为更紧的变分下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分推断依赖平均场分解，无法捕捉真实后验的复杂性；即使使用神经网络建模隐式分布，其架构约束仍会产生不精确的近似。

Method: 使用神经网络构建隐式提议分布，通过拒绝采样结合判别器网络估计隐式提议与真实后验之间的密度比，以精化近似；提出隐式重采样证据下界(IR-ELBO)作为重采样分布质量的度量。

Result: 实验表明所提方法优于传统变分推断技术。

Conclusion: IVRS通过结合隐式分布与拒绝采样，有效改善了后验近似的质量，IR-ELBO提供了一个更紧的变分下界。

Abstract: Variational Inference (VI) is a fundamental inference technique in Bayesian machine learning for approximating complex posterior distributions. Traditional VI often relies on the mean-field factorization, which can inadequately capture true posterior complexity. Recent advancements have leveraged neural networks to model implicit distributions, offering increased flexibility. However, the practical constraints of neural network architectures still produces inaccuracies. In this paper, we propose a method called Implicit Variational Rejection Sampling (IVRS), which integrates implicit distributions with rejection sampling to improve the posterior approximation. Our method uses neural networks to construct implicit proposal distributions, and rejection sampling with a discriminator network that estimates the density ratio between the implicit proposal and the true posterior for refining the approximation. Towards this end, we introduce the Implicit Resampling Evidence Lower Bound (IR-ELBO) as a metric to characterize the resampled distribution's quality and derive a tighter variational lower bound. Experimental results demonstrate that our method outperforms traditional variational inference techniques.

</details>


### [182] [Where Black-box Drug-Target Interaction Prediction Models Look: Cross-Method Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14245)
*Ali Vefghi,Zahed Rahmati,Mohammad Akbari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对BridgeDPI架构在三个DTI/DTA数据集上进行了可解释性审计，结合多种梯度归因方法和消融分析，发现解释结果揭示了模态主导性、填充/特殊标记伪影、数据集依赖的协同/抑制效应以及化学一致的片段基序，强调可解释性作为模型批判工具的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DTI/DTA预测器在基准测试中表现优异，但其内部对序列、指纹和图特征的使用方式仍然不透明，缺乏对模型决策过程的深入理解。

Method: 对BridgeDPI架构在Gao、Human、C.elegans三个数据集上进行审计，组合使用Integrated Gradients、Saliency、LRP、SmoothGrad、SmoothGrad-IG五种梯度归因方法，结合特征级遮挡消融和严格交集共识以减少单解释器偏差，分析原始输入、桥接相似性支架和图卷积层的敏感性与符号效应。

Result: 可解释性揭示了：模态主导性、填充和特殊标记伪影、数据集依赖的层间协同与抑制效应、以及化学一致的片段和组成基序（在方法一致处）。

Conclusion: 这些分析不能替代结构或实验真值，但可为计算药物发现流程中的下游验证提供可测试假设；现代XAI应用于DTI/DTA模型仍处于早期阶段，但已能帮助研究者关联预测与药物/靶点表征，并优先安排外部验证。

Abstract: Drug-target interaction (DTI) and affinity (DTA) predictors increasingly achieve strong benchmark scores, yet their internal use of sequence, fingerprint, and graph features often remains opaque. We present an interpretability audit of BridgeDPI architecture on three different datasets including Gao, Human, and C.elegans. This study combines gradient-based attributions -- integrated gradients, saliency, layer-wise relevance propagation, SmoothGrad, and SmoothGrad-IG -- with feature-wise occlusion ablation and strict intersection consensus across methods to reduce single-explainer bias. We summarize sensitivity and signed effects at raw inputs, at the bridge similarity scaffold, and through the graph convolution, including edge-level sensitivities and targeted edge removals. The results show that explainability is most informative when treated as model criticism: it reveals modality dominance, padding and special-token artifacts, dataset-dependent cooperative versus suppressive effects across layers, and chemistry-consistent fragment and composition motifs where methods agree. These analyses do not substitute for structural or experimental ground truth, yet they can provide testable hypotheses for downstream validation in computational drug discovery pipelines. More broadly, applying modern XAI to contemporary DTI/DTA models is still an early pass over the rich structure implicit in trained weights and data -- yet even this first layer of scrutiny already helps researchers relate predictions to drug- and target-side representations and to prioritize external validation.

</details>


### [183] [Beyond a Single Explanation of the Adam--SGD Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14259)
*Chenxiang Zhang,Rustem Islamov,Enea Monzio Compagnoni,Jun Pang,Aurelien Lucchi,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过控制实验研究，发现Adam与SGD之间的性能差距并非由单一因素导致，而是数据与架构的复杂交互作用。然而，存在一个"交叉批次大小"现象——随着批次大小增加，相对优势从SGD转向Adam，该现象被理论模型所捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究提出了多个可能导致Adam与SGD性能差距的因素（数据、架构设计、优化性质），但这些解释通常孤立研究，其相对重要性尚不明确。本文旨在通过控制实验系统性地重新审视这些假设，厘清各因素的相对贡献。

Method: 在视觉、语言、基因组学和图任务上，覆盖现代与经典架构，通过精心设计的训练设置进行控制性实证研究，并构建理论模型解释批次大小相关的交叉现象。

Result: (1) Adam优势在均匀词汇分布下持续存在，但在重尾分布下几乎消失；(2) 在softmax注意力模型中，SGD反超Adam；(3) 将ReLU替换为GeLU时，Adam优势扩大。同时观察到一致的"交叉批次大小"模式。

Conclusion: Adam-SGD差距源于数据与架构的非平凡交互，而非单一因素。所提出的批次大小依赖的交叉模型能有效弥合多种现有假设，并为不同领域的实践提供指导。

Abstract: Prior work has identified several factors that can contribute to the performance gap between Adam and SGD, spanning data aspects, architecture design, and optimization properties. Yet these explanations are often studied in isolation, leaving their relative importance unclear. In this work, we revisit these hypotheses through a controlled empirical study across vision, language, genomics, and graph tasks, spanning modern and classical architectures, and carefully designed training setups. Our results suggest that no single factor consistently explains the Adam--SGD gap. For instance, the Adam advantage can (1) persist under a uniform vocabulary distribution yet nearly disappear under a heavy-tailed one; (2) reverse in favor of SGD in softmax-attention models; and (3) become larger under soft architectural modifications, e.g., when ReLU is replaced by a GeLU nonlinearity. This suggests that the gap arises from nontrivial data and architecture interactions, rather than from a single common factor. Yet, we observe a pattern across our settings: a \emph{crossover batch size} at which the relative advantage shifts from SGD to Adam as the batch size scales. These empirical results are captured by our theoretical gap model, which predicts this batch-size-dependent crossover. Our perspective helps reconcile several existing hypotheses while offering practical insights across domains.

</details>


### [184] [DIFF-ERO: A Conformance-Aware Loss for Deep Learning in Process Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14283)
*Johannes De Smedt,Jari Peeperkorn,Artem Polyvyanyy,Jochen De Weerdt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DIFF-ERO，一种可微的随机一致性损失函数，通过在训练中显式引入控制流结构信息（软边转移矩阵与精确率/召回率信号），使深度学习过程分析模型在保持整体性能的同时显著提升结构敏感任务的预测准确性，并使模型内化真实过程结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准深度学习目标函数（如交叉熵）仅优化局部下一步似然，隐式捕捉控制流结构，导致模型虽在词元级别准确率高，但全局行为可能不精确，缺乏对过程模型结构一致性的显式约束。

Method: 提出DIFF-ERO损失函数，基于随机熵一致性（stochastic entropy-based conformance）的可微形式；通过构建批级别的随机转移矩阵，利用软边隶属度（soft edge memberships）使结构精确率与召回率信号直接参与反向传播；该损失与模型无关，可应用于任何最终表示参数化随机转移的模型；在Transformer编码器-解码器架构中实例化，并与交叉熵联合使用。

Result: 在多个基准上与其他损失函数和目标对比，DIFF-ERO在结构至关重要的任务上预测性能显著提升，在其他任务上性能持平；同时，学习到的随机自动机收敛于结构真实值，表明网络内化了过程模型结构。

Conclusion: DIFF-ERO作为一种可微的随机一致性损失函数，能够将控制流信息融入深度学习模型训练，在保持整体预测性能的同时，在结构敏感的任务中显著提升了预测准确性，并使得模型内化的随机自动机趋近于真实过程模型结构。

Abstract: Deep learning has driven many recent advances in process analytics, especially for predictive and prescriptive monitoring. However, standard objectives such as cross-entropy optimize local next-step likelihoods and only implicitly capture control-flow structure. As a result, models can achieve high token-level accuracy while permitting imprecise global behaviour. We introduce DIFF-ERO, a conformance-aware loss function for deep learning models on process data. DIFF-ERO is a differentiable formulation of entropy-based stochastic conformance that incorporates control-flow information during training. Our approach constructs batch-level stochastic transition matrices with soft edge memberships, allowing structural precision and recall signals to directly inform backpropagation. The loss is model-agnostic and can be applied whenever the final representation parametrizes stochastic transitions. We instantiate DIFF-ERO in transformer encoder-decoder pipelines for next-activity prediction and use it jointly with cross-entropy to analyse its theoretical components with respect to convergence. Across benchmarks comparing other loss functions and targets, DIFF-ERO shows improved predictive performance where structure matters most while maintaining parity elsewhere. At the same time, the learned stochastic automaton converges towards the structural ground truth, indicating that the network internalizes process model structure.

</details>


### [185] [Hierarchical ODE: Learning Continuous-Time Physical Prototypes for Early Link Failure Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14284)
*Jiaen Lv,Leran Qi,Shaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 为解决时间序列原型学习中的观测模糊性、离散架构无法解耦随机噪声与连续动态、以及刚性闭集假设无法处理未知多样性等问题，本文提出了一种层次化常微分方程聚类网络（Hierarchical ODE Clustering Network）。该方法利用神经ODE将潜在状态演化建模为连续积分曲线，强化时间连续性以分离平滑特征趋势与噪声，并通过自适应层次机制自主决定原型数量。在不规则采样时间序列的早期链路故障检测任务上，该方法有效提取了底层物理原型，实现了鲁棒的故障检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列原型学习面临观测模糊性、离散架构无法解耦随机噪声与连续动态、闭集假设无法捕获未知多样性等根本性挑战，亟需一种能够处理连续动态且自适应确定原型数量的方法。

Method: 提出层次化常微分方程聚类网络，利用神经ODE将潜在状态演化建模为连续积分曲线以解耦噪声与趋势，并引入自适应层次机制自主确定原型数量。

Result: 在不规则采样时间序列的早期链路故障检测任务上，该方法有效提取底层物理原型，实现了鲁棒的故障检测。

Conclusion: 层次化ODE聚类网络通过连续建模与自适应原型机制，有效解决了时间序列原型学习中的观测模糊性与闭集假设局限，在故障检测任务上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Time series prototype learning is fundamentally challenged by observational ambiguity. Discrete architectures fail to resolve this, as they lack the capacity to decouple stochastic noise from continuous dynamics. Furthermore, rigid closed-set assumptions fail to capture unseen diversity. To address these limitations, we propose a hierarchical ordinary differential equation clustering network, which utilizes neural ordinary differential equation to model latent state evolution as a continuous integral curve. This formulation enforces temporal continuity to effectively disentangle smooth feature trends from stochastic noise, while our adaptive hierarchical mechanism autonomously determines the appropriate number of prototypes without rigid prior constraints. Validated on the early link failure detection task with irregularly sampled time series, the proposed method effectively extracts underlying physical prototypes, thereby enabling robust failure detection. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJ-LNN/Hierarchical-ODE.

</details>


### [186] [Riemannian Metric Matching for Scalable Geometric Modeling of Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14334)
*Jacob Bamberger,Adam Gosztolai,Pierre Vandergheynst,Michael Bronstein,Iolo Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出黎曼度量匹配框架，通过神经网络学习数据的黎曼几何，利用carré du champ算子实现高效推理，速度比k-NN方法快400倍，无需显式构建核或图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据常集中于低维结构，但现有基于图和核的方法在数据集规模和维度增大时计算代价过高，限制了几何估计的可扩展性。

Method: 将carré du champ算子建模为关于数据随机扰动的条件期望，通过神经网络以样本级方式训练，实现常数成本摊销推理，无需显式构建核。

Result: 在精度上媲美或优于基于k-NN的扩散几何估计器，推理速度提升达400倍，支持对高维图像的无图几何分析（此时最近邻方法失效）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展的、无需显式核/图的黎曼几何学习方法，在保持精度的同时大幅提升效率，拓展了高维数据几何分析的可行性边界。

Abstract: High-dimensional datasets often concentrate near low-dimensional structures, but estimating their geometry from samples typically relies on graphs and kernels that scale poorly with dataset size and dimension. We propose Riemannian metric matching: a denoising probabilistic framework for learning the Riemannian geometry of data using neural networks. Specifically, we learn the carré du champ operator, which, using diffusion geometry, gives us access to the Riemannian geometry toolkit for downstream machine learning and statistical tasks. Our key observation is that the carré du champ operator can be formulated as a conditional expectation over random perturbations of the data, which can be exploited for sample-wise training and constant cost, amortized inference without explicit kernel construction. Empirically, metric matching rivals or improves the accuracy of $k$-NN-based diffusion geometry estimators, while enabling amortized inference that is up to $400\times$ faster, and supports graph-free geometric analysis on high-dimensional images where nearest neighbors break down.

</details>


### [187] [More with LESS -- Local Scene Representations for Tactile Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14344)
*Zohar Rimon,Elisei Shafer,Tal Tepper,Daniel Kozin,Alon Malka,Roy Holland,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LESS模型，利用局部编码器的网格结构实现触觉成像，支持手持设备操作、3D重建，并能在单包含物训练后泛化到多包含物场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督触觉成像方法依赖全局非结构化表示和机器人控制，泛化能力差且实用性受限，缺乏手持操作支持。

Method: 采用局部感受野的循环编码器网格建模触觉场景，通过融合编码器状态重建内部结构图像；结合外部位姿追踪和类人触诊数据实现手持成像。

Result: 在单包含物幻影上训练的模型能泛化到多包含物及不同尺寸物体；支持空间不确定性估计；实现了手持触觉成像和完整3D重建。

Conclusion: LESS通过局部组合式设计提升了触觉成像的泛化能力和实用性，拓展了触觉成像在手持设备及3D场景中的应用。

Abstract: Tactile imaging seeks to reconstruct the internal structure of soft objects through touch sensing, with applications in medical diagnosis and robotic manipulation. Recent self-supervised learning approaches have shown promising results, but rely on global, unstructured representations and robot-controlled sensing, limiting generalization and practical use. We propose Local Encoder for Spatial Sensing (LESS), an object-centric tactile representation that exploits the local nature of touch. The tactile scene is modeled as a grid of recurrent encoders with local receptive fields, whose states are fused to reconstruct 2D or 3D images of internal structure. This compositional design enables strong generalization: models trained on single-inclusion phantoms accurately image objects with multiple inclusions and varying sizes. The local structure further supports spatial uncertainty estimation. In addition, we enable hand-held tactile imaging via external pose tracking and human-like palpation data, and extend tactile imaging to full 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [188] [Squeeze-Release: Iterative Pruning with Exact Structural Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14346)
*Roman Denkin,Ida Akerholm,Prashant Singh,Ida-Maria Sintorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Squeeze-Release的结构化精简方法，通过最小化(miimization)将带掩码的稀疏权重矩阵转换为更小的稠密网络，并引入补偿层归一化(CompensatedLayerNorm)以支持残差流中的通道缩减。该方法在保持前向函数一致的前提下，实现39倍(全连接网络)和14.8倍(ConvNeXt-Tiny)的模型压缩，同时保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化剪枝虽然能使权重张量稀疏，但标准实现保持张量形状不变，导致部署模型实际上并未变小。现有方法无法在保持前向函数等价的前提下，将稀疏掩码网络高效转换为更小的稠密网络。

Method: 提出最小化(minimization)方法，将掩码网络精确重写为更小的稠密网络，前向函数在浮点舍入误差内等价。设计Squeeze-Release循环，交替执行剪枝与最小化，中间通过释放步骤将紧凑张量中的精确零位置重新启用为小校准噪声，将浪费的容量转化为可训练参数。引入CompensatedLayerNorm，作为函数保持的LayerNorm替代，将最小化扩展至带有LayerNorm的残差流中的通道缩减。

Result: 在全连接模型上实现39倍压缩，在ConvNeXt-Tiny上实现14.8倍压缩，且精度相当。此外，证明了该重写方法可扩展至Transformer架构。

Conclusion: Squeeze-Release通过结构化重写与循环压缩策略，有效解决了非结构化剪枝后模型实际大小不变的问题，显著压缩了可部署模型，且具备向Transformer等现代架构扩展的理论保证。

Abstract: Unstructured pruning produces sparse weight tensors, but the standard implementation keeps tensor shapes unchanged so the deployed model is no smaller than before pruning. We present an exact structural rewrite, which we call minimization, that converts a masked network into a smaller dense network with the same forward function up to floating-point rounding. The Squeeze-Release cycle iterates pruning and minimization with an intermediate release step that re-enables the exact-zero positions inside the compacted tensors as small calibrated noise, turning otherwise wasted capacity back into trainable parameters. Successive cycles use that capacity to find structural redundancy a single pass cannot reach. We additionally introduce CompensatedLayerNorm, a function-preserving replacement for LayerNorm that extends minimization to channel reduction across LayerNorm-equipped residual streams. Squeeze-Release compresses the deployable network to 39x smaller than the unpruned model on a fully-connected model network and 14.8x smaller on modern CNN (ConvNeXt-Tiny), at comparable accuracy. In addition we prove that the rewrite can be extended to transformer architectures.

</details>


### [189] [When Language Representations Interact: Separability and Cross-Lingual Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14347)
*Boris Marinov,Angira Sharma,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Philip Torr,Anisoara Calinescu,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多语言大语言模型进行因果-几何分析，发现语言概念在协方差调整的内积下具有近似线性且可分离的表征，同时语系内语言呈现类似单纯形的层次几何结构，揭示了多语言模型中语言身份如何作为近似独立因子存在，以及何时出现结构化依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多语言模型中语言身份的内部表征机制及其相互作用，以确保多语言系统在监控或干预时的可靠行为，尤其是在语言身份具有相关性和层次结构的情况下，现有因果-几何框架是否适用于多语言场景尚待探索。

Method: 对三个多语言LLM模型进行因果-几何分析，研究28种双语对比，通过协方差调整的因果内积评估语言表征的线性可分性，并分析语系内的几何结构。

Result: 语言概念存在稳定的线性表征，在因果内积下高度可分离；结构化偏离反映了语言相似性；同一语系（如日耳曼语、罗曼语）的语言呈现类似单纯形的几何结构，表明层次化组织。

Conclusion: 因果-几何可解释性框架可扩展至多语言场景，语言间的残差结构可能导致跨语言意外效应，对可信部署具有重要启示，提示需诊断概念间结构化依赖何时及如何出现。

Abstract: Large language models exhibit strong multilingual capabilities, however, their internal representations are difficult to interpret. Understanding these interactions is important for ensuring reliable behavior in multilingual systems. Recent work has shown that causal-geometric structure can explain how certain concepts are encoded as approximately linear and separable directions, but whether this framework extends to multilingual models, where language identity is correlated and hierarchical, is underexplored. We apply causal-geometric analysis to multilingual LLMs, studying 28 bilingual contrasts across three models, allowing us to analyze when languages behave as approximately independent factors and when structured dependencies persist. We find evidence that language concepts admit stable linear representations that are largely separable under a covariance-adjusted (causal) inner product, with structured deviations reflecting linguistic similarity. Moreover, languages within the same family (such as Germanic or Romance) exhibit a simplex-like geometric structure, suggesting hierarchical organization. These results extend causal-geometric interpretability to multilingual settings and provide insight into how separability and similarity may exist in multilingual LLM representations, motivating interpretability analyses that diagnose when and how structured dependencies between concepts can be anticipated. This has implications for trustworthy deployment, as residual structure between languages may lead to unintended cross-lingual effects when models are monitored or intervened upon.

</details>


### [190] [Can Deep Neural Networks Improve Compression of Very Large Scientific Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14353)
*Muhannad Alhumaidi,Guozhong Li,Spiros Skiadopoulos,Panos Kalnis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将现代机器学习模型（如VAEformer、GraphCast、Aurora）作为预测器集成到传统误差有界压缩流水线中，以提升科学数据压缩效果。尽管ML预测器能生成更准确的预测，在高度可预测变量上可将重建质量提升91%、压缩比提升9.6倍，但在整个数据集层面上并未改善压缩比。研究发现，预测精度本身不足以决定压缩效果，残差的空间结构对熵编码效率起决定性作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学数据体量快速增长，误差有界压缩是关键管理技术。传统压缩器依赖预测-残差范式，预测质量直接影响压缩效果。研究者希望探索现代机器学习模型能否作为更优预测器，但开发压缩专用的ML模型需要大量资源，因此借助气候领域已有高精度预训练基础模型（如天气预测模型）作为试验平台。

Method: 将空间和时间深度学习模型集成到传统误差有界压缩流水线中，支持自回归预测模型并避免误差累积。使用ERA5气候数据（约1.7 TB），评估三种ML预测器：CRA5（VAEformer）、GraphCast（图神经网络）、Aurora（视觉Transformer），与最先进压缩器SZ3.1在相同量化和熵编码后端下对比。

Result: ML预测器在高度可预测变量上重建质量提升最高91%，压缩比提升最高9.6倍。但在整个数据集层面，ML预测器并未带来压缩比的整体改善。预测精度更高并不自动带来更好的压缩效果。

Conclusion: 预测精度本身不足以决定压缩性能；残差的空间结构对熵编码效率起决定性作用。这挑战了"更准预测→更好压缩"的直观假设，为设计压缩专用的ML预测器提供了新方向。

Abstract: Error-bounded lossy compression is a fundamental technique for managing the rapidly growing volumes of scientific data produced by modern simulations and observational instruments. Most state-of-the-art-compressors follow a prediction-residual paradigm, where compression effectiveness depends on the quality of the predictor: more accurate predictions generate smaller residuals that are easier to compress. This observation raises a question: can modern machine learning models serve as superior predictors for scientific data compression? Answering this question directly is challenging because developing compression-specific ML predictors requires substantial resources. Instead, we leverage the climate domain where highly accurate pretrained weather forecasting foundation models already exist, making them an ideal testbed. We present a framework that integrates spatial and temporal deep learning models into a conventional error-bounded compression pipeline. The framework supports auto-regressive forecasting models and avoids error accumulation. Using ERA5 climate data as a representative large-scale scientific dataset, we evaluate three distinct ML predictors: a VAEformer-based codec (CRA5), a graph neural network forecaster (GraphCast), and a vision-transformer forecaster (Aurora), against the state-of-the-art compressor SZ3.1 under identical quantization and entropy-coding backends. Our evaluation over approximately 1.7 TB of data reveals a surprising result: although ML predictors generate more accurate predictions and can improve reconstruction quality by up to 91% while achieving up to 9.6x higher compression ratios for highly predictable variables, they do not improve overall dataset-level compression ratio. We show that prediction accuracy alone is insufficient: the spatial structure of the resulting residuals plays a decisive role in entropy coding efficiency.

</details>


### [191] [MUFFLe: Efficient Model Update Compression via Generalized Deduplication for Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14354)
*Xiaobo Zhao,Daniel E. Lucani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MUFFLe，一种基于广义去重（GD）的通信高效联邦学习更新压缩方案，通过去重更新向量中的重复模式实现固定比率、可变数量的压缩。在IID MNIST上的初步实验表明，MUFFLe仅需38 MB上行通信即可达到92.93%的目标精度，远优于8-bit量化（75 MB）、Top-k稀疏化（86 MB）和未压缩FedAvg（310 MB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在边缘环境中应用广泛，但传输模型更新的上行链路成本是主要瓶颈。

Method: 将广义去重（GD）集成到FedAvg流水线中，对更新向量中的重复模式进行去重，实现固定比率、可变数量的压缩方案。

Result: 在IID MNIST上20个客户端实验中，MUFFLe达到92.93%目标精度仅需38 MB累计上行通信，而8-bit量化需75 MB，Top-k稀疏化需86 MB，未压缩FedAvg需310 MB。

Conclusion: 证明了将广义去重应用于通信高效联邦学习的可行性。

Abstract: Federated learning is well suited to edge environments but is often limited by the uplink cost of transmitting model updates. This Work-in-Progress paper presents MUFFLe, a communication-efficient update compression scheme that integrates generalized deduplication (GD) into the FedAvg pipeline. MUFFLe deduplicates repeated patterns across the update vector, yielding a fixed-rate, variable-count compression scheme. Preliminary experiments on IID MNIST with 20 clients show that MUFFLe reaches the target accuracy of $92.93\%$ with 38~MB cumulative uplink communication, compared with 75~MB for 8-bit quantization, 86~MB for Top-$k$ sparsification, and 310~MB for uncompressed FedAvg. These results demonstrate the feasibility of applying GD to communication-efficient federated learning.

</details>


### [192] [SemPiper: Interactive Code Synthesis for Semantic Operators in Machine Learning Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14361)
*Olga Ovcharenko,Luciano Duarte,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SemPipes是一种将声明性语义数据算子与LLM能力结合的ML管道编程模型，通过SemPiper交互界面实现可视化、优化和可控的LLM集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ML管道开发中数据准备、特征工程和异构数据集成过程繁琐且易出错，基于聊天的LLM接口对管道行为控制有限，且生成的代码难以优化和集成到生产系统。

Method: 提出SemPipes编程模型，扩展ML管道以支持声明性、LLM驱动的语义数据算子。开发者可用自然语言指定高层操作，同时与标准Python数据科学库结合。在管道训练时，根据数据集特征和管道上下文合成专门的算子实现，并通过进化搜索过程进行迭代优化。

Result: 开发了SemPiper交互界面，可可视化计算图、合成算子实现及进化搜索优化轨迹。支持三个端到端场景，允许修改管道、检查生成代码，并观察语义算子的合成与迭代优化过程。

Conclusion: 声明性语义算子能够实现LLM在ML管道开发中可控、可优化且实用的集成。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) pipelines require extensive data preparation, feature engineering, and integration across heterogeneous sources, making them tedious and error-prone to develop. While large language models (LLMs) have recently shown promise for assisting programming tasks, chat-based interfaces provide limited control over pipeline behavior and often produce code that is difficult to optimize or integrate into production systems. We demonstrate SemPipes, a novel programming model that extends ML pipelines with declarative, LLM-powered semantic data operators. SemPipes allows developers to specify high-level natural language instructions for data-centric operations, while seamlessly combining these operators with arbitrary Python code from standard data science libraries. For the semantic operators, it synthesizes specialized implementations at pipeline training time, conditioned on dataset characteristics and pipeline context, enabling the flexible yet controlled integration of LLM capabilities. We demonstrate SemPipes through SemPiper, an interactive interface that visualizes computational graphs of the pipelines, synthesized operator implementations, and optimization trajectories produced by an evolutionary search procedure. Attendees can explore three end-to-end scenarios, modify pipelines, inspect generated code, and observe how semantic operators are synthesized and iteratively optimized. The demonstration highlights how declarative semantic operators enable controllable, optimizable, and practical integration of LLMs into ML pipeline development.

</details>


### [193] [Be My Tutor: On-Policy Co-Distillation for Mutual LLM Improvement via Peer Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14368)
*Woohyeon Byeon,Jiwon Jeon,Jeonghye Kim,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出On-Policy Co-Distillation (OPCoD)方法，通过两个不同领域专长的大模型相互教学和在线反馈，实现跨领域的帕累托改进，在科学问答任务上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单向蒸馏或单模型微调无法实现跨领域模型的共同进步，需要在保持各自领域优势的同时提升对方领域能力，实现帕累托改进。

Method: 提出OPCoD方法：(1) 每个学生基于自身正确轨迹和来自同伴的反馈进行自蒸馏；(2) 使用认知感知门控决定何时提供反馈；(3) 使用反馈锚定将反馈扎根于问题本身。

Result: 在科学问答任务上，OPCoD在所有评估的领域对和学生组合上均优于基线方法，并实现了帕累托改进。

Conclusion: OPCoD通过双向在线教学机制，成功实现了多领域LLM训练的帕累托改进，验证了跨模型协作训练的有效性。

Abstract: We study multi-domain LLM training in which two models, each stronger in a different domain, co-evolve by tutoring each other through on-policy feedback. Unlike one-way distillation or single-model fine-tuning, our goal is mutual Pareto improvement: each model improves across domains without losing its original strength. To this end, we propose On-Policy Co-Distillation (OPCoD), where each student's self-distillation is conditioned on its own correct rollout and feedback from its peer. To make feedback exchange effective, OPCoD uses cognizance-based gating to decide when to give feedback and feedback anchoring to ground feedback in the problem. On Science Q\&A tasks, OPCoD consistently outperforms baselines and achieves Pareto improvement across all evaluated domain pairs and students.

</details>


### [194] [Discovery under Hypothesis Redundancy: A Geometric Theory of Discovery Bottlenecks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14386)
*Li Xia,Baoxun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出搜索压缩假说，阐明混合发现系统（结合结构化局部搜索与LLM生成的非局部提议）仅在三个几何条件（频谱压缩、从已探索空间的正交逃逸、残差信号与目标对齐）同时满足时才具有优势，并通过合成实验、A股因子发现和符号回归基准验证了这一机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现在假设空间仍很大时会因新假设无法提供独立信息而饱和，亟需理解何时以及为何非局部探索（如LLM引导）能有效提升发现效率。

Method: 提出搜索压缩假说，形式化三个几何条件（频谱压缩、正交逃逸、残差信号对齐），推导混合优势的必要条件，并在受控合成环境、大规模A股因子发现和符号回归基准中测试机制，同时通过信号植入与定向vs随机实验进行消融。

Result: 仅靠新颖性不足：随机正交跳跃虽扩展覆盖但不改进产出；混合增益集中在弱表示但承载目标信号的方向上，当假设空间接近满秩时增益消失。

Conclusion: 该框架将LLM引导发现从通用新颖性搜索转变为诊断程序，用于判断何时需要定向非局部探索。

Abstract: Scientific discovery saturates when new hypotheses cease to provide independent information, even if the nominal hypothesis space remains large. We study hybrid discovery systems that combine structured local search with LLM-generated non-local proposals and pose the Search Compression Hypothesis: non-local exploration helps only when three geometric conditions co-occur: spectral compression, orthogonal escape from the explored span, and residual signal alignment with the target. We formalize these conditions, derive necessary conditions for hybrid advantage, and test the mechanism in controlled synthetic environments, large-scale A-share factor discovery, and symbolic-regression benchmarks; a public tabular operational sanity check tests the associated budget-allocation implication. Signal-planting and directed-versus-random experiments show that novelty alone is insufficient: random orthogonal jumps expand coverage but do not improve yield without predictive alignment. Across compression sweeps, real factor archives, and LLM-SRBench tasks, hybrid gains concentrate in weakly represented but target-bearing directions and vanish as the hypothesis space approaches full rank. The framework turns LLM-guided discovery from generic novelty search into a diagnostic procedure for deciding when directed non-local exploration is warranted.

</details>


### [195] [A Low-Rank Subspace Analysis of LLM Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14388)
*Angira Sharma,Christian Schroeder de Witt,Philip Torr,Anisoara Calinescu,Jialin Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出诊断框架分析LLM中行为干预的副作用，发现不同行为共享内部表征，干预一个行为会非对称地改变其他行为，并揭示了两种几何量度（子空间重叠和决策子空间角度）与干预传播程度的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中针对特定行为（如拒绝、谄媚）的干预常产生非预期的其他行为改变，缺乏精准控制能力，难以设计和实现可靠的安全控制措施。

Method: 将行为建模为激活空间中的低秩子空间，通过分析行为子空间间的重叠（主角的平均余弦平方）以及行为子空间与决策子空间的夹角，研究干预如何在行为间传播。

Result: 在多个指令微调模型（7B-70B）的拒绝、越狱和谄媚场景中：1）不同行为共享内部表征；2）干预效应非对称；3）某些行为是上游控制点，干预广泛传播；4）子空间重叠越大、源行为子空间越接近决策子空间时，干预对其他行为的影响越大。

Conclusion: 行为难以独立修改，干预会通过共享表征和非对称交互传播，这对实现精准行为控制构成挑战。

Abstract: Interventions designed to modify a particular behavior in LLMs, such as refusal or sycophancy, often produce unintended changes in other behaviors. This lack of targeted control makes it difficult to design and implement reliable safety controls. To understand these side-effects, we introduce a diagnostic framework for analyzing interacting behaviors in LLMs. We model behaviors as low-rank subspaces in activation space, and study how interventions influence across behaviors. Across multiple instruction-tuned models (7B-70B) and across refusal, jailbreak, and sycophancy settings, we find that different behaviors share internal representations, and intervening on one behavior alters others in asymmetric ways. Some behaviors act as upstream control points whose interventions propagate broadly across other behaviors, while others remain more isolated. We relate these effects to two geometric quantities: (i) the overlap between behavior subspaces, measured as the average squared cosine of principal angles, and (ii) the angle between each behavior subspace and the decision subspace (capturing the model's final decision e.g., refuse vs. comply). Empirically, intervention effects on other behaviors tend to be larger for behavior pairs with higher subspace overlap, and for source behaviors whose subspaces lie closer (smaller angle) to the decision subspace. These findings highlight a challenge for targeted behavior control: behaviors are difficult to modify independently, as interventions can propagate through shared representations and asymmetric interactions.

</details>


### [196] [Running the Gauntlet: Re-evaluating the Capabilities of Agents Beyond Familiar Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14397)
*Mykola Vysotskyi,Runqi Lin,Grzegorz Biziel,Michal Zakrzewski,Sebastian Montagna,Damian Rynczak,Shreyansh Padarha,Kumail Alhamoud,Zihao Fu,William Lugoloobi,Kai Rawal,Hanna Yershova,Xander Davies,Taras Rumezhak,Guohao Li,Fazl Barez,Baoyuan Wu,Arkadiusz Drohomirecki,Yarin Gal,Chris Russell,Christopher Summerfield,Adam Mahdi,Volodymyr Karpiv,Philip Torr,Adel Bibi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GauntletBench是一个用于评估智能体泛化能力的网页基准测试，涵盖时间感知、图形理解和3D推理三种未被充分探索的能力，以及五个专业应用领域的100个视觉密集型任务。实验表明，最先进的智能体仅达到19.1%的成功率，而人类非专家可超过80%，揭示了当前智能体与人类水平之间的巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常基于简单任务的流行应用，仅关注狭窄的能力维度，导致智能体性能饱和，无法探测其局限性。因此需要构建更具挑战性的基准来评估智能体的泛化能力。

Method: 构建了一个模块化流水线，包括与开源/闭源智能体框架兼容的环境、受控的Web应用、结构化的任务套件和具有多样化指标的自动评估引擎。基准涵盖三个未被充分探索的能力（时间感知、图形理解、3D推理）和五个专业应用（视频编辑器、工作流构建器、3D建模器、飞行分析器、电路设计器），共100个视觉密集型任务。

Result: 最先进的智能体在GauntletBench上仅达到19.1%的成功率，而人类非专家可达到超过80%的成功率。

Conclusion: 当前前沿智能体系统远未达到人类水平的表现，在被忽视的能力和泛化方面存在显著局限性，与复杂真实场景所需能力之间存在巨大差距。

Abstract: As agentic systems continue to evolve and are widely deployed in real-world scenarios, there is a growing demand to faithfully evaluate their capabilities. However, current benchmarks are typically built on popular applications with relatively simple tasks and focus on a narrow set of capabilities while overlooking broader dimensions, resulting in saturated performance on modern agents and failing to probe their limitations. To this end, we introduce GauntletBench, a web-based benchmark for evaluating agent generalisation in challenging scenarios, focusing on three underexplored capabilities (temporal perception, graphical understanding, and 3D reasoning), across five less-covered professional applications (Video Editor, Workflow Builder, 3D Modeller, Flight Analyser, and Circuit Designer), each with 20 vision-intensive tasks (100 in total). Our benchmark provides a modular pipeline that comprises an environment compatible with both open- and closed-source agent frameworks, a controlled web-based application, a well-structured task suite, and an automated evaluation engine with diverse metrics. Contrary to widespread expectations, our empirical results reveal that frontier agentic systems remain far from achieving human-level performance. Even the state-of-the-art agent achieves only a 19.1% success rate on our GauntletBench, highlighting the limitations in these overlooked capabilities and generalisation. By comparison, non-expert human annotators achieve over 80% success on our challenging yet feasible tasks, revealing the substantial gap between current agent capabilities and those required for complex real-world scenarios.

</details>


### [197] [A theoretical model for task routing in mixture-of-expert transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14398)
*Yongli Xiang,Vinoth Nandakumar,Yunzhi Yao,Peike Li,Tongliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过离散语言模型（句法模板和键值字典）首次从理论上证明了单层MoE Transformer可以通过专家专业化实现知识编码，且专家规模仅取决于任务本身的复杂度，为MoE模型中的知识局部化电路提供了理论支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论分析使用连续混合模型，无法有效建模自然语言；亟需使用离散语言模型从理论上解释MoE Transformer中任务-专家专业化现象。

Method: 将结构化知识表示为句法模板和有限键值字典，通过形式化证明单层MoE Transformer可通过查询路由到唯一、任务特定的专家来编码知识，专家规模由任务内在复杂度（句法模板和事实字典的组合大小）决定。

Result: 严格证明了MoE模型中的任务-专家专业化机制；专家规模仅取决于任务复杂度而非模型整体规模；实验验证了不同MoE损失函数下的模型性能。

Conclusion: 为MoE模型中观察到的知识局部化电路现象提供了理论支撑，弥合了离散语言建模与MoE专家专业化之间的理论鸿沟。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) layers enable the scaling of transformer models while keeping the inference compute fixed. While task-expert specialization has been observed in empirical studies of frontier MoE transformer models, existing theoretical work analyzes this using continuous mixture models that cannot be used to model natural language effectively. An important open question is to \textit{theoretically explain task-expert specialization in transformer MoE models using discrete models of language}. To address this, we represent structured knowledge via syntactic templates and finite key-value dictionaries, and prove formally that a single-layer MoE transformer can encode knowledge by using experts that specialize in the corresponding tasks. Our construction shows how queries are routed to unique, task-specific experts whose size depends solely on the intrinsic complexity of the given task (i.e. the combined size of its syntactic templates and factual dictionary). Our construction provides a theoretical support for empirical results on localized knowledge circuits in MoE models. We support our theoretical findings with experiments evaluating model performance under varying MoE loss functions.

</details>


### [198] [Federated Learning for Feature Generalization with Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14416)
*Dongwon Kim,Donghee Kim,Sung Kuk Shyn,Kwangsu Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCONST通过线性凸约束自适应调节全局模型参数更新幅度，解决联邦学习中客户端数据异构导致的过拟合与泛化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中客户端数据异构导致局部模型过拟合本地分布，且可迁移特征在聚合过程中被扭曲，限制了全局模型的泛化能力。

Method: 提出FedCONST方法，基于全局模型参数强度自适应调节更新幅度，利用线性凸约束确保训练稳定性并保留局部学习的泛化能力，通过GSNR分析验证特征可迁移性。

Result: 有效对齐局部与全局目标，缓解过拟合，在多种异构FL环境下实现最先进的泛化性能。

Conclusion: FedCONST通过参数强度感知的调制和线性凸约束，显著提升了联邦学习在异构数据下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) often struggles with generalization due to heterogeneous client data. Local models are prone to overfitting their local data distributions, and even transferable features can be distorted during aggregation. To address these challenges, we propose FedCONST, an approach that adaptively modulates update magnitudes based on the parameter strength of the global model. This prevents over-emphasizing well-learned parameters while reinforcing underdeveloped ones. Specifically, FedCONST employs linear convex constraints to ensure training stability and preserve locally learned generalization capabilities during aggregation. A Gradient Signal to Noise Ratio (GSNR) analysis further validates the effectiveness of FedCONST in enhancing feature transferability and robustness. As a result, FedCONST effectively aligns local and global objectives, mitigating overfitting and promoting stronger generalization across diverse FL environments, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [199] [EM-NeSy: Expectation Maximization for Neurosymbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14463)
*Annegret Seibt,Luc De Raedt,Giuseppe Marra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EM-NeSy将概率神经符号学习转化为期望最大化（EM）算法实例，通过在E步进行概率推理计算符号后验，在M步仅通过神经组件的梯度下降更新参数，无需符号组件可微即可实现近似推理，同时恢复端到端梯度设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号（NeSy）模型要求符号组件可微，这限制了近似推理的使用，作者希望解除这一限制。

Method: 将概率NeSy学习建模为EM算法：E步通过概率推理计算神经预测符号在标签条件下的后验分布；M步基于该后验仅对神经组件进行梯度下降更新。

Result: 实验证明了EM-NeSy的可扩展性和计算效率。

Conclusion: EM-NeSy无需对符号组件进行可微修改或额外适配即可自然扩展至近似推理，且在精确推理下能恢复标准端到端梯度NeSy设置。

Abstract: Neurosymbolic (NeSy) models integrate neural networks and symbolic reasoning for robust and interpretable AI. State-of-the-art NeSy models require that the symbolic component is expressed in a differentiable way, often complicating the use of approximate inference. We propose EM-NeSy which casts probabilistic NeSy learning as an instance of the Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm. In the expectation step, we compute the posterior over the neurally predicted symbols conditioned on the label via probabilistic inference. In the maximization step, we update the neural parameters based on this posterior using gradient descent only through the neural component. This formulation unlocks the full potential of the EM algorithm for NeSy learning. It allows NeSy to extend naturally to approximate reasoning without any additional modifications or differentiability requirements of the symbolic component. Furthermore, it recovers the standard end-to-end gradient-based NeSy setting under exact inference. Our experimental results demonstrate the scalability and computational efficiency of EM-NeSy.

</details>


### [200] [Recipe-Controlled Decoder Audit for Structural Knowledge-Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14492)
*Xihang Shan,Ye Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种配方控制的解码器审计方法（RCDA），用于评估知识图谱补全中编码器与解码器的贡献。通过在固定训练配方下交换解码器（ComplEx vs DistMult），发现解码器效果在大型数据集上差异适中，但在小型知识图谱上成为主要诊断因素，且结果对配方和数据来源敏感。最后提出简洁审计协议：报告解码器行、记录小KG来源、在作编码器声明前扫描解码器×深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱补全研究中，性能提升常被归因于编码器或训练配方的改进，但解码器的影响往往被忽视。本文旨在通过受控实验明确解码器在其中的真实贡献，尤其针对不同规模的知识图谱。

Method: 使用配方控制的解码器审计（RCDA），以ComplEx和DistMult作为受控对比对，辅以RotatE/TransE的定点检查，在7个基准数据集上评估。固定训练配方，交换解码器观察性能变化，并考虑编码器深度、数据来源（provenance）等变量。

Result: 在5个标准KG上，ComplEx vs DistMult差异适中（+0.005至+0.012 MRR），CompGCN风格编码器效果变化更大。在小型KG上，解码器成为主要因素：Kinship上ComplEx稳定优势+0.143 MRR；UMLS在洁净实验下ComplEx优势+0.022 MRR，但在早期变体中反转。解码器选择与编码器深度在WN18RR上存在交互，YAGO3-10上L=0 ComplEx达到0.6971 MRR。

Conclusion: 解码器选择对知识图谱补全结果有显著影响，尤其在小型KG上高度依赖配方和数据来源。建议采用简洁审计协议：报告解码器行、记录小KG来源、在作编码器声明前扫描解码器×深度。

Abstract: We present a recipe-controlled decoder audit (RCDA) for structural transductive knowledge-graph completion (KGC). The audit asks a simple reporting question: before attributing gains to an encoder or training recipe, what changes when the decoder is swapped under the same recipe? Using ComplEx and DistMult as the primary controlled pair, with targeted RotatE/TransE spot-checks, we evaluate seven benchmarks. On five standard KGs, ComplEx-vs-DistMult differences are modest but consistent under our recipe (+0.005 to +0.012 MRR), whereas CompGCN-style encoder effects vary more by dataset. On small KGs, decoder effects become the main diagnostic: Kinship shows a stable ComplEx advantage of +0.143 MRR (6 seeds), while UMLS favours ComplEx by +0.022 MRR in a clean 6-seed server rerun but reverses in an earlier provenance variant. We therefore treat small-KG decoder choice as recipe- and provenance-sensitive rather than as a fixed dataset winner. We further show that decoder choice interacts with encoder depth on WN18RR, and that under our recipe L=0 ComplEx on YAGO3-10 reaches 0.6971 +/- 0.0048 MRR at d=128. The result is a compact audit protocol: report matched decoder rows, log small-KG provenance, and sweep decoder x depth before making encoder-level claims.

</details>


### [201] [EqCollide: Equivariant and Collision-Aware Deformable Objects Neural Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.05797)
*Qianyi Chen,Tianrun Gao,Chenbo Jiang,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EqCollide，首个端到端等变神经场模拟器，用于可变形物体的碰撞模拟。该方法通过等变编码器、基于GNN的神经常微分方程和神经场查询，实现了准确、稳定且可扩展的模拟，在2D和3D场景中 rollout MSE 降低了24.34%至57.62%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可变形物体碰撞模拟方法存在三个主要问题：缺乏对物理对称性的等变性、对碰撞处理的不足以及可扩展性有限。数据驱动方法未能充分利用物理对称性，导致模拟精度和泛化能力受限。

Method: 提出一个端到端等变神经场模拟框架，包含三个关键组件：1）等变编码器，将物体几何和速度映射到潜在控制点；2）基于等变图神经网络的神经常微分方程，通过碰撞感知的消息传递建模控制点间交互；3）神经场解码器，通过查询由控制点特征条件化的神经场来重建速度场，实现连续且分辨率无关的运动预测。

Result: 在2D和3D场景中，相比最佳基线模型，rollout MSE降低了24.34%至57.62%。该方法能泛化到更多碰撞物体和更长的时间范围，并对群作用下的输入变换保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EqCollide通过引入等变性和神经场表示，在可变形物体碰撞模拟中实现了更高的精度、稳定性和可扩展性，为物理模拟领域提供了一种新的数据驱动范式。

Abstract: Simulating collisions of deformable objects is a fundamental yet challenging task due to the complexity of modeling solid mechanics and multi-body interactions. Existing data-driven methods often suffer from lack of equivariance to physical symmetries, inadequate handling of collisions, and limited scalability. Here we introduce \name, the first end-to-end equivariant neural fields simulator for deformable objects and their collisions. We propose an equivariant encoder to map object geometry and velocity into latent control points. A subsequent equivariant Graph Neural Network-based Neural Ordinary Differential Equation models the interactions among control points via collision-aware message passing. To reconstruct velocity fields, we query a neural field conditioned on control point features, enabling continuous and resolution-independent motion predictions. Experimental results on 2D and 3D scenarios show that \name achieves accurate, stable, and scalable simulations across diverse object configurations. It achieves $24.34\%$ to $57.62\%$ lower rollout MSE, even compared with the best-performing baseline model. Furthermore, \name could generalize to more colliding objects and extended temporal horizons, and stay robust to input transformed with group action. Code is available at: https://github.com/AI4Science-WestlakeU/EqCollide

</details>


### [202] [PepALD: Macrocyclic Peptide Generation via Autoregressive Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14510)
*Junming Zhang,Siyu Yi,Wei Ju,Zhonghui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PepALD是一个自回归潜在扩散基础模型，通过结构化化学嵌入、上下文条件扩散、R基团感知环闭合及偏好优化对齐，实现从头生成具有膜渗透性和靶点结合能力的宏观环肽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观环肽是面向细胞内靶点的有前景的治疗候选物，但其设计需要同时控制非天然单体化学、环拓扑、膜渗透性和靶点结合。现有基于SMILES或HELM字符串的生成模型要么在原子级长序列空间操作，要么将单体视为缺乏化学基础化的符号化token，难以满足上述多目标协同设计需求。

Method: 提出PepALD模型：1) 使用结构化化学嵌入表示HELM单体；2) 在化学信息注入的潜在空间中通过上下文条件扩散生成每个残基；3) 在自回归生成过程中预测R基团感知的环闭合；4) 使用胜者保护式扩散适应偏好优化（DPO）将去噪器与亲和力奖励对齐。

Result: 在计算机模拟实验中，PepALD在生成质量和奖励优化性能上均优于代表性肽生成基线方法。

Conclusion: PepALD通过结合结构化化学嵌入、潜在空间扩散生成与偏好优化，有效解决了宏观环肽从头设计的多个关键挑战，为治疗性环肽的自动设计提供了新的基础模型范式。

Abstract: Macrocyclic peptides are promising therapeutic candidates for intracellular targets, but their design requires simultaneous control over non-natural monomer chemistry, ring topology, membrane permeability, and target binding. Existing SMILES- or HELM-string generative models either operate in long atom-level sequence spaces or treat monomers as symbolic tokens with limited chemical grounding. We introduce PepALD, an Autoregressive Latent Diffusion (ALD) foundation model for \textit{de novo} macrocyclic peptide generation. The model represents HELM monomers with structured chemical embeddings, generates each residue through context-conditioned diffusion in chemically informed latent space, predicts R-group-aware ring closures during autoregressive generation, and aligns the denoiser to affinity rewards using winner-protected diffusion-adapted preference optimization. In silico experiments demonstrate PepALD's generation quality and reward-optimization performance against representative peptide generation baselines.

</details>


### [203] [Unsupervised Learning of Efficient Exploration: Pre-training Adaptive Policies via Self-Imposed Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19810)
*Octavio Pappalardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ULEE提出了一种无监督元学习方法，结合上下文学习器和对抗性目标生成策略，在XLand-MiniGrid基准上通过训练前沿能力提升智能体的探索与适应能力，实现了更强的零样本/少样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的下游任务分布广泛，目标任务可能位于预训练分布之外或身份未知，导致零样本求解不可行。受人类发展启发，作者希望智能体能通过自主设定和追求目标来获得可迁移的探索与适应能力。

Method: 在元学习框架中优化多回合探索与适应：(i) 利用上下文学习器实现快速适应；(ii) 采用对抗性目标生成策略，根据智能体适应后性能的演化估计来维持训练在能力前沿，使训练课程动态调整。

Result: 在XLand-MiniGrid基准上，ULEE预训练得到的策略在零样本和少样本场景下均优于从零学习、DIAYN预训练及替代课程方法，并能作为长微调过程的强初始化。

Conclusion: 无监督元学习结合上下文学习与对抗性课程可以显著提升智能体对新颖目标、环境动态和地图结构的泛化能力，验证了该范式在未知下游任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Unsupervised pre-training can equip reinforcement learning agents with prior knowledge and accelerate learning in downstream tasks. A promising direction, grounded in human development, investigates agents that learn by setting and pursuing their own goals. The core challenge lies in how to effectively generate, select, and learn from such goals. Our focus is on broad distributions of downstream tasks where solving every task zero-shot is infeasible. Such settings naturally arise when the target tasks lie outside of the pre-training distribution or when their identities are unknown to the agent. In this work, we (i) optimize for efficient multi-episode exploration and adaptation within a meta-learning framework, and (ii) guide the training curriculum with evolving estimates of the agent's post-adaptation performance. We present ULEE, an unsupervised meta-learning method that combines an in-context learner with an adversarial goal-generation strategy that maintains training at the frontier of the agent's capabilities. On XLand-MiniGrid benchmarks, ULEE pre-training yields improved exploration and adaptation abilities that generalize to novel objectives, environment dynamics, and map structures. The resulting policy attains improved zero-shot and few-shot performance, and provides a strong initialization for longer fine-tuning processes. It outperforms learning from scratch, DIAYN pre-training, and alternative curricula. Code is available at: https://github.com/Octavio-Pappalardo/ulee-jax

</details>


### [204] [Behavioral Audit of Machine Unlearning Has a Privacy Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14518)
*Liou Tang,James Joshi,Ashish Kundu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信息论证明，在互不信任的场景下（模型所有者不诚实、审计者诚实但好奇），仅依赖查询模型行为信号的通用审计方案无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的情况下识别未充分卸载的凸模型，揭示了隐私-审计间的固有权衡；实验验证了该结论在凸模型上的稳健性，并表明该权衡在非凸模型中同样存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器卸载审计工作存在空白：模型所有者可能伪造证据逃避卸载，审计者（或对手）能通过有限访问推断模型及训练数据的隐私敏感属性，但双方互不信任场景下的审计问题尚未被探索。

Method: 采用信息论方法对互不信任场景进行理论证明；在凸ML模型上进行理论推导，并通过实验验证；进一步在非凸模型上实验分析隐私-审计权衡的持续性。

Result: 理论证明：对于凸模型，仅依赖行为信号的审计方案无法在不泄露保留集成员信息的前提下检测未充分卸载；实验验证了该结论在凸模型上的有效性，并表明非凸模型中也存在类似的隐私-审计权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果呼吁在更现实的审计者威胁模型下仔细考量隐私-审计权衡，为设计隐私保护的机器卸载审计方案提供了理论基础和警示。

Abstract: The removal of learned data from Machine Learning models through Machine Unlearning (MU) has been widely studied; however, there has yet to be an agreed-upon scheme for auditing MU. Existing work has shown that a dishonest model owner can falsify evidence to avoid executing MU, while curious auditors (and adversaries) can infer the privacy-sensitive properties of the model and its training data even with limited access. Yet auditing of MU under mutual distrust between the model owner and the auditor remains unexplored. We provide an information-theoretic proof for this scenario: for convex ML models, a generic audit scheme that relies solely on querying the model for \textit{behavioral} signals cannot identify insufficiently unlearned models without revealing membership information of the retained set. Therefore, auditing MU under the assumption of a dishonest model owner and an honest-but-curious auditor faces an inherent privacy-audit tradeoff. Our empirical results on convex models strongly supports this result, while further experiments demonstrate that this privacy-audit tension persists in non-convex models. Our results call for a more careful consideration of the privacy-audit tension under a realistic auditor threat model, and serve as a foundation for more scrutiny of designs of privacy-preserving audit schemes for the MU pipeline. We also release our code implementation at https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit.

</details>


### [205] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过残差化方法研究Qwen3-4B模型隐藏状态中的代码正确性可读性：模型首轮生成代码的正确性可从隐藏状态线性解码（AUC≈0.93），且不受提示长度混淆影响；但修复失败代码时隐藏状态的方向性变化是修复上下文的伪影，而非真正的修复理解特征。方法贡献与实证发现并重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在生成代码前和修复代码时，其隐藏状态是否编码了代码正确性的可读信息，以及这些信号是真正的因果特征还是统计混淆。

Method: 在444个LiveCodeBench任务上使用Qwen3-4B模型，通过线性探针（嵌套交叉验证选择层）从提示最终隐藏状态解码首轮代码正确性；对236个修复案例，分析隐藏状态从失败到修复的对比方向，采用残差化控制提示长度和修复上下文协变量；使用泄漏-free的AUC评估、排列检验和分割样本检验。

Result: 1）首轮代码正确性线性可解码AUC=0.931，残差化后降至0.911但仍远高于提示长度基线0.754；2）修复方向在统计上显著，但残差化控制修复上下文后不再显著，说明是上下文协变量驱动而非真正的修复理解特征。

Conclusion: 模型隐藏状态在生成前编码了代码正确性（正结果），但修复过程中的方向性变化是上下文伪影（负结果）；残差化是控制混淆变量的有效工具，方法学贡献在于同时报告正负结果的诊断诚实性。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [206] [OGPO: Sample Efficient Full-Finetuning of Generative Control Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.03065)
*Sarvesh Patil,Mitsuhiko Nakamoto,Manan Agarwal,Shashwat Saxena,Jesse Zhang,Giri Anantharaman,Cleah Winston,Chaoyi Pan,Douglas Chen,Nai-Chieh Huang,Zeynep Temel,Oliver Kroemer,Sergey Levine,Abhishek Gupta,Hongkai Dai,Paarth Shah,Max Simchowitz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OGPO是一种基于离策略的生成式控制策略微调算法，通过修改PPO目标函数和利用评论网络作为终端奖励，实现了对扩散/流式控制策略的高效微调，在多样化操作任务中达到SOTA性能，且能以极低样本效率从欠初始化行为克隆策略恢复完全任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式控制策略（如扩散/流策略）虽然有效，但缺乏高效的微调方法，尤其在离策略设置下难以实现全局策略改进；同时存在从欠初始化行为克隆策略恢复的困难，以及评论网络过度利用等问题。

Method: OGPO维护离策略评论网络以最大化数据复用，通过修改的PPO目标将策略梯度传播至生成过程的完整轨迹，使用评论网络作为终端奖励；引入成功缓冲区正则化、双边保守优势函数和Q值方差缩减等稳定性技巧。

Result: 在多任务、高精度插入和灵巧控制等操作任务上达到SOTA；是唯一能从欠初始化行为克隆策略几乎完全恢复任务成功率的方法（无需专家数据）；在策略引导和残差学习上大幅优于现有方法。

Conclusion: OGPO有效地弥合了离策略微调与生成式控制策略之间的鸿沟，验证了离策略全策略改进的可行性与关键机制，为GCP的实用微调提供了系统性的实证理解与稳定化方案。

Abstract: Generative control policies (GCPs), such as diffusion- and flow-based control policies, have emerged as effective parameterizations for robot learning. This work introduces Off-policy Generative Policy Optimization (OGPO), a sample-efficient algorithm for finetuning GCPs that maintains off-policy critic networks to maximize data reuse and propagate policy gradients through the full generative process of the policy via a modified PPO objective, using critics as the terminal reward. OGPO achieves state-of-the-art performance on manipulation tasks spanning multi-task settings, high-precision insertion, and dexterous control. To our knowledge, it is also the only method that can fine-tune poorly-initialized behavior cloning policies to near full task-success with no expert data in the online replay buffer, and does so with few task-specific hyperparameter tuning. Through extensive empirical investigations, we demonstrate that OGPO drastically outperforms methods alternatives on policy steering and learning residual corrections, and identify the key mechanisms behind its performance. We further introduce practical stabilization tricks, including success-buffer regularization, two-sided conservative advantages, and Q-variance reduction, to mitigate critic over-exploitation across state- and pixel-based settings. Beyond proposing OGPO, we conduct a systematic empirical study of GCP finetuning, identifying the stabilizing mechanisms and failure modes that govern successful off-policy full-policy improvement.

</details>


### [207] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明传统PCA在保留方差的同时会彻底抹去罕见高风险事件的信息（称为"风险阴影"），并提出Expectile PCA和Tail-Preserving PCA两种方法来解决这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCA以最大化方差为目标，但方差最大化与尾部风险感知之间存在根本性错配，导致高维数据降维时罕见灾难性事件的信息被完全消除，这对高风险决策场景（如欺诈检测）构成严重威胁。

Method: 论文从理论上证明了风险阴影的存在性：PCA可保留超过99.9999%的总方差，同时完全抹去所有关于罕见高风险故障的信号。为突破阴影，提出两种方法：Expectile PCA (ExPCA) 和 Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA)，通过对数据协方差进行重加权，使其偏向高风险事件。

Result: 理论上证明ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息方面严格优于PCA；在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究呼吁在高风险决策场景中从根本上重新审视基于方差的降维方法。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [208] [CARE: Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14581)
*Guanyu Liu,Weiyi Kong,Zeyu Wang,Boer Zhang,Baiqing Li,Peiyu Zhang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARE是一个可审计的控制器，通过保留非LLM优化器作为默认路径，仅在公开证据支持时才授权LLM挑战者策略，在科学实验优化中实现了比现有方法更高的性能（Minerva/Olympus上88.5，ChemLex上92.1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接让LLM控制昂贵且不可逆的科学实验会导致不安全探索和不稳定性能，但完全抛弃LLM的创造力会牺牲大量优化潜力。

Method: CARE保持非LLM的incumbent优化器作为默认动作路径，使用LLM修正挑战者排序策略；通过公开证据干预门（public-evidence intervention gate）在结果揭示前比较挑战者与incumbent，仅当选择前可用证据支持变更时才授权挑战者选择，并将决策记录在审计日志中。

Result: 在Minerva/Olympus基准上，final-best从80.0提升至88.5；在ChemLex基准上，从83.9提升至92.1，均优于所有其他评估方法。

Conclusion: LLM自我进化在可审计控制器下扩展提议空间比直接选择实验更可靠。

Abstract: Granting LLMs direct control over costly, irreversible scientific experiments leads to unsafe exploration and unstable performance, but discarding LLM creativity entirely sacrifices significant optimization potential. We introduce CARE (Controlling LLM-Generated Policies through Auditable Review of Evidence in Scientific Experimentation), an auditable controller for high-throughput experimentation (HTE) optimization that keeps a non-LLM incumbent optimizer as the default action path while using LLMs to revise challenger ranking policies. Before each outcome is revealed, a public-evidence intervention gate compares the challenger with the incumbent. It authorizes the challenger's selection only when the evidence available before selection supports the change, with the decision recorded in the audit log. CARE outperforms all other evaluated methods on Minerva/Olympus and ChemLex benchmarks, with final-best improving from 80.0 to 88.5 on Minerva/Olympus and from 83.9 to 92.1 on ChemLex, relative to the public incumbent. Our experiments indicate that LLM self-evolution is more reliable when it expands the proposal space under an auditable controller, rather than directly choosing experiments.

</details>


### [209] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可分解的注意力偏置机制，结合旋转位置编码，使Transformer-based神经算子能够在零样本情况下泛化到比训练时更大的空间域上，无需改变模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer-based神经算子隐式假设固定域大小，导致模型无法在推理时泛化到更大的空间域上。本文旨在解决这一域扩展（domain extension）问题，即零样本推理扩展到更大空间域。

Method: 通过注意力logits计算中引入可分解偏置来实现空间局部性，该偏置完全可分解为查询-键内积，与优化的注意力内核兼容。结合旋转位置嵌入（RoPE），在保持可表达性的同时实现可控的空间支持范围，且不改变Transformer架构。

Result: 在两个PDE基准和一个3D工业大气流动应用中，所提方法显著提升了到更大域的零样本泛化性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入可分解的局部性偏置和旋转位置编码，能够有效实现Transformer神经算子在更大空间域上的零样本泛化，且无需修改架构。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [210] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，消费级Ampere GPU上的INT8量化扩散 Transformer 实际速度慢于FP8/NF4，原因是"INT8"实现仅做量化后立即反量化为bf16计算，未利用INT8张量核心。作者通过融合Triton INT8 GEMM内核（int8×int8→int32，集成per-token×per-channel反量化与bias）替换线性层，实现2.8-4.2x每GEMM加速，端到端在RTX 3090上1024px图像生成156.5秒，超过NF4(164.5s)和FP8(172.9s)，成为最快变体。该方案仅适用于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100/B200上不及原生bf16/FP8路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示扩散Transformer后训练INT8量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际速度低于FP8和NF4替代方案的软件层原因——现有"INT8"实现仅做"量化-反量化-bf16计算"的伪INT8路径，未利用INT8张量核心的计算优势，并试图通过纯INT8计算内核消除这一性能差距。

Method: 设计一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核，在Ampere张量核心上执行int8×int8→int32矩阵乘法，并将per-token×per-channel反量化与bias融合到epilogue中，按GEMM形状自动调优。将该内核替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中原有的"反量化到bf16"路径。

Result: 每GEMM操作比bf16快2.8-4.2x，int8×int8→int32累加与torch._int_mm位精确，反量化输出余弦相似度1.0且无NaN。端到端在768px下获得~1.1x加速；1024px下单卡RTX 3090耗时156.5秒，优于NF4（164.5s）和FP8（172.9s），无质量损失（PickScore/CLIPScore）。INT8从最慢变体变为最快。

Conclusion: 通过纯INT8张量核心计算，INT8量化在消费级Ampere GPU上可从最慢变体反超为最快，使1024px扩散推理在单卡可行。但该优势具有平台特异性：在A100/B200等支持快速原生bf16/FP8的GPU上，相同内核反而性能不如原方案。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [211] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Operational Threshold Detection and Deployable Dispatch Controller Development in Hydrogen Multi-Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14601)
*Shadi Heenatigala,Hasanika Samarasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于一年高分辨率运行数据，运用统计与机器学习框架对氢基多能源系统（H-MES）进行表征。统计分析发现系统呈二元运行模式（由可再生盈余驱动），太阳辐照度解释了氢气产量45.7%的秩次方差；随机森林分析揭示风速在预测中的非线性主导作用；序列模型利用24小时自相关进行运行预测，强化学习代理优化了氢气收益调度。核心贡献在于证明统计与机器学习方法对H-MES建模与控制具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氢基多能源系统的运行建模与优化控制需要深入理解其复杂运行特性，传统纯参数化方法难以捕捉系统中的非线性动态和交互效应，因此需要结合统计分析与机器学习方法进行综合表征。

Method: 采用统计分析方法（秩次方差分析、多元回归）与机器学习方法（随机森林、序列模型、强化学习）相结合，基于一年高分辨率运行数据对H-MES进行建模，具体包括：1) 统计分析识别关键驱动因素及效应量；2) 随机森林揭示非线性预测关系；3) 序列模型利用自相关性进行预测；4) 强化学习代理优化收益调度。

Result: 1) 太阳辐照度解释氢气产量45.7%秩次方差（大效应）；2) 电力需求呈现弱逆抑制效应（ε²=0.126）；3) 随机森林中风速排名第一但双变量相关性弱（r=0.167），揭示非线性动力学；4) 序列模型利用24小时强自相关（r=0.845）进行有效预测；5) 强化学习成功优化氢气收益调度。

Conclusion: 统计方法与机器学习方法在氢基多能源系统建模与控制中具有高度互补性：统计方法提供可解释的效应量和线性关系，机器学习方法捕捉非线性动态和复杂交互，二者结合能够更全面地表征和优化H-MES运行。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [212] [A Comparative Study of Deep Learning Architectures for Multi-Horizon Behavioural Forecasting for Mobile Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14604)
*Pavlos Nicolaou,Kleanthis Malialis,Artemis Kontou,Panayiotis Kolios*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对六种深度学习架构、两种零样本基础模型及统计基线在三个公开数据集（800+参与者）上进行了系统比较，评估了步数、屏幕使用时间和睡眠时长在1-8天时间范围内的预测表现，并研究了模型泛化、个性化微调及基础模型迁移性。主要发现：PatchTST在训练模型中表现最优；TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配或超越训练模型；个性化微调使RMSE降低16-60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备和智能手机生成的行为时间序列数据可用于主动健康干预，但目前缺乏对这些数据的现代预测架构的系统性比较，尤其是在跨人群泛化、个性化微调响应以及多天预测精度退化方面的研究尚属空白。

Method: 在三个公开数据集（800+参与者）上，对六种深度学习架构（含PatchTST、TCN、MLP、Transformer等）、两种零样本基础模型（TimesFM等）及统计基线进行基准测试，按特征（步数、屏幕使用时间、睡眠时长）报告1-8天时间范围内的指标，并进行个性化研究和基础模型迁移性评估。

Result: (i) 无单一架构全面占优，PatchTST在训练模型中领先，但第二名（TCN、MLP、Transformer）间无显著差异；(ii) TimesFM在零样本场景下匹配或超越训练模型，尤其在低数据场景下；(iii) 参与者级微调使RMSE降低16-60%，睡眠改善最大，步数最小。

Conclusion: 该研究为移动健康预测中的架构选择、基础模型适用性及个性化策略提供了实用指导，是首个联合评估现代深度学习、基础模型及个性化方法用于可穿戴设备多时间范围行为预测的研究。

Abstract: Wearable devices and smartphones generate rich behavioural time series that can support proactive health interventions, yet systematic comparisons of modern forecasting architectures for these data are lacking. In particular, it remains unclear how models generalise across populations, how different architectures respond to participant-level fine-tuning and how forecasting accuracy degrades across multi-day horizons. We benchmark six deep learning architectures, two zero-shot Foundation Models (FM) and statistical baselines on three public datasets encompassing over 800 participants, reporting per-feature metrics for step counts, screen time and sleep duration across 1-8 day horizons. We further conduct a per-feature personalisation study across all six architectures and assess FM transferability across dataset sizes and temporal granularities. Our key findings are: (i) no single architecture dominates, PatchTST leads among trained models while the three runners-up (TCN, MLP, Transformer) show no meaningful performance difference; (ii) the FM TimesFM matches or exceeds trained models zero-shot, especially in low-data regimes and (iii) participant-level fine-tuning reduces per-feature RMSE by 16-60\%, with sleep benefiting most and step counts least. These results provide practical guidance on architecture selection, FM applicability and personalisation strategies for mobile health forecasting. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to jointly evaluate modern deep learning, FMs and personalisation for multi-horizon behavioural forecasting from wearables.

</details>


### [213] [Expert-Driven Survival Machines: Improving Stratification and Interpretability in Multiple Clinical Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14608)
*Farica Zhuang,Zixuan Wen,Christos Davatzikos,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于混合专家（MoE）增强的自适应深度聚类生存分析框架（AdaCSM），通过路由专家机制动态分配患者到专门的风险预测器，同时保留生存和子类型聚类目标，在多个临床队列上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度生存模型通常为所有患者学习单一共享特征表示，忽视了患者子群体间的重要差异，导致个体化风险分层不够精准。

Method: 提出AdaCSM框架，引入基于路由的专家机制，在参数化生存建模框架内实现条件性专业化，动态分配患者到专门的风险预测器，同时优化生存预测和患者子类型聚类两个目标。

Result: 在多个真实世界纵向临床队列（涵盖不同疾病领域）上与最先进的生存分析和深度聚类模型对比，AdaCSM展现出更优的预测性能，并产生可解释的生存分析结果。

Conclusion: MoE框架通过让模型不同部分关注不同患者模式，实现更个体化的特征表示，有效提升了生存预测的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Survival prediction plays a central role for healthcare providers and clinical researchers. Accurate risk stratification enables early intervention and improved patient management. Most existing deep survival models learn one common feature representation for all patients, which may hide important differences between patient subgroups. In contrast, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework allows different parts of the model to focus on different patient patterns, leading to more individualized representations. Therefore, in this work, we propose a mixture-of-experts enhanced adaptive deep clustering survival framework (AdaCSM) for modeling such heterogeneous survival patterns. We introduce a routing-based expert mechanism that enables conditional specialization within a parametric survival modeling framework. The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [214] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对DiffusionGemma 26B模型的实证分析，发现其解码既非并行也非块自回归，而是呈现从左到右的偏置提交顺序，且这种顺序的强弱取决于分析粒度；模型以大批量同时提交令牌，批次内顺序实质未定义；提交行为具有任务依赖性，且提交置信度仅在数学推理任务中追踪正确性。核心贡献在于方法论层面：揭示了测量解码顺序时必须处理尾部EOS填充、区间混淆、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大批量平局等潜在陷阱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣传为并行非自回归解码器，但实际检查点提交令牌的顺序几乎从未被测量，存在宣称与实证之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在DiffusionGemma 26B模型（基于Gemma 4的掩码离散扩散混合专家模型）的采样器接受步骤中插入钩子，记录每个画布位置提交的时间、顺序和置信度，构建686个提示、六种场景的探测套件进行系统分析。

Result: 解码呈现部分从左到右偏置，但逐令牌级别顺序弱，随分析粒度粗化而平滑增强；模型以大批量同步提交，批次内顺序未定义；结构化JSON以任意顺序提交；提交置信度仅在数学推理中与正确性相关；提交激进，在预算步数内以短时爆发完成；任务准确率与自回归Gemma-4兄弟模型匹配。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的解码顺序不是架构固有的特性，而是测量尺度的产物；方法论上需警惕多种混淆因素，否则会制造出并不存在的解码顺序结论。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [215] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性工具热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过导频扩散检测处理变量的较大跳跃，减弱跨跳跃的传导，并用稀疏图预解器计算生成的控制变量。其观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可接受性过滤。实验表明，在54个合成基准单元中，A-IHF系列在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线，且在图形捕捉分段光滑一阶结构时性能最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制函数工具变量估计器需要一阶段残差而非预测，但高容量一阶段模型可能插值处理变量，导致用于结果方程的残差信息不足。现有方法在灵活控制函数中难以有效提取残差。

Method: 提出A-IHF方法：(1) 将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号；(2) 通过导频扩散检测处理变量的较大跳跃；(3) 减弱跨跳跃的传导；(4) 使用稀疏图预解器计算生成的控制变量。观测选择规则仅用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可接受性过滤。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元中，受保护观测A-IHF的平均结构MSE最低；A-IHF系列在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。性能在图形捕捉分段光滑一阶结构时最强。

Conclusion: A-IHF通过图扩散方法有效解决高容量一阶段模型插值导致的残差信息不足问题，在多种基线比较中表现优越，尤其当图结构能捕捉分段光滑一阶特征时效果最佳。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [216] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化中的探测（probing）模型，允许学习者在每轮进行最多k次成对比较查询（pairwise probes），即使查询带有噪声（以δ概率返回错误比较），也能显著降低遗憾上界，达到O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，该界在T、k、δ上紧（对数因子除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将两类近期工作统一：专家设定中子线性最优专家查询，以及OCO中每轮可用的成对（比较型）反馈。探究有限预算的成对查询能否在完整反馈OCO场景下改进最坏情况遗憾。

Method: 提出了统一的探测模型；利用方差缩减效应量化探测收益；结合连续指数权重的二阶（方差基）分析来推导遗憾上界。

Result: 在OCO中，使用k次δ-噪声成对探测，得到Reg_T ≤ O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，该界在T、k、δ上紧（对数因子除外）。在有限K的专家设定中，得到在所有参数（含d）上完全紧的速率。

Conclusion: 即使亚线性且有噪声的探测预算也能在完整反馈OCO中可证明地改进最坏情况遗憾；遗憾保证随噪声参数δ向1/2（即纯随机响应）平滑退化。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO.
