In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [217] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对有限字典和预算约束下的不确定性集选择问题，提出基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，证明其单调子模性，给出贪心算法的(1-1/e)近似保证及匹配的硬度下界，并提供损失证书和半径校准规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习和优化依赖于不确定性模型的选择，但如何从有限字典中选择子集以构成原子不确定性集，并确保其在评估方向（如梯度、对抗扰动、分布偏移）上的覆盖能力，缺乏系统的理论指导。

Method: 定义原子不确定性集及其闭式支撑函数；提出基于覆盖目标的贪心选择规则；证明目标函数的单调子模性；推导(1-1/e)近似保证与匹配硬度下界；设计损失证书和半径校准规则。

Result: 覆盖目标是单调且子模的；贪心算法获得(1-1/e)近似比且该下界不可改进；提出损失上界证书和带样本外控制的半径校准方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限字典下的不确定性集选择提供了具有理论保证的贪心框架，兼顾覆盖性能与计算可处理性，适用于鲁棒优化中不确定性建模的数据驱动场景。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [218] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于图的因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程近似的通用自适应策略，用于在互联数据中识别最优结构，具有次线性时间复杂度和线性数据量复杂度，并在合成与真实交通数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模互联数据中识别最优结构需要大量采样和计算资源，而学习非线性统计依赖关系虽能提升效率，但非线性关系的普遍性极大增加了复杂度，现有方法缺乏通用且自适应的解决方案。

Method: 开发了结合图基因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程近似的通用自适应策略；建立了次线性时间和线性数据量的理论性能保证；分析了噪声干扰、模型渐进收敛和解空间不匹配等多种不确定性的鲁棒性。

Result: 理论分析表明算法在时间上呈次线性复杂度、在数据量上呈线性复杂度；在合成数据集和真实交通数据集上的数值实验验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架仅需极少的条件假设或先验估计，具有广泛适用性，并提供多种针对特定或扩展场景的修改方案，为大规模互联数据中的最优结构识别提供了有效的通用工具。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [219] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了预训练音频嵌入模型在生物声学中编码的声学特征，发现没有单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间，组合嵌入表现最佳，响度特征最易恢复（R²=0.76），基频最难恢复（R²=0.33），并提供了数据驱动的模型选择指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物声学中广泛使用预训练音频嵌入，但对其编码了哪些声学特征以及哪些特征对特定任务有用知之甚少，这阻碍了透明度并限制了向稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展。

Method: 使用88个eGeMAPS特征，跨越六个分类群，应用线性和非线性回归探针来量化每个模型捕获的声学属性。

Result: 不存在免费午餐模式：无单一模型捕获完整特征空间；拼接嵌入表现最佳；响度特征编码最好（R²=0.76），F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 通过交叉引用特征可恢复性与逐物种特征显著性（NMI），为生物声学任务提供数据驱动的模型选择指导。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [220] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路由专用的双适配器知识编辑方法（\method{}），通过相关性路由器决定是否应用编辑记忆，分别使用编辑适配器（修改目标对象偏好）和局部性适配器（保留/恢复原始对象偏好）处理路由和非路由提示。在三个基准测试和两个7B/8B模型上取得最优概率偏好准确率，消融实验表明收益主要来自编辑注入与离路抑制的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要在更新选定事实的同时保持邻近但不相关行为不变，现有方法主要关注如何写入编辑，但忽略了何时抑制编辑的问题。

Method: 提出\method{}方法，采用路由专用的双适配器架构：一个相关性路由器判断提示词是否应接收编辑记忆；路由后的提示词使用编辑适配器（偏好新对象），非路由的直接提示词使用局部性适配器（保留/恢复原始对象偏好）。适配器采用参数高效方式，在推理时检索编辑记忆。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，\method{}在三个基准测试上取得最优概率偏好准确率：CounterFact 0.8180、zsRE 0.8946、MQuAKE 0.9922。该趋势在Qwen3-8B上一致。消融实验显示：不同数据集的最优路由边界不同（CF需词汇神经路由器，zsRE和MQuAKE需BGE嵌入路由）；收益主要来自编辑注入与离路抑制的分离，而非简单增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: \method{}通过分离编辑注入和离路抑制的双适配器路由机制，在知识编辑中同时实现了高编辑成功率和良好的局部性保持，验证了"何时抑制编辑"与"如何写入编辑"同等重要。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [221] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否属于叠加计算（computation in superposition）的实例。实验发现，CC模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，产生标签中的非预期混合矩阵；模型性能提升与混合矩阵的幅度相关，消除该矩阵后性能消失。学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间，表明混合项主导了解决方案。基于混合矩阵的半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）基线虽未达到训练模型水平，但能复现定性损失曲线并优于先前基线。这些结果表明CC不适合作为叠加计算的玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Braun等人(2025)提出的压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否真正属于叠加计算（computation in superposition）的实例，即模型是否通过神经元的重叠表示来实现高效计算。

Method: 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项，分析混合矩阵对性能的影响；通过特征值分解研究神经元方向与混合矩阵子空间的关系；构建基于混合矩阵的SNMF基线进行对比实验。

Result: 模型性能增益与混合矩阵幅度正相关，消除混合矩阵后性能消失；学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间；SNMF基线能复现定性损失曲线并优于先前基线，但未达到训练模型水平。

Conclusion: CC模型并非叠加计算的有效玩具模型，其性能主要由非预期的混合项驱动，而非通过神经元的叠加表示实现计算。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [222] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于隐藏目标与投影实现的在线库存优化通用原则，证明其在任意有界凸容量集上达到最优遗憾界，并通过范数对齐原理将高维问题约简为一维队列控制，首次得到强凸损失下的多对数遗憾界和动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线库存优化中，历史决策通过库存结转影响当前可行动作集，导致状态依赖性问题。现有方法仅针对单一线性容量约束取得进展，缺乏对一般凸容量集的理论保证。

Method: 提出隐藏目标维护与投影实现原则：由在线学习器维护一个隐藏目标，然后将其投影到当前可行的订货上限集上执行。采用在线梯度下降作为基学习器，引入范数对齐原理，将隐藏目标到可行集的距离（用与投影相同的范数度量）建模为标量队列，将目标移动视为到达、共同需求视为服务。

Result: 1) 将一般凸集上的遗憾界从需求概率的逆平方根依赖改进为逆线性依赖，并证明匹配下界；2) 首次给出强凸损失下的多对数遗憾界；3) 首次给出适应欧几里得路径变动的动态遗憾界；4) 在合成与真实库存数据上验证了理论。

Conclusion: 隐藏目标投影原则对一般有界凸容量集上的在线库存优化是最优的；范数对齐原理将高维状态依赖问题约简为一维队列控制，突破了此前仅适用于乘积结构集的局限。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [223] [Flood and Harvest: The Provable Necessity of Trivia for Generating Valuable Mathematics via the Lens of Language Generation in the Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14688)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Dai Shi,Zheng Gao,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从计算学习理论角度建模AI辅助形式化数学中的价值生成问题，证明验证器（proof checker）能保证正确性但无法替代品味——覆盖未知有价值语句必然伴随无限但渐近可忽略的琐碎输出，并给出了两个严格的信息论下界：有限琐碎生成器的最优覆盖率为α/2，无限琐碎生成器的最优覆盖率为1-α/2，两者之间存在尖锐二分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证系统（如Lean、Coq）能够保证数学证明的正确性，但AI系统生成的大量形式化正确但数学上无意义的语句（"trivial"）成为瓶颈。本文旨在从理论上回答：验证器能否同时保证正确性和价值性？覆盖未知有价值数学知识需要付出何种代价？

Method: 将数学价值生成建模为嵌套语言在极限下的生成问题：形式语言F（可验证）包含未知有价值语言H，H通过核C（文献，密度α）以对抗性枚举方式揭示。输出分为有价值（∈H）、琐碎（∈F\H）、幻觉（∉F）三类。采用计算学习理论中的Angluin条件刻画可学习性，分析有/无验证器条件下的覆盖能力，并建立有限/无限琐碎生成二分法。

Result: （1）验证器不能替代品味：可生成具有广度的语言集合与无验证器模型相同，由Angluin条件刻画；（2）验证器提供可靠覆盖：能断言仅有效语句的同时覆盖所有未见有价值语句，将不可避错误从假阳性转移到琐碎；（3）核心二分定理：有限琐碎生成器最优覆盖率α/2，无限琐碎生成器（即使无限但渐近率趋于0）最优覆盖率跳升至1-α/2，两者均紧，且存在单一生成器同时达到两端；（4）两个最优都在压缩模型中有实例化。

Conclusion: 完美的验证器无法替代价值判断：无限多正确但无价值的语句流不是工程意外而是理论必然，因为要覆盖未记录的有价值数学知识，必须付出无限但渐近可忽略的琐碎语句作为代价。1-α的差距即未被记录的有价值知识"质量"。

Abstract: AI systems coupled to proof assistants now generate formal mathematics at scale, and the gap between what a checker can verify and what a mathematician would value has become the binding constraint. We model the generation of valuable mathematics as nested language generation in the limit: a verifiable formal language $F$, accessed through a membership oracle (the proof checker), contains an unknown valuable language $H \in \mathcal{H}$ revealed only through an adversarial enumeration of a core $C \subseteq H$ of exact density $α$ (the literature). Every output is valuable ($\in H$), trivial ($\in F \setminus H$), or a hallucination ($\notin F$). We settle four questions. First, the verifier is not taste: the collections admitting generation with breadth are exactly those of the oracle-free model, characterized fiber-wise by Angluin's condition. Second, the verifier does buy sound coverage, covering all unseen valuable statements while asserting only valid ones: possible with it, impossible without it; it relocates unavoidable errors from false to trivial. Third, and centrally, a sharp dichotomy on the tight family: generators emitting finitely many trivia achieve optimal coverage $α/2$, while any infinite trivia allowance, even at vanishing rate, jumps the optimum to $1-α/2$ (both tight, for cores presented as the candidate intersection), and one generator attains both ends. The transition is in trivia count, not rate; the gap $1-α$ is the unrecorded mass. Fourth, both regimes instantiate in a compression model of mathematics. A perfect verifier cannot substitute for taste: the unbounded stream of correct-but-worthless statements is not an engineering accident but a provable necessity, since covering unrecorded valuable mathematics requires an infinite, but asymptotically negligible, stream of certified trivia.

</details>


### [224] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多组均值估计中基于最大风险目标的主动学习问题，提出了局部极小极大框架并给出了首个通用下界，该下界将难度分解为预算项、异方差性指标和方差局部曲率三个正交因素，并引入了损失诱导的ℓ1几何和基于表示的实例生成器两种关键工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多组均值估计d臂赌博机中的主动学习问题，研究如何通过自适应分配T个样本以最小化最坏情况下的不确定性指标，填补该目标下通用下界的研究空白。

Method: 发展局部极小极大框架，提出损失诱导的ℓ1几何和基于表示的实例生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算；引入方差局部曲率（VLC）度量假设类内部方差局部变化的信息量。

Result: 首次给出适用于任意有限方差假设类的通用下界，将难度分解为预算项、异方差性指标和VLC三个正交因素；对于光滑类，VLC可视为方差-费舍尔信息的重新参数化且具有闭式解；与最强上界对比显示在广泛范围内达到对数因子内的近最优性，但在高度异质实例中存在系统性差距。

Conclusion: 建立了多组均值估计中最大风险目标的极小极大下界理论，揭示了影响学习难度的三个正交因素，为主动学习策略设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


### [225] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单个角色描述中分离出角色特定的子网络，构建轻量级角色扮演模型，在保持角色扮演性能的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型作为角色扮演聊天机器人虽有潜力，但在同时运行大量NPC等实际应用场景中，为单个角色部署完整通用模型存在严重的计算效率低下的问题。

Method: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，从单个角色描述中隔离出角色特定的子网络（sub-networks），从而构建轻量级角色扮演模型。

Result: 在RoleBench上，Persona-Pruner比现有的SOTA剪枝技术最高减少93.8%的性能下降（以LLM-as-a-judge分数衡量），同时保持通用LLM能力。

Conclusion: 角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一小部分，通过识别角色特定子网络可以实现高效的角色扮演模型压缩，验证了无需为单个人物分配完整通用模型的观点。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>


### [226] [Interpretable Alzheimer's Diagnosis via Multimodal Fusion of Regional Brain Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10966)
*Farica Zhuang,Shu Yang,Dinara Aliyeva,Zixuan Wen,Duy Duong-Tran,Christos Davatzikos,Tianlong Chen,Song Wang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MREF-AD，一种基于混合专家（MoE）的多模态区域专家融合模型，将每个模态内的脑区域视为独立专家，通过门控网络学习受试者特定的融合权重，用于阿尔茨海默病诊断，在ADNI数据集上取得优异性能并提供可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态融合方法简单拼接特征，无法自适应平衡不同模态（如淀粉样蛋白PET和MRI）在不同脑区的贡献，亟需一种能动态权衡区域级模态信息的融合方法。

Method: 提出MREF-AD，基于MoE框架，将每个模态中的脑区建模为独立专家，通过门控网络学习受试者特异性的融合权重，整合表格化神经影像和人口统计学信息进行AD诊断。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上，MREF-AD相比强基线和经典深度基线方法取得了有竞争力的性能，同时提供了模态和区域级别的可解释性分析。

Conclusion: MREF-AD通过区域级专家门控机制实现了自适应多模态融合，在提升AD诊断性能的同时揭示了结构和分子影像对诊断的联合贡献，代码已开源。

Abstract: Accurate and early diagnosis of Alzheimer's disease (AD) is critical for effective intervention and requires integrating complementary information from multimodal neuroimaging data. However, conventional fusion approaches often rely on simple concatenation of features, which cannot adaptively balance the contributions of biomarkers such as amyloid PET and MRI across brain regions. In this work, we propose MREF-AD, a Multimodal Regional Expert Fusion model for AD diagnosis. It is a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that models mesoscopic brain regions within each modality as independent experts and employs a gating network to learn subject-specific fusion weights. Utilizing tabular neuroimaging and demographic information from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI), MREF-AD achieves competitive performance over strong classic and deep baselines while providing interpretable, modality- and region-level insight into how structural and molecular imaging jointly contribute to AD diagnosis. The source code is available at https://github.com/PennShenLab/mref-ad.

</details>


### [227] [Equivariant Representation Learning via Class-Pose Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2207.03116)
*Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Gustaf Tegnér,Anastasiia Varava,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种将潜空间分解为不变因子和对称群本身的通用表示学习方法，通过学习相对对称信息实现等变表示，具备无损、可解释、解耦的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习方法未能充分利用数据的对称性结构，缺乏既能保持等变性又能实现可解释解耦的通用框架。

Method: 将潜空间分解为不变因子（对应数据类别）和对称群本身（对应姿态），利用相对对称信息的监督信号训练等变性损失函数。

Result: 在多种对称性的数据集上，该方法捕捉了数据的几何结构，并在等变表示学习任务中优于其他框架。

Conclusion: 基于群论的理论保证确保了表示的无损性、可解释性和解耦性，为对称性等变表示学习提供了一种通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce a general method for learning representations that are equivariant to symmetries of data. Our central idea is to decompose the latent space into an invariant factor and the symmetry group itself. The components semantically correspond to intrinsic data classes and poses respectively. The learner is trained on a loss encouraging equivariance based on supervision from relative symmetry information. The approach is motivated by theoretical results from group theory and guarantees representations that are lossless, interpretable and disentangled. We provide an empirical investigation via experiments involving datasets with a variety of symmetries. Results show that our representations capture the geometry of data and outperform other equivariant representation learning frameworks.

</details>


### [228] [On the Geometry and Optimization of Polynomial Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00722)
*Vahid Shahverdi,Giovanni Luca Marchetti,Kathlén Kohn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用单项式激活函数的卷积神经网络，证明了其参数化映射是正则的且几乎处处同构（除滤波器缩放外）。利用代数几何工具，探讨了该映射在函数空间中的像（神经流形）的几何性质，包括维数、度数（衡量模型表达能力）和奇点描述。此外，针对一般大数据集，推导了回归损失优化中临界点数量的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示单项式激活函数下卷积神经网络的参数化映射的几何结构，量化模型的表达能力与优化景观的复杂性。

Method: 利用代数几何工具（正则映射、同构、维数、度数、奇点分析）对神经流形进行理论分析。

Result: 证明了参数化映射的正则性和几乎处处同构性；计算了神经流形的维数和度数；描述了其奇点结构；推导了回归损失下临界点数量的显式公式。

Conclusion: 单项式激活函数的CNN具有良好的几何性质，其表达能力可通过流形的代数不变量量化，且优化景观中的临界点数量具有解析表达式。

Abstract: We study convolutional neural networks with monomial activation functions. Specifically, we prove that their parameterization map is regular and is an isomorphism almost everywhere, up to rescaling the filters. By leveraging on tools from algebraic geometry, we explore the geometric properties of the image in function space of this map - typically referred to as neuromanifold. In particular, we compute the dimension and the degree of the neuromanifold, which measure the expressivity of the model, and describe its singularities. Moreover, for a generic large dataset, we derive an explicit formula that quantifies the number of critical points arising in the optimization of a regression loss.

</details>


### [229] [SMART: Scalable Mesh-free Aerodynamic Simulations from Raw Geometries using a Transformer-based Surrogate Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18707)
*Jan Hagnberger,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMART是一个神经代理模型，仅使用几何点云表示而不依赖仿真网格，即可预测任意查询位置的物理量，其性能可与甚至超越依赖网格的现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于机器学习代理模型进行物理仿真时，现有方法依赖仿真网格作为输入，但为新几何体生成仿真网格计算成本高昂；而无网格方法虽然避免了网格生成，但误差通常更大。作者希望结合两者优势，提出一种无需仿真网格但仍能保持高精度的模型。

Method: 将几何和仿真参数编码到共享隐空间中，捕捉物理场的结构和参数特征；物理解码器通过跨层注意力机制与编码器的中间隐表示交互，将空间查询映射为物理量；模型联合更新几何隐特征和演化中的物理场。

Result: 大量实验表明，SMART在性能上与依赖仿真网格作为输入的现有方法相当，且在多数情况下超越它们，证明了其在工业级仿真中的能力。

Conclusion: SMART成功实现了无网格的物理场预测，同时保持了与有网格方法竞争甚至更优的性能，为工业级物理仿真提供了一种高效替代方案。

Abstract: Machine learning-based surrogate models have emerged as more efficient alternatives to numerical solvers for physical simulations over complex geometries, such as car bodies. Many existing models incorporate the simulation mesh as an additional input, thereby reducing prediction errors. However, generating a simulation mesh for new geometries is computationally costly. In contrast, mesh-free methods, which do not rely on the simulation mesh, typically incur higher errors. Motivated by these considerations, we introduce SMART, a neural surrogate model that predicts physical quantities at arbitrary query locations using only a point-cloud representation of the geometry, without requiring access to the simulation mesh. The geometry and simulation parameters are encoded into a shared latent space that captures both structural and parametric characteristics of the physical field. A physics decoder then attends to the encoder's intermediate latent representations to map spatial queries to physical quantities. Through this cross-layer interaction, the model jointly updates latent geometric features and the evolving physical field. Extensive experiments show that SMART is competitive with and often outperforms existing methods that rely on the simulation mesh as input, demonstrating its capabilities for industry-level simulations.

</details>


### [230] [Denoising Score Matching with Random Features: Insights on Diffusion Models from Precise Learning Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.00336)
*Anand Jerry George,Rodrigo Veiga,Nicolas Macris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析揭示了扩散模型中泛化与记忆化的机制，重点研究了Denoising Score Matching中每个数据样本的噪声采样数m对模型性能的影响，并在高斯分布和随机特征网络的设定下推导了测试/训练误差的精确表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解扩散模型中泛化与记忆化现象及其背后的机制，特别是模型复杂度、训练数据集大小以及噪声采样数m如何影响这些现象。

Method: 在高维渐进极限下（d, n, p → ∞，保持ψ_n=n/d和ψ_p=p/d固定），使用随机特征神经网络参数化得分函数，以d维高斯分布为目标分布，推导Denoising Score Matching测试误差和训练误差的渐近精确表达式。

Result: 确定了泛化和记忆化区域作为ψ_n、ψ_p和m的函数，理论结果与经验观察一致。

Conclusion: 噪声采样数m在扩散模型的泛化与记忆化中起关键作用，理论分析能够捕捉并解释这些行为。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the phenomena of generalization and memorization in diffusion models. Empirical studies suggest that these phenomena are influenced by model complexity and the size of the training dataset. In our experiments, we further observe that the number of noise samples per data sample ($m$) used during Denoising Score Matching (DSM) plays a significant and non-trivial role. We capture these behaviors and shed insights into their mechanisms by deriving asymptotically precise expressions for test and train errors of DSM under a simple theoretical setting. The score function is parameterized by random features neural networks, with the target distribution being $d$-dimensional Gaussian. We operate in a regime where the dimension $d$, number of data samples $n$, and number of features $p$ tend to infinity while keeping the ratios $ψ_n=\frac{n}{d}$ and $ψ_p=\frac{p}{d}$ fixed. By characterizing the test and train errors, we identify regimes of generalization and memorization as a function of $ψ_n,ψ_p$, and $m$. Our theoretical findings are consistent with the empirical observations.

</details>


### [231] [MOSIC: Model-Agnostic Optimal Subgroup Identification with Multi-Constraint for Improved Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20908)
*Wenxin Chen,Weishen Pan,Kyra Gan,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一优化框架，通过将带约束的子群识别问题转化为无约束可微极小-极大目标，利用梯度下降-上升算法直接求解，从而在优化过程中直接融入子群规模、倾向性重叠等约束，优于传统两阶段CATE阈值方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子群识别方法采用先估计CATE再阈值化的两阶段方式，无法纳入临床决策中关键的约束（如子群规模、倾向性重叠），这些约束与CATE估计在不同轴上运作，现有框架难以自然兼容，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出统一优化框架，将带约束的原始问题重写为无约束可微极小-极大目标，通过梯度下降-上升算法求解。理论证明了收敛到可行且局部最优解。框架模型无关，兼容多种CATE估计器，并可扩展至成本限制、公平性等额外约束。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法能有效识别高获益子群，同时更好地满足约束条件。

Conclusion: 该统一优化框架通过直接在优化过程中施加约束，克服了两阶段方法的缺陷，提供了更实用、灵活的临床子群识别方案。

Abstract: Current subgroup identification methods typically follow a two-step approach: first estimate conditional average treatment effects and then apply thresholding or rule-based procedures to define subgroups. While intuitive, this decoupled approach fails to incorporate key constraints essential for real-world clinical decision-making, such as subgroup size and propensity overlap. These constraints operate on fundamentally different axes than CATE estimation and are not naturally accommodated within existing frameworks, thereby limiting the practical applicability of these methods. We propose a unified optimization framework that directly solves the primal constrained optimization problem to identify optimal subgroups. Our key innovation is a reformulation of the constrained primal problem as an unconstrained differentiable min-max objective, solved via a gradient descent-ascent algorithm. We theoretically establish that our solution converges to a feasible and locally optimal solution. Unlike threshold-based CATE methods that apply constraints as post-hoc filters, our approach enforces them directly during optimization. The framework is model-agnostic, compatible with a wide range of CATE estimators, and extensible to additional constraints like cost limits or fairness criteria. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate its effectiveness in identifying high-benefit subgroups while maintaining better satisfaction of constraints.

</details>


### [232] [Fractured Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12992)
*Baohao Liao,Hanze Dong,Yuhui Xu,Doyen Sahoo,Christof Monz,Junnan Li,Caiming Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Fractured Sampling方法，通过在推理轨迹数量、每条轨迹的最终解数量和推理截断深度三个维度上灵活插值，实现了比完整CoT更优的准确率-成本权衡，在多个推理基准上取得了显著的log-linear扩展增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chain-of-Thought（特别是Long CoT）虽能提升LLM推理能力，但产生大量token成本，限制了其在延迟敏感场景中的部署。作者希望找到一种更高效的推理时扩展策略，在保持推理质量的同时大幅降低token开销。

Method: 提出Fractured Sampling统一框架，沿三个正交轴插值：1) 推理轨迹数量（采样多条轨迹）；2) 每条轨迹的最终解数量；3) 推理轨迹截断深度（截断CoT提前生成答案）。通过调整这三个维度来优化准确率与token成本的权衡。

Result: 在五个多样化的推理基准和多个模型规模上，Fractured Sampling始终实现更优的准确率-成本权衡，在Pass@k与token预算之间呈现陡峭的log-linear扩展增益。

Conclusion: 揭示了如何跨不同维度分配计算资源以最大化推理性能，为更高效、可扩展的LLM推理铺平了道路。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling techniques have significantly bolstered the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by harnessing additional computational effort at inference without retraining. Similarly, Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting and its extension, Long CoT, improve accuracy by generating rich intermediate reasoning trajectories, but these approaches incur substantial token costs that impede their deployment in latency-sensitive settings. In this work, we first show that truncated CoT, which stops reasoning before completion and directly generates the final answer, often matches the full CoT sampling while using dramatically fewer tokens. Building on this insight, we introduce Fractured Sampling, a unified inference-time strategy that interpolates between full CoT and solution-only sampling along three orthogonal axes: (1) the number of reasoning trajectories, (2) the number of final solutions per trajectory, and (3) the depth at which reasoning traces are truncated. Through extensive experiments on five diverse reasoning benchmarks and several model scales, we demonstrate that Fractured Sampling consistently achieves superior accuracy-cost trade-offs, yielding steep log-linear scaling gains in Pass@k versus token budget. Our analysis reveals how to allocate computation across these dimensions to maximize performance, paving the way for more efficient and scalable LLM reasoning. Code is available at https://github.com/BaohaoLiao/frac-cot.

</details>


### [233] [Ensembling Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16077)
*Soham Gadgil,Chris Lin,Su-In Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了稀疏自编码器（SAE）集成方法，包括朴素装袋（bagging）和提升（boosting），实验证明相比于单一扩大规模的SAE，SAE集成能更好地重建语言模型激活、提升稳定性，并在概念检测和虚假相关去除等下游任务中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单个SAE只能捕获激活空间中有限的子集特征，存在表达能力不足的局限。

Method: 提出SAE集成形式化框架，具体采用朴素装袋（不同初始化训练多个SAE）和提升（顺序训练以最小化残差）两种集成策略。

Result: 在三种语言模型和SAE架构设置下，相比于同等特征数量的扩大型SAE，集成的SAE在激活重建准确性和稳定性上更优；在概念检测和虚假相关去除等下游任务中表现更好。

Conclusion: SAE集成是一种有效提升特征提取能力和下游任务实用性的方法，其理论基础是降低重建误差。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are used to decompose neural network activations into human-interpretable features. Typically, features learned by a single SAE are used for downstream applications. However, it has recently been shown that a single SAE captures only a limited subset of features that can be extracted from the activation space. Motivated by this limitation, we introduce and formalize SAE ensembles. Furthermore, we propose to ensemble multiple SAEs through naive bagging and boosting. In naive bagging, SAEs trained with different weight initializations are ensembled, whereas in boosting SAEs sequentially trained to minimize the residual error are ensembled. Theoretically, naive bagging and boosting are justified as approaches to reduce reconstruction error. Empirically, we evaluate our ensemble approaches with three settings of language models and SAE architectures. Our empirical results demonstrate that, compared to an expanded SAE that matches the number of features in the ensemble, ensembling SAEs improves the reconstruction of language model activations along with SAE stability. Additionally, on downstream tasks such as concept detection and spurious correlation removal, SAE ensembles achieve better performance, showing improved practical utility.

</details>


### [234] [FreshRetailNet-LT: A Stockout-Annotated Censored Demand Dataset for Latent Demand Recovery and Forecasting in Fresh Retail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16319)
*Yangyang Wang,Jiawei Gu,Li Long,Xin Li,Li Shen,Zhouyu Fu,Xiangjun Zhou,Xu Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FreshRetailNet-50K，首个大规模生鲜零售需求估计基准数据集，包含50,000条店铺-产品时间序列、每小时粒度销售数据及库存缺货标注，通过两阶段需求建模将预测精度提升2.73%并消除系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售生鲜品的准确需求估计对库存和定价至关重要，但缺货期间的销售数据截断（censored sales data）导致未被观察到的需求产生系统性政策偏差。现有数据集缺乏解决该截断效应所需的时间分辨率和标注信息。

Method: 构建FreshRetailNet-50K数据集，包含898家门店、863个SKU、18个城市的50,000条时间序列，提供每小时粒度销售数据、库存状态记录及促销、降水、时间特征等协变量。提出两阶段需求建模方法：第一阶段利用精确的每小时标注重建缺货期间的潜在需求，第二阶段使用恢复的需求训练鲁棒的预测模型。

Result: 两阶段方法在预测精度上提升2.73%，同时将系统性需求低估从7.37%降至接近零偏差。

Conclusion: FreshRetailNet-50K以空前的时间粒度和真实世界信息，为需求插补、生鲜库存优化和因果零售分析开辟新方向，其标注质量和规模解决了零售AI长期存在的局限。

Abstract: Accurate demand estimation is critical for the retail business in guiding the inventory and pricing policies of perishable products. However, it faces fundamental challenges from censored sales data during stockouts, where unobserved demand creates systemic policy biases. Existing datasets lack the temporal resolution and annotations needed to address this censoring effect. To fill this gap, we present FreshRetailNet-50K, the first large-scale benchmark for censored demand estimation. It comprises 50,000 store-product time series of detailed hourly sales data from 898 stores in 18 major cities, encompassing 863 perishable SKUs meticulously annotated for stockout events. The hourly stock status records unique to this dataset, combined with rich contextual covariates, including promotional discounts, precipitation, and temporal features, enable innovative research beyond existing solutions. We demonstrate one such use case of two-stage demand modeling: first, we reconstruct the latent demand during stockouts using precise hourly annotations. We then leverage the recovered demand to train robust demand forecasting models in the second stage. Experimental results show that this approach achieves a 2.73% improvement in prediction accuracy while reducing the systematic demand underestimation from 7.37% to near-zero bias. With unprecedented temporal granularity and comprehensive real-world information, FreshRetailNet-50K opens new research directions in demand imputation, perishable inventory optimization, and causal retail analytics. The unique annotation quality and scale of the dataset address long-standing limitations in retail AI, providing immediate solutions and a platform for future methodological innovation. The data (https://huggingface.co/datasets/Dingdong-Inc/FreshRetailNet-50K) and code (https://github.com/Dingdong-Inc/frn-50k-baseline}) are openly released.

</details>


### [235] [DiffusionBlocks: Block-wise Neural Network Training via Diffusion Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14202)
*Makoto Shing,Masanori Koyama,Takuya Akiba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffusionBlocks是一种将基于transformer的网络转化为真正独立可训练块的框架，利用残差连接与去噪过程的对应关系，通过分数匹配目标实现逐块独立训练，在多种架构上匹配端到端训练性能，同时显著降低内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端反向传播需要存储所有层的激活值，造成内存瓶颈，限制了模型的可扩展性。现有分块训练方法依赖特设的局部目标函数，且主要局限于分类任务。

Method: 利用残差连接天然对应于动力系统更新的特性，通过最小化修改将其转化为去噪过程的更新，每个块可独立使用分数匹配目标进行学习，一次只训练一个块。

Result: 在多种transformer架构（视觉、扩散、自回归、循环深度、掩码扩散）上，DiffusionBlocks训练匹配端到端训练性能，同时实现内存需求与块数成比例减少。

Conclusion: DiffusionBlocks提供了一种理论上有依据的方法，成功将分块训练扩展到现代生成任务及多种架构。

Abstract: End-to-end backpropagation requires storing activations throughout all layers, creating memory bottlenecks that limit model scalability. Existing block-wise training methods offer means to alleviate this problem, but they rely on ad-hoc local objectives and remain largely unexplored beyond classification tasks. We propose $\textit{DiffusionBlocks}$, a principled framework for transforming transformer-based networks into genuinely independent trainable blocks that maintain competitive performance with end-to-end training. Our key insight leverages the fact that residual connections naturally correspond to updates in a dynamical system. With minimal modifications to this system, we can convert the updates to those of a denoising process, where each block can be learned independently by leveraging the score matching objective. This independence enables training with gradients for only one block at a time, thereby reducing memory requirements in proportion to the number of blocks. Our experiments on a range of transformer architectures (vision, diffusion, autoregressive, recurrent-depth, and masked diffusion) demonstrate that DiffusionBlocks training matches the performance of end-to-end training while enabling scalable block-wise training on practical tasks beyond small-scale classification. DiffusionBlocks provides a theoretically grounded approach that successfully scales to modern generative tasks across diverse architectures. Code is available at https://github.com/SakanaAI/DiffusionBlocks .

</details>


### [236] [UltraSketchLLM: Sub-1-Bit LLM Compression via Sketch and Hardware-Friendly Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17255)
*Sunan Zou,Xueting Sun,Ziyun Zhang,Guojie Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UltraSketchLLM通过数据草图技术将LLM权重压缩至0.5比特/权重，结合硬件友好实现，在保持可接受的性能损失下实现14.9倍加速，显著降低GPU内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要巨大的GPU内存，现有压缩方法要么受限于每权重1比特的理论极限，要么面临严重的性能下降和效率低下问题，亟需在资源受限场景下部署LLM的高效极低比特压缩方法。

Method: 提出UltraSketchLLM方法，利用数据草图（data sketch）技术对LLM进行压缩，将权重压缩至0.5比特/权重的超高压缩率，并采用硬件友好的实现方案降低延迟开销。

Result: 达到0.5比特/权重的压缩率，相比朴素草图方案实现14.9倍加速，峰值GPU内存占用显著降低，延迟开销极低，性能退化在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: UltraSketchLLM成功突破了每权重1比特的理论压缩极限，以0.5比特/权重的超低比特率实现了LLM的高效部署，通过硬件友好设计兼顾了压缩率、性能和速度，为资源受限场景下的LLM应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) require larger GPU memory size these days, necessitating efficient and extreme weight compression methods. Existing compression methods are either theoretically limited by 1 bit per weight or face severe performance degradation and inefficiency. To deploy LLMs in resource-constrained scenarios, we introduce UltraSketchLLM, compressing LLMs with data sketch. It reduces peak GPU memory footprint with a high compression rate down to 0.5 bit per weight. Combined with hardware-friendly implementation, UltraSketchLLM keeps tolerable performance degradation and extremely low latency overhead with 14.9x speedup compared to naive sketch solution.

</details>


### [237] [From Small to Large: A Graph Convolutional Network Approach for Solving Assortment Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.10834)
*Guokai Li,Pin Gao,Stefanus Jasin,Zizhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图卷积网络（GCN）的框架，用于高效求解带约束的品类优化问题。该框架将问题构建为图结构，训练GCN学习从问题参数到最优品类的映射，并开发了三种基于GCN输出的推理策略。由于GCN具备跨实例规模的泛化能力，从小样本中学到的模式可迁移至大规模问题。实验表明，在20个产品上训练的GCN能在数秒内对多达2000个产品的问题实现超过85%的最优收益，在精度和效率上优于现有启发式方法。该框架还扩展到了基于交易数据的未知选择模型场景，展现了相似性能与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 品类优化问题具有组合优化和非线性特性，属于NP-hard问题，在电商等行业中需要每分钟求解数千个此类问题，亟需高效且可扩展的求解方法。

Method: 构建问题对应的图表示，训练图卷积网络（GCN）学习从问题参数到最优品类集合的映射，并设计三种基于GCN输出的推理策略实现决策。

Result: 在20个产品上训练的GCN，对多达2000个产品的问题能在数秒内达到85%以上的最优收益，在准确性和效率上均优于现有启发式方法。

Conclusion: GCN框架能高效求解大规模品类优化问题，具备良好的尺寸泛化能力，并可扩展至未知选择模型的实际场景，具有显著的应用价值。

Abstract: Assortment optimization seeks to select a subset of substitutable products, subject to constraints, to maximize expected revenue. The problem is NP-hard due to its combinatorial and nonlinear nature and arises frequently in industries such as e-commerce, where platforms must solve thousands of such problems each minute. We propose a graph convolutional network (GCN) framework to efficiently solve constrained assortment optimization problems. Our approach constructs a graph representation of the problem, trains a GCN to learn the mapping from problem parameters to optimal assortments, and develops three inference policies based on the GCN's output. Owing to the GCN's ability to generalize across instance sizes, patterns learned from small-scale samples can be transferred to large-scale problems. Theoretical results are established to show the expressive power of the proposed GCN, and explain the underlying mechanism of the size generalization ability. Numerical experiments show that a GCN trained on instances with 20 products achieves over 85% of the optimal revenue on problems with up to 2,000 products within seconds, outperforming existing heuristics in both accuracy and efficiency. We further extend the framework to settings with an unknown choice model using transaction data and demonstrate similar performance and scalability.

</details>


### [238] [From Sorting Algorithms to Scalable Kernels: Bayesian Optimization in High-Dimensional Permutation Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13263)
*Zikai Xie,Linjiang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于归并排序的Merge Kernel框架，用于高维排列空间的贝叶斯优化，将计算复杂度从O(n²)降低至O(n log n)，且在大规模排列优化中显著优于现有Mallows Kernel方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯优化方法在高维排列空间中受限于密集表示的计算瓶颈（Mallows Kernel需要O(n²)的成对比较），难以扩展到大规模排列问题。

Method: 提出基于排序算法的核函数框架，将Mallows Kernel视为枚举排序的特例；核心贡献是引入Merge Kernel，利用归并排序的分治结构生成紧凑的排列表示，实现Θ(n log n)复杂度且无信息损失。

Result: 在低维设置中Merge Kernel与Mallows Kernel性能相当；在高维场景下，Merge Kernel在优化性能和计算效率上均显著优于Mallows Kernel。

Conclusion: Merge Kernel为高维排列空间提供了可扩展且更有效的贝叶斯优化方案，能够解决大规模特征排序和组合神经架构搜索等此前难以处理的问题。

Abstract: Bayesian Optimization (BO) is a powerful tool for black-box optimization, but its application to high-dimensional permutation spaces is severely limited by the challenge of defining scalable representations. The current state-of-the-art BO approach for permutation spaces relies on an exhaustive $Ω(n^2)$ pairwise comparison, inducing a dense representation that is impractical for large-scale permutations. To break this barrier, we introduce a novel framework for generating efficient permutation representations via kernel functions derived from sorting algorithms. Within this framework, the Mallows kernel can be viewed as a special instance derived from enumeration sort. Further, we introduce the \textbf{Merge Kernel} , which leverages the divide-and-conquer structure of merge sort to produce a compact, $Θ(n\log n)$ to achieve the lowest possible complexity with no information loss and effectively capture permutation structure. Our central thesis is that the Merge Kernel performs competitively with the Mallows kernel in low-dimensional settings, but significantly outperforms it in both optimization performance and computational efficiency as the dimension $n$ grows. Extensive evaluations on various permutation optimization benchmarks confirm our hypothesis, demonstrating that the Merge Kernel provides a scalable and more effective solution for Bayesian optimization in high-dimensional permutation spaces, thereby unlocking the potential for tackling previously intractable problems such as large-scale feature ordering and combinatorial neural architecture search.

</details>


### [239] [PERRY: Policy Evaluation with Confidence Intervals using Auxiliary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20068)
*Aishwarya Mandyam,Jason Meng,Ge Gao,Jiankai Sun,Mac Schwager,Barbara E. Engelhardt,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于数据增强的离线策略评估（OPE）置信区间构建方法：第一种利用共形预测为连续状态MDP的条件策略价值提供置信区间；第二种结合双重稳健估计与预测驱动推断为平均策略性能提供置信区间。在库存管理、机器人、医疗模拟及MIMIC-IV真实医疗数据上的实验表明，所提方法能有效利用辅助数据并生成覆盖真实值的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPE方法虽可利用生成模型合成的辅助数据集提升精度，但这类数据可能存在偏差，且现有数据增强OPE方法缺乏原则性的不确定性量化。在高风险领域（如医疗），可靠的不确定性估计对确保策略的安全部署至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出一种适用于连续状态空间MDP的共形预测方法，用于构建条件策略价值V^π(s)的置信区间；2) 结合双重稳健估计与预测驱动推断，构建平均策略性能V^π的置信区间。

Result: 在库存管理、机器人、医疗模拟器及MIMIC-IV真实医疗数据集上的实验表明，所提方法能有效利用辅助数据，并持续生成覆盖真实策略价值的置信区间，而现有方法无法做到。

Conclusion: 本文工作使得OPE在高风险领域能够提供严格的不确定性估计，为安全部署强化学习策略奠定基础。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) methods estimate the value of a new reinforcement learning (RL) policy prior to deployment. Recent advances have shown that leveraging auxiliary datasets, such as those synthesized by generative models, can improve the accuracy of OPE methods. Unfortunately, such auxiliary datasets may also be biased, and existing methods for using data augmentation within OPE lack principled uncertainty quantification. In high stakes domains like healthcare, reliable uncertainty estimates are important for ensuring safe and informed deployment of RL policies. In this work, we propose two methods to construct valid confidence intervals for OPE with data augmentation. The first provides a confidence interval over $V^π(s)$, the policy value conditioned on an initial state $s$. To do so we introduce a new conformal prediction method suitable for Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) with continuous state spaces, extending prior work to higher-dimensional settings. Second, we consider the more common task of estimating the average policy performance over all initial states, $V^π$; we introduce a method that draws on ideas from doubly robust estimation and prediction powered inference. Across simulators spanning inventory management, robotics, healthcare, and a real healthcare dataset from MIMIC-IV, we find that our methods can effectively leverage auxiliary data and consistently produce confidence intervals that cover the ground truth policy values, unlike previously proposed methods. Our work enables a future in which OPE can provide rigorous uncertainty estimates for high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [240] [Tackling GNARLy Problems: Graph Neural Algorithmic Reasoning Reimagined through Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18930)
*Alex Schutz,Victor-Alexandru Darvariu,Efimia Panagiotaki,Bruno Lacerda,Nick Hawes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GNARL框架，将神经算法推理问题重新建模为马尔可夫决策过程，结合模仿学习与强化学习，解决了传统NAR在解的有效性、多解推理、NP难问题及缺少专家算法时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算法推理（NAR）存在四大局限：需要后处理才能构造有效解、无法推理多个正确解、在组合NP难问题上表现差、对尚未存在强算法的问題无法应用。

Method: 将算法轨迹学习重新定义为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），引入模仿学习和强化学习工具，提出GNARL框架，包含从NAR到RL的问题转化方法论和适用于图问题的学习架构。

Result: 在CLRS-30多个问题上取得高图准确率，NP难问题性能达到或超过更窄的NAR方法，且能在缺乏专家算法时仍具适用性。

Conclusion: GNARL框架有效突破了NAR的多项限制，将算法推理拓展到强化学习范式，在更广泛的图问题中展现出更强的泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: Neural algorithmic reasoning (NAR) is a paradigm that trains neural networks to execute classic algorithms by supervised learning. Despite its successes, important limitations remain: inability to construct valid solutions without post-processing and to reason about multiple correct ones, poor performance on combinatorial NP-hard problems, and inapplicability to problems for which strong algorithms are not yet known. To address these limitations, we reframe the problem of learning algorithm trajectories as a Markov decision process, which imposes structure on the solution construction procedure and unlocks the powerful tools of imitation and reinforcement learning (RL). We propose the GNARL framework, encompassing the methodology to translate problem formulations from NAR to RL and a learning architecture suitable for a wide range of graph-based problems. We achieve very high graph accuracy results on several CLRS-30 problems, performance matching or exceeding much narrower NAR approaches for NP-hard problems and, remarkably, applicability even when lacking an expert algorithm.

</details>


### [241] [Q-Net: Queue Length Estimation via Kalman-based Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24725)
*Ting Gao,Elvin Isufi,Winnie Daamen,Erik-Sander Smits,Serge Hoogendoorn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Net，一种基于AI增强卡尔曼滤波器的信号控制交叉口排队长度估计框架，通过融合环形线圈检测器聚合车辆计数和聚合浮动车数据（aFCD），在保持物理可解释性的同时实现了实时、高精度且具有空间迁移性的排队长度估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信号控制交叉口的排队长度估计是交通管理中的长期难题，车辆流的部分可观测性使得这一任务复杂化。现有两种隐私保护数据源（环形线圈检测器的聚合车辆计数和aFCD的区间平均速度）在空间和时间分辨率上存在差异，如何有效融合这些数据源进行排队长度估计尚不明确。

Method: Q-Net基于状态空间模型构建，采用卡尔曼滤波的预测-更新结构，在状态演化和观测模型中保持物理可解释性。通过AI增强的卡尔曼滤波器从数据中学习时变增益动力学；将aFCD测量值分组为固定大小的局部组，使可学习参数数量独立于路段长度，从而提升空间迁移性。

Result: 在荷兰鹿特丹城市主干道上的评估表明，Q-Net优于基线方法，能够准确跟踪排队的形成和消散过程，并有效缓解aFCD引入的延迟问题。

Conclusion: Q-Net结合了数据效率、可解释性、实时适用性和空间迁移性，实现了无需摄像头或雷达等昂贵传感基础设施的准确排队长度估计。

Abstract: Estimating queue lengths at signalized intersections is a long-standing challenge in traffic management. Partial observability of vehicle flows complicates this task despite the availability of two privacy-preserving data sources: (i) aggregated vehicle counts from loop detectors near stop lines, and (ii) aggregated floating car data (aFCD) that provide segment-wise average speed measurements. However, how to integrate these sources with differing spatial and temporal resolutions for queue length estimation is rather unclear. Addressing this question, we present Q-Net: a queue estimation framework built upon a state-space formulation. This design addresses key challenges in queue modeling, such as violations of traffic conservation assumptions. Q-Net follows the Kalman predict-update structure and maintains physical interpretability in both the state evolution and measurement models. Q-Net uses an AI-augmented Kalman filter to learn time-varying gain dynamics from data. The framework supports real-time implementation and improves spatial transferability by grouping aFCD measurements into fixed-size local groups, making the number of learnable parameters independent of section length. Evaluations on urban main roads in Rotterdam, the Netherlands, show that Q-Net outperforms baseline methods, tracks queue formation and dissipation accurately, and mitigates aFCD-induced delays. By combining data efficiency, interpretability, real-time applicability, and spatial transferability, Q-Net makes accurate queue length estimation possible without costly sensing infrastructure like cameras or radar.

</details>


### [242] [Multidimensional Bayesian Active Machine Learning of Working Memory Task Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.00375)
*Dom CP Marticorena,Chris Wissmann,Zeyu Lu,Dennis L Barbour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了一种基于贝叶斯主动分类的双轴自适应实验设计方法，在5×5工作记忆重建任务中同时控制空间负荷L和特征绑定负荷K两个变量，使用高斯过程概率分类器输出(L, K)曲面而非单一阈值，仅需约30个样本即可完成模型拟合，并与传统自适应阶梯法在K=3时达到0.755组内相关系数的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知实验大多控制单一因子并用标量总结表现，而自适应实验设计已超越一维阶梯式适应方法，需要更灵活的能够同时控制多个变量的实验设计框架来揭示个体差异和变量间的交互作用。

Method: 采用贝叶斯双轴主动分类方法，在沉浸式虚拟测试环境中进行5×5工作记忆重建任务，同时控制空间负荷L（占用瓷砖数量）和特征绑定负荷K（物品的不同颜色数量）两个变量，通过非参数高斯过程概率分类器的不确定性引导刺激采集，输出(L, K)曲面。

Result: 在年轻成人群体中，GP驱动的自适应模式与传统自适应阶梯经典模式在K=3时达到0.755的组内相关系数；自适应模式揭示了空间负荷与特征绑定之间的个体差异交互作用；约30个样本即可完成完整模型的准确拟合，收敛速度快于其他采样策略。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯双轴主动分类方法能够有效控制多个实验变量，快速收敛并揭示个体差异，为认知实验设计提供了超越传统一维阶梯法的新范式。

Abstract: While adaptive experimental design has outgrown one-dimensional, staircase-based adaptations, most cognitive experiments still control a single factor and summarize performance with a scalar. We show a validation of a Bayesian, two-axis, active-classification approach, carried out in an immersive virtual testing environment for a 5-by-5 working-memory reconstruction task. Two variables are controlled: spatial load L (number of occupied tiles) and feature-binding load K (number of distinct colors) of items. Stimulus acquisition is guided by posterior uncertainty of a nonparametric Gaussian Process (GP) probabilistic classifier, which outputs a surface over (L, K) rather than a single threshold or max span value. In a young adult population, we compare GP-driven Adaptive Mode (AM) with a traditional adaptive staircase Classic Mode (CM), which varies L only at K = 3. Parity between the methods is achieved for this cohort, with an intraclass coefficient of 0.755 at K = 3. Additionally, AM reveals individual differences in interactions between spatial load and feature binding. AM estimates converge more quickly than other sampling strategies, demonstrating that only about 30 samples are required for accurate fitting of the full model.

</details>


### [243] [Shift-Invariant Attribute Scoring for Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks via Shapley Value](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01663)
*Wangxuan Fan,Ching Wang,Siqi Li,Nan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ShapKAN剪枝框架，利用Shapley值对KAN网络中节点重要性进行平移不变性评估，实现有效网络压缩的同时保持节点真实重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KAN网络通过可学习样条激活函数增强了可解释性，但其架构给剪枝带来挑战——传统基于幅值的方法因输入坐标偏移而不可靠，需要一种平移不变的重要性评估方法。

Method: 提出ShapKAN框架，使用Shapley值归因来评估节点重要性，该方法具有平移不变性，不受输入参数化影响，能量化每个节点的实际贡献。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，ShapKAN能够保持节点真实重要性，同时实现有效的网络压缩。

Conclusion: ShapKAN改善了KAN的可解释性优势，有助于在资源受限环境中的部署。

Abstract: For many real-world applications, understanding feature-outcome relationships is as crucial as achieving high predictive accuracy. While traditional neural networks excel at prediction, their black-box nature obscures underlying functional relationships. Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) address this by employing learnable spline-based activation functions on edges, enabling recovery of symbolic representations while maintaining competitive performance. However, KAN's architecture presents unique challenges for network pruning. Conventional magnitude-based methods become unreliable due to sensitivity to input coordinate shifts. We propose \textbf{ShapKAN}, a pruning framework using Shapley value attribution to assess node importance in a shift-invariant manner. Unlike magnitude-based approaches, ShapKAN quantifies each node's actual contribution, ensuring consistent importance rankings regardless of input parameterization. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ShapKAN preserves true node importance while enabling effective network compression. Our approach improves KAN's interpretability advantages, facilitating deployment in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [244] [RAMAC: Multimodal Risk-Aware Offline Reinforcement Learning and the Role of Behavior Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02695)
*Kai Fukazawa,Kunal Mundada,Iman Soltani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAMAC是一个结合生成式表达性策略（如扩散模型）与分布性评论家，并通过CVaR和BC联合优化实现风险敏感离线强化学习的模块化框架，在随机D4RL基准上同时提升了低风险分位数回报和平均回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险厌恶离线RL方法要么依赖值/模型基础的悲观主义（牺牲效率），要么依赖受限策略类（限制表达能力），而扩散/流等表达性生成策略主要用于风险中性场景。亟需一个既能利用生成式策略的表达能力、又能实现风险敏感学习的框架。

Method: 提出RAMAC框架，核心包括：(1) 表达性生成式actor（如扩散/流模型）；(2) 分布性critic（估计完整回报分布而非期望）；(3) 联合优化CVaR（条件风险价值）与行为克隆（BC）的复合目标。理论分析表明BC通过控制行为发散抑制OOD动作，稳定CVaR学习。

Result: 在2-D风险bandit上验证了框架的有效性；在随机D4RL基准上，RAMAC在保持强劲平均回报的同时，一致性地提升了CVaR_{0.1}（低风险分位数回报）。

Conclusion: RAMAC成功弥合了表达性生成策略与风险敏感离线RL之间的鸿沟，通过BC正则化有效抑制OOD动作驱动的灾难性失败，为安全关键场景下的离线RL提供了实用方案。

Abstract: In safety-critical domains where online data collection is infeasible, offline reinforcement learning (RL) is attractive only if policies achieve high returns without catastrophic lower-tail risk. Prior work on risk-averse offline RL achieves safety at the cost of either (i) value/model-based pessimism or (ii) restricted policy classes that limit expressiveness, whereas diffusion/flow-based expressive generative policies have largely been used in risk-neutral settings. We introduce \textbf{Risk-Aware Multimodal Actor-Critic (RAMAC)}, a simple, modular, model-free framework that couples an expressive generative actor (e.g., diffusion/flow) with a distributional critic and optimizes a composite objective that combines Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) with behavioral cloning (BC), enabling risk-sensitive learning in complex multimodal scenarios. Since out-of-distribution (OOD) actions are a major driver of catastrophic failures in offline RL, we further provide an objective-level analysis showing that controlling behavior divergence via BC suppresses OOD actions and stabilizes CVaR. Instantiating RAMAC with a diffusion actor, we illustrate these insights on a 2-D risky bandit and evaluate on Stochastic-D4RL, observing consistent gains in $\mathrm{CVaR}_{0.1}$ while maintaining strong returns. The code and experimental results are available on the \href{https://kaifukazawa.github.io/ramac-project/} {project website}

</details>


### [245] [Distributional Biases in Post-Training: A Markovian Analysis of Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07368)
*Dake Bu,Wei Huang,Andi Han,Atsushi Nitanda,Bo Xue,Qingfu Zhang,Hau-San Wong,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了强化学习与测试时扩展策略在推理任务中的矛盾：虽然探索提升了pass@K，但RLVR和ORM/PRM实际倾向于强化已有路径而非扩展推理范围。作者通过将推理步骤建模为不同概率的马尔可夫转移，证明RLVR和ORM/PRM会过度聚焦高概率路径而遗忘罕见但关键的推理链，并理论证明拒绝简单实例和KL正则化等探索策略有助于保留这些稀有推理路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和实证中的悖论：RLVR和ORM/PRM通常强化已有路径而非扩展推理范围，但探索却能提升pass@K，亟需理论解释这一矛盾。

Method: 将推理步骤建模为低（困难步骤，如发现对称性）与高（简单步骤，如化简分数）概率马尔可夫转移；预训练对应树图发现，后训练对应思维链重加权；通过理论证明分析不同策略的影响。

Result: 证明RLVR和ORM/PRM会过度偏向若干高概率路径，导致遗忘稀有但关键的思维链；证明拒绝简单实例和KL正则化等探索策略有助于保留稀有推理路径；仿真实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 探索策略（如拒绝简单实例和KL正则化）对于保留模型中稀有但关键的推理路径至关重要，是提升推理能力的关键机制。

Abstract: Foundation models exhibit broad knowledge but limited task-specific reasoning, motivating post-training strategies such as RL with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and test-time scaling (TTS). While recent work highlights the role of exploration in improving pass@K, empirical evidence points to a paradox: RLVR and ORM/PRM typically reinforce existing paths rather than expanding the reasoning scope, raising the question of why exploration helps if no new patterns emerge.
  To reconcile this paradox, we adopt the perspective of Kim et al. (2025), viewing easy (e.g., simplifying a fraction) versus hard (e.g., discovering the some symmetry) reasoning steps as low versus high probability Markov transitions. In this tractable model, pretraining corresponds to tree-graph discovering, while post-training corresponds to CoT reweighting. We provably show that, both RLVR and ORM/PRM would favor heavily to several high-probability paths, and thereby forget rare-but-crucial CoTs. Building on this, we further prove that exploration strategies such as rejecting easy instances and KL regularization help preserve rare CoTs. Empirical simulations corroborate our theoretical results.

</details>


### [246] [Trend-Aware Multi-Task Learning for Short-Term Energy Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.09789)
*Fulong Yao,Wanqing Zhao,Chao Zheng,Xiaofei Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种趋势感知的多任务预测框架，通过将预测输出分解为方向性变动和相对偏差幅度，兼顾数值精度与趋势预测能力，在短期能源预测任务中取得了与SOTA相当的数值精度，并显著提升了趋势预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短期能源预测方法将问题纯粹视为回归任务，难以显式捕捉运营决策所需的步进方向变动和趋势一致性，导致预测结果在方向性信号上不够可靠。

Method: 提出趋势感知多任务预测框架，采用任务特定的双流架构，将预测输出分解为方向性变动（分类任务）和偏差幅度（回归任务）；探索了硬/概率趋势表示、对称/非对称偏差建模、并行/顺序条件策略等设计选择；引入基于不确定性的任务加权机制自动平衡多任务学习。

Result: 在真实能源数据集上，与SOTA算法相比取得了具有竞争力的数值精度，同时以适中的计算成本持续改善了趋势预测性能。

Conclusion: 该框架在需要方向性预测一致性的短期能源系统管理场景（如市场竞价、资源调度、风险感知能源管理）中能提供更可靠的决策支持。

Abstract: Short-term energy forecasting plays an important role in real-time operational decision-making, such as electricity market bidding and power system dispatch, where both numerical accuracy and correct directional signals are essential. However, most existing forecasting approaches formulate the problem purely as a regression task, limiting their ability to explicitly capture stepwise directional movements and trend consistency required for operational decisions. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a trend-aware multi-task forecasting framework that decomposes forecasting outputs into directional movements and deviation magnitudes relative to the latest observation, enabling both accurate numerical prediction and interpretable trend-aware outputs. The framework adopts a task-specific dual-stream architecture and explores key design choices for integrating trend and deviation information, including hard versus probabilistic trend representations, symmetric versus asymmetric deviation modelling, and parallel versus sequential conditioning strategies. To stabilize multi-task learning and reduce manual tuning, an uncertainty-aware task weighting scheme is incorporated to automatically balance directional classification, deviation regression, and final output prediction during training. Experimental results on real-world energy datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves competitive numerical accuracy compared with state-of-the-art algorithms, while consistently improving trend prediction performance with moderate computational cost. This capability is particularly beneficial in short-term energy system management, where consistent directional forecasting can provide more reliable decision support for practical operational scenarios such as market bidding, resource scheduling, and risk-aware energy management.

</details>


### [247] [Lower Complexity Bounds for Nonconvex-Strongly-Convex Bilevel Optimization with First-Order Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.19656)
*Kaiyi Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对光滑非凸-强凸双层优化，在确定性和随机一阶Oracle下分别证明了Ω(κ^{3/2}ε^{-2})和Ω(κ^{5/2}ε^{-4})的下界，揭示了现有上下界之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管双层优化的上界保证已被广泛研究，但由于双层结构的复杂性，其下界进展有限。

Method: 构造新的困难实例，在确定性和随机一阶Oracle模型下分析零阶保持算法下界。

Result: 确定性情形：Ω(κ^{3/2}ε^{-2})；随机情形：Ω(κ^{5/2}ε^{-4})。

Conclusion: 现有上下界存在显著差距，即使简化设置（如二次下层目标）也需进一步研究以理解最优复杂度。

Abstract: Although upper bound guarantees for bilevel optimization have been widely studied, progress on lower bounds has been limited due to the complexity of the bilevel structure. In this work, we focus on the smooth nonconvex-strongly-convex setting and develop new hard instances that yield nontrivial lower bounds under deterministic and stochastic first-order oracle models. In the deterministic case, we prove that any first-order zero-respecting algorithm requires at least $Ω(κ^{3/2}ε^{-2})$ oracle calls to find an $ε$-accurate stationary point, improving the optimal lower bounds known for single-level nonconvex optimization and for nonconvex-strongly-convex min-max problems. In the stochastic case, we show that at least $Ω(κ^{5/2}ε^{-4})$ stochastic oracle calls are necessary, again strengthening the best known bounds in related settings. Our results expose substantial gaps between current upper and lower bounds for bilevel optimization and suggest that even simplified regimes, such as those with quadratic lower-level objectives, warrant further investigation toward understanding the optimal complexity of bilevel optimization under standard first-order oracles.

</details>


### [248] [Adaptive Identification and Modeling of Clinical Pathways with Process Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.03787)
*Francesco Vitale,Nicola Mazzocca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于过程挖掘的两阶段临床路径建模方法，通过一致性检测扩展知识库。使用Synthea数据集验证，在SARS-CoV-2治疗路径建模上达到95.62% AUC和67.11%的弧简化度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动基于临床指南和领域知识建模临床路径困难且难以反映不同疾病变异或组合下的实际最佳实践，需要自动化方法。

Method: 两阶段过程挖掘方法：第一阶段收集历史数据构建参考过程模型；第二阶段将新数据与参考模型进行一致性检测，根据结果扩展知识库，增加针对新变体或疾病组合的特化模型。

Result: 在Synthea模拟的COVID-19并发症数据集上，方法实现了95.62% AUC的精确度，同时保持67.11%的弧简化度。

Conclusion: 该方法有效扩展了临床路径知识库，在保持模型简洁性的同时实现了高精度，可用于自动化临床路径建模与优化。

Abstract: Clinical pathways are specialized healthcare plans that model patient treatment procedures. They are developed to provide criteria-based progression and standardize patient treatment, thereby improving care, reducing resource use, and accelerating patient recovery. However, manual modeling of these pathways based on clinical guidelines and domain expertise is difficult and may not reflect the actual best practices for different variations or combinations of diseases. We propose a two-phase modeling method using process mining, which extends the knowledge base of clinical pathways by leveraging conformance checking diagnostics. In the first phase, historical data of a given disease is collected to capture treatment in the form of a process model. In the second phase, new data is compared against the reference model to verify conformance. Based on the conformance checking results, the knowledge base can be expanded with more specific models tailored to new variants or disease combinations. We demonstrate our approach using Synthea, a benchmark dataset simulating patient treatments for SARS-CoV-2 infections with varying COVID-19 complications. The results show that our method enables expanding the knowledge base of clinical pathways with sufficient precision, peaking to 95.62% AUC while maintaining an arc-degree simplicity of 67.11%.

</details>


### [249] [Multi-fidelity aerodynamic data fusion by autoencoder transfer learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13069)
*Javier Nieto-Centenero,Esther Andrés,Rodrigo Castellanos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合自编码器迁移学习与多分裂共形预测（MSCP）的多保真深度学习框架，在极低数据稀缺条件下实现气动数据的高精度融合与不确定性量化，仅用少量高保真样本即可有效修正低保真数据偏差，并达到95%以上的逐点覆盖置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真气动模拟计算成本极高，严重限制了其在数据驱动建模中的应用，因此需要开发能够利用廉价低保真信息同时保持精度的多保真策略。

Method: 采用自编码器迁移学习框架，先用丰富的低保真数据学习紧凑的隐空间物理表征作为冻结知识库，再用少量高保真样本微调解码器；同时提出多分裂共形预测（MSCP）策略实现不确定性量化。

Result: 在NACA翼型（二维）和跨声速机翼（三维）数据库上的测试表明，模型能有效修正低保真偏差，用极少的高保真训练数据实现高精度压力预测；MSCP框架提供稳健的不确定性区间，逐点覆盖率超过95%。

Conclusion: 该工作将极致的样本效率与不确定性量化相结合，为数据稀缺环境下的气动回归问题提供了可扩展且可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate aerodynamic prediction often relies on high-fidelity simulations; however, their prohibitive computational costs severely limit their applicability in data-driven modeling. This limitation motivates the development of multi-fidelity strategies that leverage inexpensive low-fidelity information without compromising accuracy. Addressing this challenge, this work presents a multi-fidelity deep learning framework that combines autoencoder-based transfer learning with a newly developed Multi-Split Conformal Prediction (MSCP) strategy to achieve uncertainty-aware aerodynamic data fusion under extreme data scarcity. The methodology leverages abundant Low-Fidelity (LF) data to learn a compact latent physics representation, which acts as a frozen knowledge base for a decoder that is subsequently fine-tuned using scarce HF samples. Tested on surface-pressure distributions for NACA airfoils (2D) and a transonic wing (3D) databases, the model successfully corrects LF deviations and achieves high-accuracy pressure predictions using minimal HF training data. Furthermore, the MSCP framework produces robust, actionable uncertainty bands with pointwise coverage exceeding 95%. By combining extreme data efficiency with uncertainty quantification, this work offers a scalable and reliable solution for aerodynamic regression in data-scarce environments.

</details>


### [250] [Deep Learning and Elicitability for McKean-Vlasov FBSDEs With Common Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.14967)
*Felipe J. P. Antunes,Yuri F. Saporito,Sebastian Jaimungal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Picard迭代、可 elicitability 和深度学习的数值方法，用于求解含共同噪声的McKean-Vlasov正倒向随机微分方程（MV-FBSDEs），避免了嵌套蒙特卡洛模拟，并在多个经济模型中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 求解含共同噪声的MV-FBSDEs在数学上具有挑战性，传统方法需要计算昂贵的嵌套蒙特卡洛模拟，难以扩展到高维或复杂经济模型。

Method: 利用可 elicitability 性质导出路劲损失函数，用前馈和循环神经网络混合架构近似解耦场，用RNN参数化均场交互项，通过最小化可 elicited 得分进行训练。

Result: 在系统风险银行间借贷模型（有解析解）上准确恢复真实解；扩展到分位数介导交互模型；应用于非平稳Aiyagari-Bewley-Huggett经济增长模型，验证了对无闭式解的复杂均场博弈的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提方法高效、灵活，适用于含共同噪声的MV-FBSDEs及其在均场博弈中的应用，且可推广到条件期望之外的分位数等统计泛函。

Abstract: We present a novel numerical method for solving McKean--Vlasov forward--backward stochastic differential equations (MV--FBSDEs) with common noise, combining Picard iterations, elicitability and deep learning. The key innovation involves elicitability to derive a pathwise loss function, enabling efficient training of neural networks to approximate both the backward process and the conditional expectations arising from common noise, without requiring computationally expensive nested Monte Carlo simulations. The mean-field interaction term is parameterized via a recurrent neural network trained to minimize an elicitable score, while the backward process is approximated through a hybrid feedforward and recurrent network representing the decoupling field. We validate the algorithm on a systemic-risk inter-bank borrowing and lending model, where analytical solutions exist, demonstrating accurate recovery of the true solution. We further extend the model to quantile-mediated interactions, showcasing the flexibility of the elicitability framework beyond conditional means or moments. Finally, we apply the method to a non-stationary Aiyagari--Bewley--Huggett economic growth model with endogenous interest rates, illustrating its applicability to complex mean-field games without closed-form solutions.

</details>


### [251] [Private Prediction via PAC Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14033)
*Xiaochen Zhu,Mayuri Sridhar,Srinivas Devadas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用PAC隐私（而非差分隐私）来实现私有预测，通过自适应噪声校准实现线性累积的互信息泄露，在极小的每查询预算下（CIFAR-10上MI预算2^{-32}）实现高精度（87.79%），并支持大规模查询（百万次查询时成员推断成功率仅51.08%），同时可利用大量PAC私有预测蒸馏出可无限查询的公开模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型通过API提供服务时，参数隐私保护面临挑战：模型输出维度低且对训练数据变化更稳定，但差分隐私（DP）需要针对最坏情况敏感度校准噪声，这对非凸模型难以处理。因此需要一种更适合私有预测场景的隐私框架。

Method: 提出PAC隐私作为私有预测的自然框架，基于实例的稳定性校准噪声以控制互信息泄露；核心贡献是新的自适应组合结果：通过自适应噪声校准，证明互信息在自适应和对抗性查询下仅线性累积。

Result: CIFAR-10上每查询MI预算2^{-32}时达到87.79%准确率；百万次查询后成员推断成功率仅51.08%，相当于(0.04, 10^{-5})-DP。利用21万张ImageNet子集的私有标签蒸馏出学生模型，在CIFAR-10上达91.86%准确率，成员推断成功率50.49%，相当于(0.02, 10^{-5})-DP。

Conclusion: PAC隐私通过实例级的稳定性校准和线性自适应组合，在私有预测场景中显著优于DP，既能以极低预算服务大量查询，又能通过知识蒸馏生成可无限查询的公开模型，实现了隐私与效用的更好权衡。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly served behind APIs. This renders private prediction, i.e., privatizing a model's outputs rather than its parameters, a natural privacy target: model outputs are lower-dimensional and far more stable to training-data changes than weights.
  While differential privacy (DP) cannot effectively exploit this as it calibrates noise to worst-case sensitivity that is intractable to bound for non-convex models, we argue that PAC privacy is a natural fit for private prediction. It is instance-based, and calibrates noise to a black-box function's empirical stability to control mutual-information (MI) leakage. The missing ingredient is efficient, adaptive composition. Serving predictions means answering a long stream of adaptively chosen queries from untrusted users; existing composition either fails under adaptivity, grows quadratically, or reverts to input-independent, DP-like noise. We close this gap with a new adversarial composition result via adaptive noise calibration and prove that MI accumulates only linearly under adaptive and adversarial querying.
  Experiments across modalities show that prediction stability enables high utility even at a tiny per-query budget: on CIFAR-10, we achieve 87.79% accuracy with a per-query MI budget of $2^{-32}$. This enables serving one million queries while provably bounding membership-inference success to 51.08% -- the same guarantee as $(0.04, 10^{-5})$-DP. Further, in the presence of auxiliary public data, the large volume of PAC-private predictions enables us to distill a publishable model that can be queried without limit. Concretely, 210,000 private labels on an ImageNet subset distill into a student reaching 91.86% accuracy on CIFAR-10 with membership inference success bounded by 50.49%, comparable to $(0.02, 10^{-5})$-DP.

</details>


### [252] [Learning What to Predict: Downstream-Guided Task Design for Continued Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22108)
*Shuqi Ke,Giulia Fanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出V-pretraining方法，通过轻量级任务设计器为自监督学习构建目标/视图，基于下游损失一阶减少进行优化，在语言建模和视觉任务上提升目标能力而不损害泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有继续预训练存在粗粒度反馈循环：固定自监督目标与下游选择脱节，单个更新缺乏目标能力导向，导致效率低下。

Method: V-pretraining将学习器（仅自监督损失训练）与轻量级任务设计器解耦。设计器为未标注批次构建候选目标/视图，通过预测该自监督更新后下游损失的1阶减少来评分，最大化该值后学习器执行更新（目标/视图与下游标签分离）。

Result: 语言建模：Qwen模型使用仅1024个GSM8K样本作为反馈，在GSM8K Pass@1上提升，Qwen2.5-0.5B单次运行+7.4点。视觉：DINOv3迁移至ADE20K语义分割和NYUv2深度估计提升，同时保持ImageNet线性准确率。

Conclusion: 反馈引导的任务构建能在不破坏通用表示的前提下提升目标能力，验证了通过少量可验证下游样本提供步骤级反馈的有效性。

Abstract: Continued pretraining is optimized with fixed self-supervised tasks but selected by downstream performance, creating a coarse feedback loop in which practitioners evaluate checkpoints, change data mixtures or objectives, and restart runs, while individual updates remain blind to target capabilities. We ask whether a small set of verifiable downstream examples can provide step-level feedback without directly supervising the learner. We introduce V-pretraining, which decouples a learner trained only with a self-supervised loss from a lightweight task designer that constructs targets or views for unlabeled batches. Given the current learner and batch, V-pretraining scores a candidate construction by predicting the first-order reduction in downstream loss after the induced self-supervised update. The designer maximizes this value; the learner then applies the update with targets or views detached, so downstream labels never update learner parameters. We instantiate V-pretraining as adaptive top-K soft targets for language modeling and learned views or masks for self-supervised vision. Across both modalities, V-pretraining improves target capabilities without degrading generalization. Under wall-clock-matched continued pretraining, it improves GSM8K Pass@1 for Qwen models using 1,024 GSM8K examples only as feedback, including a +7.4 point single-run gain for Qwen2.5-0.5B. In vision, it improves DINOv3 transfer to ADE20K semantic segmentation and NYUv2 depth estimation while preserving ImageNet linear accuracy, suggesting that feedback-guided task construction can improve target capabilities without collapsing general-purpose representations.

</details>


### [253] [Quantized Evolution Strategies: High-precision Fine-tuning of Quantized LLMs at Low-precision Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03120)
*Yinggan Xu,Kajetan Schweighofer,Risto Miikkulainen,Xin Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量化进化策略（QES），一种直接在量化空间中执行全参数微调的方法，通过累积误差反馈和无状态种子重放两大创新，在不依赖反向传播的条件下实现对量化大语言模型的高效微调，在多项任务上显著优于现有的零阶微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化（PTQ）虽能部署大语言模型到内存受限设备，但使模型静态化、难以微调。标准微调（包括强化学习）依赖反向传播和连续权重，无法在离散非可微的量化参数空间上应用。进化策略虽无需反向传播，但在量化参数优化中仍会因梯度估计消失或不准确而失败。

Method: QES基于两项创新：(1) 集成累积误差反馈，保留高精度权重更新信号；(2) 利用无状态种子重放，将内存使用降低到低精度推理水平。

Result: 在多种任务上显著优于现有的零阶微调方法，使得量化模型的直接微调成为可能。

Conclusion: QES打开了完全在量化空间中扩展大语言模型的可能性，为量化模型的直接微调提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) is essential for deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) on memory-constrained devices, yet it renders models static and difficult to fine-tune. Standard fine-tuning paradigms, including Reinforcement Learning (RL), fundamentally rely on backpropagation and continuous weights to compute gradients. Thus they cannot be used on quantized models, where the parameter space is discrete and non-differentiable. While Evolution Strategies (ES) offer a backpropagation-free alternative, optimization of the quantized parameters can still fail due to vanishing or inaccurate gradient estimation. This paper introduces Quantized Evolution Strategies (QES), an optimization paradigm that performs full-parameter fine-tuning directly in the quantized space. QES is based on two innovations: (1) it integrates accumulated error feedback to preserve high-precision weight updating signals, and (2) it utilizes a stateless seed replay to reduce memory usage to low-precision inference levels. QES significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art zeroth-order fine-tuning methods on a variety of tasks, making direct fine-tuning for quantized models possible. It therefore opens up the possibility for scaling up LLMs entirely in the quantized space. The source code is available at https://github.com/dibbla/Quantized-Evolution-Strategies .

</details>


### [254] [Rethinking the Trust Region in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04879)
*Penghui Qi,Xiangxin Zhou,Zichen Liu,Tianyu Pang,Chao Du,Min Lin,Wee Sun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出PPO中的比率裁剪机制因使用单样本蒙特卡洛估计，对LLM大词表场景存在低概率token过度惩罚、高概率token约束不足的问题，提出DPPO算法，用直接策略散度估计（TV/KL）替代启发式裁剪，并通过Binary和Top-K近似降低内存开销，实验证明其训练稳定性和效率优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPO的核心比率裁剪机制在LLM大词表场景下存在结构性问题：低概率token的更新被过度惩罚，而高概率token的潜在灾难性偏移却约束不足，导致训练效率低下和不稳定。

Method: 提出DPPO算法，用基于直接策略散度估计（如TV散度或KL散度）的原则性约束替代PPO的启发式比率裁剪；为避免巨大内存开销，引入高效的Binary和Top-K近似方法来捕获关键散度。

Result: 大量实验证明DPPO相比现有方法在训练稳定性和效率上均更优，为基于RL的LLM微调提供了更稳健的基础。

Conclusion: DPPO通过更合理的策略散度约束方式，解决了PPO在LLM微调中的固有缺陷，是更优的替代方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a cornerstone for fine-tuning Large Language Models (LLMs), with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) serving as the de facto standard algorithm. Despite its ubiquity, we argue that the core ratio clipping mechanism in PPO is structurally ill-suited for the large vocabularies inherent to LLMs. PPO constrains policy updates based on the probability ratio of sampled tokens, which serves as a noisy single-sample Monte Carlo estimate of the true policy divergence.
