</details>


### [290] [Let's Ask Gauss: Improved One-Run Privacy Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12733)
*Adya Agrawal,Yu Wei,Jaspal Singh,Malik Magdon-Ismail,Vassilis Zikas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布视角的高效隐私审计框架，利用白盒DP-SGD中金丝雀对齐信号的渐近正态性，通过单次训练运行获得更紧的隐私下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单次运行的隐私审计方法将训练样本或"金丝雀"阈值化为二元成员猜测，丢弃了大量有用信息，导致隐私下界不够紧。

Method: 在白盒DP-SGD设置下，证明了金丝雀对齐信号自然形成随机变量序列，其归一化和渐近服从高斯分布。基于此分布视角，开发了一个差分隐私审计框架。

Result: 从单次训练运行中获得了比先前方法更紧的隐私下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布化隐私审计框架能更有效地利用信号信息，提升了单次运行下隐私估计的紧致性。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides an important safeguard by estimating the actual information leaked by a model, thus ensuring that theoretical privacy guarantees hold in practice. We study empirical privacy auditing for differentially private (DP) machine learning, focusing on efficient one-run methods for mechanisms such as DP-SGD. Prior one-run approaches threshold training examples or "canaries" into binary membership guesses, which discards useful information. We show that, in the white-box DP-SGD setting, canary-aligned signals naturally form a sequence of random variables whose normalized sum is asymptotically Gaussian. Leveraging this distributional perspective, we develop a DP-auditing framework that leads to tighter privacy lower bounds from a single training run.

</details>


### [291] [Order Is Not Control: Driven-Dissipative Response Laws Across Artificial and Biological Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"秩序不等于控制"，定义了基于接收器门控响应律的控制框架，并在生物系统、LLM、适配器和随机算子面板中验证了局部可测量但非全局可部署的控制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐、可解释性、干预研究识别了诱导秩序的物体，但混淆了秩序与控制。需要明确控制的定义：控制需要接收器门控的响应律，而非仅仅是可观测的秩序模式。

Method: 定义分母索引算子（denominator-indexed operator），建立接收器门控响应律框架。在四个面板（生物系统：小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼；LLM；适配器；随机算子）中跨介质验证局部响应律的存在性、类型（接纳、饱和、变号、泄漏、过驱）及可分配性条件。

Result: LLM面板：四种材料条件下响应向量分量符号预测准确率72.8-73.7%，非零分量达84.3-84.8%；留出观测者对系统效应预测93.6%，目标/预言家族预测91.7%。生物面板提供物理响应算子证据，排除坐标同一性和控制器结论。随机算子面板区分了测量机会与可部署行动策略。

Conclusion: 支持局部接纳控制和可测量随机响应算子，但将可部署预生成控制、隐藏/logit因果充分性、生物到LLM坐标同一性、字面热力学量排除在范围之外。控制是局部的、介质依赖的，而非全局可部署的秩序。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [292] [DeepJEB++: Foundation Model-Driven Large-Scale 3D Engineering Dataset via 2D Latent Space Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12994)
*Soyoung Yoo,Leekyo Jeong,Jinsu Ra,Dongeon Lee,Sunwoong Yang,Hyogu Jeong,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeepJEB++ 是一个基于基础模型的数据增强框架，通过2D潜空间扩散、VLM质量过滤、生成式3D重建和自动仿真标注流水线，将不到400个种子设计扩展为15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架数据集（40倍扩展），解决了工程设计中3D数据稀缺和自动标注难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的工程设计受限于缺乏大规模同时包含几何形状和基于物理的性能标签的3D数据集；现有3D数据增强技术在保持细微多样的几何变化方面存在局限，且后续仿真标注过程难以自动化，因为边界条件随生成几何形状而变化。

Method: 三阶段框架：(1) 在2D潜空间微调预训练扩散模型，通过潜变量插值合成新视图，并用VLM过滤可制造性；(2) 通过域自适应生成式基础模型将验证图像提升为3D网格；(3) 自动化流水线识别载荷和螺栓接口并分配有限元标签（质量、应力、位移）。

Result: 从不到400个种子设计出发，在每个阶段仅使用单GPU的条件下，生成15,360个带仿真标签的3D支架，实现40倍扩展。沿可制造性、标签保真度（与SimJEB真值对比）和分布一致性三个内在轴评估了增强质量。

Conclusion: DeepJEB++ 有效解决了工程3D数据稀缺和自动标注难题，数据集将公开发布以支持可复现的工程AI研究。

Abstract: Data-driven engineering design is constrained by the lack of large-scale 3D datasets that pair geometry with physics-based performance labels. In particular, existing 3D data augmentation techniques have limitations in preserving subtle and diverse geometric variations, and it remains difficult to automate the subsequent simulation-labeling process, where boundary conditions vary depending on the generated geometry. We present DeepJEB++, a foundation-model-driven data-augmentation framework that expands a small seed set of jet engine brackets into a large, simulation-labeled 3D dataset under constrained resources. Our key idea is to augment in the data-rich 2D latent space, then transfer to 3D. In Stage 1, we fine-tune a pretrained 2D latent diffusion model on multi-view renders and synthesize novel views by latent interpolation, retaining manufacturable designs through a vision-language-model (VLM) quality filter. In Stage 2, the validated images are lifted to 3D meshes by a domain-adapted generative foundation model. In Stage 3, an automated pipeline recognizes the load and bolt interfaces on each mesh and assigns finite-element labels -- mass, stress, and displacement -- without manual intervention. We assess augmentation quality along three intrinsic axes: manufacturability, label fidelity against the SimJEB ground truth, and distributional consistency. Starting from fewer than 400 seed designs, DeepJEB++ yields 15,360 simulation-labeled 3D brackets -- a 40x expansion -- using a single GPU per stage. The dataset will be made publicly available to support reproducible engineering-AI research.

</details>


### [293] [TWLA: Achieving Ternary Weights and Low-Bit Activations for LLMs via Post-Training Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13054)
*Zhixiong Zhao,Zukang Xu,Zhixuan Chen,Xing Hu,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TWLA是一个后训练量化框架，通过对权重进行1.58比特三元量化、激活值进行4比特量化，在保持高精度的同时实现显著的推理加速。其核心贡献包括非对称三元量化器、Kronecker正交旋转重塑分布以及考虑层间交互的混合精度分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有三元量化方法无法有效处理重尾激活分布，导致激活值仍需保持高精度，从而限制了端到端推理加速。

Method: (1) E2M-ATQ：从欧几里得初始化到流形重定位的两阶段优化，最小化权重三元化下的层输出误差；(2) KOTMS：Kronecker结构正交旋转，将权重重塑为三元友好的三模态分布，同时统计上抑制激活异常值；(3) ILA-AMP：引入相邻层二阶交互代价进行比特分配，联合优化由共享正交变换引起的层间激活量化增益差异，防止弱层引发的级联效应。

Result: 在W1.58A4设置下保持高精度，并实现显著的推理加速。

Conclusion: TWLA通过权重三元化与低比特激活量化的结合，有效克服了重尾激活分布带来的限制，实现了高效的端到端推理加速。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit exceptional general language processing capabilities, but their memory and compute costs hinder deployment. Ternarization has emerged as a promising compression technique, offering significant reductions in model size and inference complexity. However, existing methods struggle with heavy-tailed activation distributions and therefore keep activations in high precision, fundamentally limiting end-to-end inference acceleration. To overcome this limitation, we propose TWLA, a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that achieves 1.58-bit weight compression and 4-bit activation quantization while maintaining high accuracy. TWLA comprises three components: (1) Euclidean-to-Manifold Asymmetric Ternary Quantizer (E2M-ATQ) minimizes layer-output error under weight ternarization via a two-stage optimization from Euclidean initialization to manifold relocation; (2) Kronecker Orthogonal Tri-Modal Shaping (KOTMS) applies a Kronecker-structured orthogonal rotation to reshape weights into ternary-friendly tri-modal distributions, while the shared rotation statistically suppresses activation outliers; and (3) Inter-Layer Aware Activation Mixed Precision (ILA-AMP) explicitly introduces adjacent-layer second-order interaction costs in bit allocation and jointly optimizes for the layer-wise disparity of activation quantization gains induced by the shared orthogonal transform, preventing cascades triggered by a few weak layers. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TWLA maintains high accuracy under W1.58A4, while delivering significant inference acceleration. The code is available at https://github.com/Kishon-zzx/TWLA.

</details>


### [294] [MP3: Multi-Period Pattern Pre-training for Spatio-Temporal Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13119)
*Lilan Peng,Yandi Liu,Qingren Yao,Chongshou Li,Tianrui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MP3的多周期模式预训练插件，用于解决时空预测中的"时间幻象"问题（即相似短窗口输入对应不同未来趋势）。MP3通过多周期模式学习、异质全局空间建模和跨周期因果Transformer，可无缝集成至现有STGNN中，在五个数据集上平均降低MAE 4.7%、RMSE 5.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有STGNN无法有效识别时空数据中的"时间幻象"现象（短期窗口输入相近但未来趋势迥异，或反之），其根本原因在于短窗口输入存在周期观测不完整、全局空间关联异质化以及跨周期叠加因果性。

Method: 提出MP3预训练插件，包含三个核心模块：(1) 多周期时序建模：利用边卷积识别不同多周期模式；(2) 多周期空间建模：采用瓶颈投影+全局记忆库捕获异质全局空间关系；(3) 跨周期模式交互：使用因果增强型Transformer捕捉不同周期模式间的依赖关系。该插件可直接插入现有STGNN骨干网络。

Result: 在五个STGNN基线模型和五个真实数据集（含大规模CA数据集）上验证，MP3带来一致且鲁棒的性能提升，平均MAE降低4.7%，RMSE降低5.0%，展现出优异的可扩展性和强适应性。

Conclusion: MP3作为一种即插即用的预训练插件，能有效区分时间幻象，显著提升现有STGNN的时空预测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Spatio-Temporal forecasting is crucial in diverse fields, such as transportation, climate, and energy. Urban spatio-temporal data exhibits temporal mirage: similar short-window inputs have divergent future trends, and vice versa. Existing spatio-temporal graph neural networks (STGNNs) cannot effectively identify such mirages. We argue that the core reason lies in the short-window inputs that have incomplete period observation, heterogeneous global spatial correlation, and cross-period superposition causality. To bridge this gap, we develop a novel Multi- Period Pattern Pre-training (MP3), a plug-and-play pre-training plugin for distinguishing temporal mirages. MP3 presents two core innovations: (1) The multi-period pattern learning is designed to learn multi-period patterns from long time series. Specifically, multi-period temporal modeling leverages edge convolution to identify different multi-period patterns. Multi-period spatial modeling uses a bottleneck project and a global memory bank to capture heterogeneous global spatial relations efficiently. Cross-period pattern interaction employs a causality-enhanced Transformer to capture dependencies across different period patterns. (2) This plugin can seamlessly integrate into existing STGNN backbones to strengthen their forecasting performance. The experiment on five STGNN baselines across five real-world datasets (including a large-scale dataset CA) verify the effectiveness, superior scalability and strong adaptability of MP3, which brings consistent and robust performance improvements across all evaluated baselines. On average, MP3 reduces the MAE 4.7% and the RMSE 5.0%. The code can be available at https://github.com/YAN-outlook/MP3.

</details>


### [295] [From Uncertain Judgments to Calibrated Rankings: Conformal Elo Estimation for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13221)
*Bora Kargi,David Salinas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种低成本评估框架SoftElo，通过局部校准（将硬标签替换为软概率引入Bradley-Terry模型）和全局校准（分裂共形预测为LLM与人类Elo差异提供分布自由的不确定区间），将LLM-as-a-judge的Elo评分误差从不可靠降至17.9 MAE，并为开发者提供校准后的Elo估计和可信的不确定边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模人类标注成本高昂，而LLM-as-a-judge虽廉价但存在位置偏差、自我偏好、不可传递性等系统误差，导致模型排名严重失准。需要一种既能利用LLM判断的低成本优势，又能对其误差进行量化校准的方法。

Method: 局部层面：将判断器输出的分数差异转换为校准后的胜率概率（软标签），而非硬分类标签，代入Bradley-Terry模型估计Elo。全局层面：对LLM导出与人类导出的Elo评分之间的残差应用分裂共形预测，生成具有分布自由边际覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在LMArena的55个保留模型上，局部校准使LLM导出的Elo与人类导出的Elo的平均绝对误差（MAE）降至17.9；全局共形预测提供了有效的边际覆盖保证的不确定区间。

Conclusion: 该两层次校准框架无需大规模人类标注即可获得接近人类质量的模型排名，并附带诚实的不确定度估计，为低成本模型评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Evaluating new large language models typically requires costly human annotation campaigns at scale. LLM-as-a-judge offers a cheaper alternative, but judge scores carry systematic errors - such as position bias, self-preference, or intransitivity - that can strongly miscalibrate the resulting rankings. We quantify the resulting judge-human disagreement at two complementary levels. At the local level, we estimate per-battle uncertainty from the judge's own score differences by propagating calibrated win probabilities rather than hard labels into the Bradley-Terry procedure. This alone provides a drastic improvement to Elo estimation accuracy, bringing LLM-derived ratings within 17.9 Elo MAE of human-derived ones when averaged over 55 held-out models on LMArena. At the global level, we apply split conformal prediction to the residual gap between LLM-derived and human-derived Elo ratings across held-out models, producing prediction intervals with distribution-free marginal coverage guarantees that account for irreducible LLM-human disagreement. Together, these two layers yield a low-cost evaluation tool that provides developers with calibrated Elo estimates and honest uncertainty bounds, without access to large-scale human annotations. To facilitate reproducibility, we release our code at https://github.com/kargibora/SoftElo .

</details>


### [296] [Quantizing Time-Series Models As Dynamical Systems: Trajectory-Based Quantization Sensitivity Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13300)
*Mariya Pavlova,Harrison Bo Hua Zhu,Elizsveta Semenova,Yingzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于轨迹的量化敏感度评分（TQS），将后训练量化（PTQ）重新定义为离散时间动力系统的稳定性问题，实现与量化器选择无关的先验敏感度估计，并构建无需标定数据和二阶近似的混合精度框架TQS-PTQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法中敏感度分析常与量化过程耦合，无法在量化器选择和位宽分配之前进行预算规划，尤其对黑盒或编译后的网络（含融合算子）缺乏灵活性和先验评估能力。

Method: 将网络 rollout 建模为离散时间动力系统，定义TQS指标刻画量化误差在 rollout  horizon上的传播与放大效应；该指标与量化器选择和位宽分配解耦，实现先验敏感度估计；基于此构建TQS-PTQ混合精度框架，无需标定数据和二阶近似。

Result: 实验表明，基于动力系统视角的TQS-PTQ在资源受限场景下提供了稳健且高性能的低精度部署路径。

Conclusion: 动力系统视角为PTQ提供了一种解耦敏感度分析与量化过程的鲁棒方法，适用于黑盒网络和资源受限环境下的混合精度部署。

Abstract: We introduce the Trajectory-based Quantization Sensitivity Score (TQS), a metric that reframes post-training quantization (PTQ) through the lens of dynamical-systems stability. By modeling the network's rollout as a discrete-time dynamical system, TQS characterizes how quantization-induced errors propagate and amplify over the rollout horizon. Unlike conventional PTQ methods, where sensitivity analysis is often coupled to the quantization procedure, TQS enables a priori sensitivity estimation decoupled from quantizer selection and bit-width assignment. This separation allows for quantization budget planning even for black-box or compiled networks with fused operators. Building on this, we present TQS-PTQ, a flexible mixed-precision framework that requires no calibration data or costly second-order approximations. Our experiments show that a dynamical-systems perspective provides a robust, high-performing pathway for low-precision deployment in resource-constrained settings.

</details>


### [297] [Dense Supervision, Sparse Updates: On the Sparsity and Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13657)
*Guo Yu,Wenlin Liu,Yulan Hu,Hao-Xuan Ma,Jun-Peng Jiang,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的参数更新特性，发现其更新具有稀疏性（集中在FFN模块、可仅训练子网络恢复性能）和几何特殊性（更新在数值上满秩但频谱集中、远离源权重的主奇异子空间、集中在源权重接近零的坐标上），表明密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写，而是保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管OPD结合了在线策略学生轨迹和密集教师监督这两个理想特性，并已成为流行的后训练方法，但其对模型参数的具体影响机制尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示OPD参数更新的结构特征，以深化对该方法的理解。

Method: 在多个语言和视觉-语言模型对上，针对OPD的不同使用场景进行分析。从稀疏性角度，测量更新幅度、层间分布，并通过子网络训练和优化器消融（AdamW vs SGD）验证；从几何角度，分析数值秩、频谱分布、与源权重奇异子空间的关系以及坐标分布特征。

Result: 发现：1）稀疏性方面：OPD更新幅度小且坐标稀疏，主要分布在FFN模块；仅训练发现的子网络可近乎恢复全量训练性能；AdamW优于SGD，说明密集教师监督保留了有用的动量结构和异构二阶矩尺度。2）几何方面：更新在数值上满秩但频谱集中，远离源权重的主奇异子空间，且不成比例地落在源权重接近零的坐标上。

Conclusion: 密集教师监督并未使OPD变成普通的密集参数重写；OPD保留了在线策略后训练的重要几何特征。这些发现深化了对OPD机制的理解，并为实际应用（如子网络训练、优化器选择）提供了指导。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (\textsc{OPD}) has recently become a prominent post-training recipe by combining two desirable ingredients: on-policy student trajectories and dense teacher supervision. However, how this hybrid changes a model's parameters remains unclear. Across several language and vision-language model pairs and \textsc{OPD} use cases, our analysis yields two main findings. On sparsity, \textsc{OPD} updates are small and coordinate-sparse. They are distributed across layers, with the largest relative movement usually appearing in FFN modules. This sparse structure is operationally useful: training only the discovered subnetwork nearly recovers full-training performance. The sparse support does not remove the need for adaptive optimization: SGD, previously reported to be competitive in \textsc{RLVR}, underperforms AdamW in our \textsc{OPD} optimizer ablation, suggesting that dense teacher supervision preserves useful momentum structure and heterogeneous second-moment scales. On geometry, the updates are numerically full-rank but spectrally concentrated; they lie mostly away from the principal singular subspaces of the source weights and fall disproportionately on coordinates where the source weights are close to zero. These findings suggest that dense teacher supervision does not turn \textsc{OPD} into ordinary dense parameter rewriting; instead, \textsc{OPD} retains important geometric signatures of on-policy post-training.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [298] [Fair Influence Maximization in Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14189)
*Zoë Abhelakh,Tianrui Mao,Huijuan Wang*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FIMH算法，用于超图上的公平影响力最大化问题。FIMH在SICP传播模型下，通过无参数乌托邦距离准则迭代权衡影响力和公平性选择种子节点。在7个真实超图上的实验表明，FIMH在保持与现有方法相当影响力的同时，显著降低了社区间的影响力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有影响力最大化研究忽略了社区间影响力分布的公平性问题，且超图上的公平影响力最大化问题尚未被探索。超图能更准确建模真实系统中的群体交互，因此需要设计适用于超图的公平影响力最大化算法。

Method: 提出FIMH启发式种子选择算法，在SICP传播模型下，迭代估计每个候选节点对影响力和公平性的贡献，使用无参数的乌托邦距离准则权衡这两个目标，选择最优节点加入种子集。

Result: 在7个真实超图数据集上，FIMH达到了与最先进IM方法相当的影响力水平，同时显著降低了影响力差异（即社区间的不公平性）。种子节点的拓扑属性分析进一步验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: FIMH有效解决了超图上的公平影响力最大化问题，在保持影响力的同时实现了更公平的社区间影响力分布，为超图上的社会影响传播和群体交互场景提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The influence maximization problem aims to select a set of seed nodes that maximize the influence, i.e., the average number of influenced nodes, at the end of a spreading process. It has been widely studied with applications in viral marketing, public health campaigns, and social influence. In networks with pronounced community structure, existing approaches often yield an uneven distribution of influenced nodes across communities, which is unfair. Although the fair influence maximization (FIM) problem has been studied for pairwise networks, it remains largely unexplored for hyper graphs, which more accurately represent real-world systems involving group interactions. We introduce FIMH, a heuristic seed-selection algorithm for FIM on hyper graphs, under the Susceptible-Infected Contact Process (SICP) spreading model. FIMH iteratively estimates the contribution of each candidate node to influence and fairness and selects the node that best trades off these two objectives as an additional seed using a parameter-free utopia-distance criterion. Experiments on seven real-world hypergraphs demonstrate that FIMH achieves an influence comparable to that of state-of-the-art IM methods while significantly reducing influence disparity. Analysis of the topological properties of the selected seed nodes and their contributions to influence and fairness further supports the effectiveness of FIMH.

</details>


### [299] [Games Mapper: Topological Data Analysis of Steam Genres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14376)
*Nicolas Grelier,Stéphane Kaufmann,Johannes Pfau*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出Games Mapper，一种基于拓扑数据分析中Mapper算法的分析工具，用于捕捉视频游戏类型随时间演化的连续拓扑关系，并通过自动化聚类标注方法实现可解释的可视化。基于Steam平台2015-2025年模拟游戏数据的案例研究证明，该工具能自主分割出连贯的子类型并捕捉市场动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频游戏行业主题和类型不断演变，工作室和发行商需要理解特定游戏类别的结构动态和时间演化，以识别可行的市场切入点，但传统聚类技术无法捕捉数据间的连续拓扑关系。

Method: 基于拓扑数据分析中的Mapper算法，扩展了标准算法，增加了自动化聚类标注方法，生成高可解释性和交互式的类型演化可视化。

Result: 在Steam平台2015-2025年模拟游戏数据上的案例研究表明，Games Mapper能够自主将游戏类型分割成连贯、持久的子类型，并捕捉动态市场变化。

Conclusion: Games Mapper是一种可扩展、通用化的工具，可用于研究者和行业从业者解析复杂的市场结构并追踪Steam生态系统的演化。

Abstract: The video game industry comprises a vast, continuously evolving landscape of themes and genres. For studios and publishers that navigate this competitive market, understanding the structural dynamics and temporal evolution of specific game categories is crucial for identifying viable entry points. In this paper, we introduce Games Mapper, a novel analytical tool based on the Mapper algorithm from topological data analysis. Unlike traditional clustering techniques, Games Mapper captures the continuous topological relationships between datasets over time (or other guiding variables). We extend the standard algorithm with an automated cluster labelling method, ensuring highly interpretable and interactive visualisations of genre evolution. To demonstrate the efficacy of our approach, we present a comprehensive case study on Simulation games released on Steam between 2015 and 2025. Games Mapper autonomously segments the genre into coherent, persistent subgenres, and captures dynamic market shifts. Ultimately, we provide a scalable, generalisable tool for researchers and industrials to unravel complex market structures and track the evolution of the Steam ecosystem.

</details>


### [300] [Understanding the Personal Networks of People Experiencing Homelessness in King County, WA with aggregate Relational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.13517)
*Zack Almquist,Ihsan Kahveci,Owen Kajfasz,Janelle Rothfolk,Amy Hagopian*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 基于2022-2024年对西雅图金县3000多名无家可归者的调查数据，研究发现无家可归者的社会网络连接性持续下降：平均熟人数量从80人降至40人，亲密朋友数量保持稳定但相对比例下降，亲属网络有所扩大。这些趋势表明社会隔离加剧，可能由流离失所和新近无家可归者增加导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无家可归者的社会网络是研究中被忽视但至关重要的方面，影响着他们获取信息、支持和安全的能力。鉴于美国无家可归者数量持续增长（2023年较2022年增长23%），有必要系统研究这一群体的社会网络结构及其变化趋势。

Method: 采用基于网络的抽样方法，在2022-2024年间收集华盛顿州金县超过3000名无家可归者的调查数据，分别分析其熟人网络、亲密朋友网络、亲属网络和同伴推荐网络。

Result: (1) 平均熟人数量从2023年的80人降至2024年的40人；(2) 亲密朋友数量稳定在2.5人左右，但鉴于无家可归人口增长，表明网络连接性下降；(3) 亲属网络扩大，更多无家可归者的家庭成员也处于无家可归状态；(4) 总体呈现社会连接性下降趋势，可能由流离失所和新近无家可归者增加导致。

Conclusion: 无家可归者正经历日益加剧的社会隔离，这可能减少信息共享和互助的机会。政策应关注促进社会连接和社区建设，如减少流离失所和提供公共聚集空间，以应对无家可归者群体中日益增长的失范状态。

Abstract: The social networks of people experiencing homelessness are an understudied but vital aspect of their lives, offering access to information, support, and safety. In 2023, the U.S. Department of Housing and Urban Development reported 653,100 people experiencing homelessness on any given night -- a 23% rise since 2022, though likely an undercount. This paper examines a unique three-year dataset (2022-2024) of survey responses from over 3,000 unhoused individuals in King County, WA, collected via network-based sampling methods to estimate the unsheltered population. Our study analyzes the networks of the unsheltered population, focusing on acquaintance, close friendship, kinship, and peer referral networks. Findings reveal a decline in social connectivity over time. The average number of acquaintances dropped from 80 in 2023 to 40 in 2024. Close friendship levels remained stable at 2.5, but given the growth in the homeless population, this suggests decreased network connectivity. Kinship networks expanded, indicating that more family members of unhoused individuals are also experiencing homelessness. These trends suggest increasing social disconnection, possibly driven by displacement and a rise in newly homeless individuals. The growing isolation may reduce opportunities for information sharing and mutual support. However, the increased reliance on family networks highlights the shifting dynamics of social support within this community. This research underscores the need for policies fostering social connections and community building, such as reducing displacement and providing spaces for congregation, to counter the growing anomie among unhoused populations.

</details>


### [301] [Climate Policy Elites' Twitter Interactions across Nine Countries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.15545)
*Ted Hsuan Yun Chen,Arttu Malkamäki,Ali Faqeeh,Esa Palosaari,Anniina Kotkaniemi,Hasti Narimanzadeh,Laura Funke,Cáit Gleeson,James Goodman,Antti Gronow,Marlene Kammerer,Myanna Lahsen,Alexandre Marques,Petr Ocelik,Shivangi Seth,Mark Stoddart,Martin Svozil,Pradip Swarnakar,Matthew Trull,Paul Wagner,Yixi Yang,Mikko Kivelä,Tuomas Ylä-Anttila*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究收集了9个国家904个气候政策行动者在Twitter（2017-2022年）上的4000万条活动数据，从多中心治理视角分析其互动模式。发现各国政策系统对气候事件的响应具有时间与跨国差异但模式相似，且国家系统间缺乏直接互动，而是通过共同参与国际组织、气候活动家和研究者发布的内容间接连接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体已成为气候政策行动者互动与政治博弈的关键场域，但现有研究缺乏对跨国家、多层级气候政策行动者在线互动模式的大规模系统分析。本文旨在填补这一空白，从多中心治理理论视角，理解公私边界模糊如何塑造在线政策过程。

Method: 识别9个国家中904个气候政策行动者的Twitter账号，收集2017-2022年间16,086个账号共4000万条活动数据，采用多中心治理框架进行网络分析与比较研究。

Result: 各国气候活动在时间序列和跨国层面存在显著差异，但所有国家政策系统对气候抗议等事件呈现相似的响应模式；国家政策系统之间很少直接互动，而是通过共同关注国际组织、气候活动家和研究者发布的内容实现间接连接。

Conclusion: 气候政策行动者在社交媒体上的互动呈现多中心但碎片化的特征，国家系统间的直接协作不足，国际组织与行动者扮演了关键的"连接枢纽"角色。这提示需要加强跨国政策系统间的直接对话与协调。

Abstract: Social media is an important space for interactions between climate policy actors, with a burgeoning literature recognizing them as critical platforms of political contestation. We identified Twitter accounts associated with 904 climate change policy actors across nine countries, and collected their activities from 2017--2022, totalling 40 million activities from 16,086 accounts at different organizational levels. We studied these actors and their interactions as a polycentric governance system, emphasizing how boundary blurring between the public and private on social media platforms uniquely shapes the online policy process. Initial results show there is considerable temporal and cross-national variation in how prominent climate-related activities were, but all national policy systems generally responded to climate-related events, such as climate protests, in a similar manner. Examining patterns of interaction within and across countries, we find that these national policy systems rarely directly interact with one another, but are connected through consistently engaging with the same content produced by accounts of international organizations, climate activists, and researchers.

</details>


### [302] [From Birdwatch to Community Notes, from Twitter to X: four years of community-based content moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09585)
*Saeedeh Mohammadi,Narges Chinichian,Hannah Doyal,Anna Bertani,Kristina Skutilova,Hao Cui,Michele d'Errico,Siobhan Grayson,Taha Yasseri*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文对X平台（原Twitter）于2021年1月启动的大规模众包内容审核机制——Community Notes（原Birdwatch）进行了前四年的描述性研究，涵盖语言多样性、信息来源、贡献者活动、评分行为及互动网络，并发布了 curated 数据集、源代码及文献综述以支持后续研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Community Notes模式在其他社交平台推广，亟需系统评估其运行机制与实际效果，为社区驱动的内容审核提供实证基础。

Method: 解析Community Notes前四年的Notes与评分数据，进行语种检测；对英文Notes提取嵌入URL并识别讨论主题；构建贡献者月度互动网络，进行描述性分析。

Result: 形成了涵盖语言多样性、信息来源、贡献者活动、评分行为及互动网络的描述性分析结果，并发布了数据集、代码及文献综述。

Conclusion: 本文的描述性分析、数据集、代码及文献综述为推进Community Notes及社区式内容审核的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Community Notes (formerly known as Birdwatch) is the first large-scale crowdsourced content moderation initiative launched by X (formerly Twitter) in January 2021. As the Community Notes model gains momentum across other social media platforms, there is a growing need to assess its underlying dynamics and effectiveness. This paper provides a descriptive investigation of Community Notes during its first four years, examining its linguistic diversity, sourcing practices, Contributor activity, rating behaviour, and interaction networks. In addition, we release a curated dataset and accompanying source code to support future research, along with a review of prior research on Community Notes. We parsed Notes and ratings data from the first four years of the program and conducted language detection across all Notes. For English-language Notes, we extracted embedded URLs and identified discussion topics in each Note. Additionally, we constructed monthly interaction networks among the Contributors. Together, the descriptive analysis, dataset, code, and literature review provide a foundation for advancing research on Community Notes and community-based content moderation more broadly.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [303] [Decoding Semantic Categories from Picture-Naming EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14614)
*Wei Hu,Binbin Xu*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究通过高密度脑电图(EEG)和深度学习解码方法，证明了在图片命名任务中，语义类别信息可以从脑电活动中解码出来，且早期感知窗口和晚期命名窗口的EEG信息具有互补性，九类语义分类的平衡准确率达到0.781。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在图片命名过程中，语义类别信息是否可以从高密度EEG信号中恢复出来，以验证现代神经解码方法在口语产出中研究词汇-语义处理的可行性。

Method: 16名法语母语者执行线条画图片命名任务；使用多语言文本嵌入模型将图片标签嵌入并组织为9个可解释语义类别；采用预训练的单通道EEG编码器对早期刺激后窗口、命名相关窗口及两者组合的通道级EEG活动进行表征，并进行九类语义解码。

Result: 所有时间窗口的语义类别判别均高于偶然水平；平衡准确率从早期窗口的0.562提升至命名窗口的0.610，两者组合后达到0.781，最高Macro-F1为0.784；各类别的F1分数均有一致提升，传感器级解码图显示空间分布式的类别信息。

Conclusion: 语义类别结构在图片命名过程的EEG活动中有所反映，早期和命名相关的时间窗口提供互补信息，支持使用现代神经解码方法研究口语产出中的词汇-语义加工。

Abstract: Picture naming requires the transformation of visual object information into a spoken lexical response through perceptual, semantic, lexical, and articulatory processes. This study asked whether semantic-category information is recoverable from high-density EEG during overt picture naming. Sixteen native French-speaking participants performed a picture-naming task using line drawings. Picture labels were embedded with a multilingual text-embedding model and organized into nine interpretable semantic categories, providing a data-driven semantic target space for neural decoding. EEG activity was represented channel-wise using a pre-trained single-channel EEG encoder over an early post-stimulus window, a later naming-related window, and their combination. Nine-class decoding showed above-chance semantic-category discrimination in all temporal representations. Balanced accuracy increased from 0.562 in the early window to 0.610 in the naming-related window, and reached 0.781 when both windows were combined, with a maximum Macro-F1 of 0.784. Class-level F1 scores showed consistent gains across semantic categories, and sensor-level decoding maps indicated spatially distributed category information. These findings suggest that semantic-category structure is reflected in EEG activity during overt picture naming and that early and naming-related temporal windows provide complementary information. The results support the use of modern neural decoding methods as tools for investigating lexical-semantic processing in spoken language production.

</details>


### [304] [Prospective Coding and Path Integration Emerge as Equilibrium Solutions of Self-Organizing Neural Networks with Firing-Rate Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14649)
*Facundo Emina,Emilio Kropff*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个理论框架，揭示连续吸引子神经网络（CANNs）的循环连接结构如何通过赫布可塑性、放电率适应和全局抑制自组织涌现。平移不变输入自发形成高斯型前馈权重，且前馈架构本身即可产生预期性活动偏移（前瞻编码），无需循环兴奋性侧支；引入循环交互后可学习自持活动凸包，并通过外部时变基线电流（编码速度）调节，实现精确的单向路径积分。研究表明，前瞻编码和路径积分是自组织竞争网络自然共生的属性，而非人工设计的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CANN依赖预先设定的循环连接来建模空间表征、路径积分和预期动力学，但该结构化连接如何通过学习涌现的生物学机制尚不清楚。本文旨在揭示这一自组织过程。

Method: 建立理论框架，结合赫布可塑性、放电率适应和全局抑制机制，分析平移不变输入下前馈权重的自组织（高斯轮廓），并考察前馈架构中预期性偏移的自发产生、多层网络的线性放大、循环连接的自持凸包学习，以及外部基线电流调制下的速度编码和路径积分。

Result: 平移不变输入驱动稳定高斯型前馈权重涌现；前馈架构自发产生预期性活动凸包偏移；该偏移可被多层网络线性放大；引入循环交互后可学习自持移动凸包；通过外部时变基线电流（编码速度）调制，系统可作为精确的单向路径积分器。

Conclusion: 前瞻编码和路径积分不是人工设计的特征，而是自组织竞争网络自然共生的涌现属性，为内嗅皮层浅层预期活动的形成机制提供了理论解释。

Abstract: Continuous Attractor Neural Networks (CANNs) traditionally rely on pre-wired recurrent connectivity to model spatial representations, path integration, and anticipatory dynamics. However, the biological mechanisms through which this structured connectivity emerges via learning remain relatively unexplored. This work presents a theoretical framework revealing how continuous attractor connectivity and its computational properties self-organize through Hebbian plasticity, firing-rate adaptation, and global inhibition. We show that translationally invariant inputs naturally drive the emergence of stable, Gaussian-profiled feedforward weights. Crucially, anticipatory dynamics arise spontaneously within these feedforward architectures, shifting the activity bump forward without requiring recurrent excitatory collaterals. This predictive shift can be linearly amplified across multilayer networks, consistent with anticipatory activity observed in the superficial layers of the entorhinal cortex. Furthermore, introducing recurrent interactions allows the network to learn connections capable of self-sustaining a moving bump of activity. Finally, by modulating the network with an external, time-varying baseline current that encodes speed, the system adjusts its intrinsic velocity to function as a precise unidirectional path integrator. Ultimately, this study suggests that prospective coding and path integration are not manually engineered features, but rather naturally co-emergent properties of a single self-organizing competitive network.

</details>


### [305] [Implications of hierarchical Markov models of behavior: on irreversibility, predictability, and dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14692)
*John J. Vastola,Kanaka Rajan*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了用马尔可夫模型描述动物行为序列的理论含义，指出模型特征值和特征向量可解释行为的时间尺度及相应修改，并通过玩具示例和真实数据说明马尔可夫表示对量化行为理论意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量化工具（尤其是将自发行为表示为神经可定义的“音节”序列的工具）日趋成熟，领域需要重新审视一个基本理论问题：如果行为的粗粒度结构能被马尔可夫模型准确描述，这些模型究竟告诉我们关于行为的什么信息？

Method: 从理论上探讨马尔可夫模型的行为序列含义，分析模型关联矩阵的特征值和特征向量，并用玩具示例和真实数据拟合的马尔可夫模型进行说明。

Result: 发现模型关联矩阵的特征值和特征向量提供了可解释的行为时间尺度以及在该时间尺度上发生的行为修改，从而阐明了量化行为的理论意义。

Conclusion: 通过分析马尔可夫表示的后果，澄清了行为量化进展的理论含义，为理解行为序列性质和有效维度提供了定量框架。

Abstract: The maturation of quantitative tools for studying the high-level structure of animal behavior, and especially tools which represent spontaneous behavior as a sequence of stereotyped and neurally well-defined 'syllables', demands that the field revisit a fundamental theoretical question: if the coarse structure of behavior can be accurately described by Markov models, what do these models really tell us about behavior? In this work, we explore the theoretical implications of these models and discuss how they allow us to quantitatively formulate questions about the sequence-like nature and effective dimensionality of behavior. One important insight is that the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of various model-associated matrices furnish interpretable time scales and modifications of behavior that occur on those time scales. We illustrate our points using both toy examples and Markov models fit to real data. By analyzing the consequences of Markov representations, we clarify the theoretical meaning of progress in quantifying behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [306] [Occupancy-Grounded Room Segmentation for Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13727)
*Carlos Cueto Zumaya,Iacopo Catalano,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于占用空间分解的层次化3D场景图构建方法，通过将房间节点锚定到追踪的自由空间区域并生成显式多边形足迹，在12个Matterport3D场景上相比于现有基线方法（Hydra）恢复了更多房间实例，但精度较低，且墙准确的房间边界仍是开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次化3D场景图中房间层的构建依赖不同的空间基底（如位置聚类、墙平面或分割输出），导致房间节点缺乏统一的几何评估标准，无法在不同方法间进行公平比较。

Method: 提出占用空间锚定的3DSG管线，将房间节点锚定到由占用分解追踪的自由空间区域，为每个房间生成显式的多边形足迹；在12个Matterport3D场景上，将预测的房间多边形与标注的房间实例进行匹配评估，并与Hydra方法对比。

Result: 占用空间锚定方法恢复了比Hydra（位置连通性基线）更多的房间实例，但精度较低；两种方法在获得墙准确的房间边界方面仍存在挑战。

Conclusion: 占用空间分解为房间层构建提供了一种更统一的几何评估基础，但房间边界的精确恢复仍是开放问题，需进一步研究。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs (3DSGs) for indoor robots organize geometric and semantic information across spatial scales, with a room layer that connects object-level perception to room-scale reasoning. Existing systems construct this layer from different spatial substrates (\eg{} place clusters, wall planes, or segmentation outputs), and as a result, room nodes are not evaluated on a common geometric criterion. We present an occupancy-grounded 3DSG pipeline in which room nodes are anchored to tracked free-space regions derived from occupancy decomposition, giving each room an explicit polygonal footprint. We evaluate the pipeline on 12 Matterport3D scenes by matching predicted room polygons to annotated room instances and compare against Hydra, a representative state-of-the-art place-connectivity baseline. The results show that occupancy-grounded anchoring recovers substantially more room instances than place-connectivity construction, at the cost of lower precision, and that wall-accurate room boundaries remain an open problem for both methods. Code is available at https://github.com/crcz25/OccuSG.

</details>


### [307] [Scalable Dynamic Tactile Sensing Enabled by Passive and Flexible Acoustic Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13746)
*Guimin Long,Changhong Linghu,Chuanping Liu,Ke Xu,Xingjian Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深亚波长声波导的被动分布式触觉传感范式，利用弹性膜帽 Helmholtz 谐振器和弹簧增强微管网络，在宏观弯曲下保持声学传输不变，通过稀疏麦克风实现高精度触觉定位与波形重建，具有可扩展、柔性和低成本特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态触觉传感技术在扩展到大面积阵列时面临灵敏度、鲁棒性与柔顺性之间的权衡，且布线复杂度和成本高，亟需一种解耦性能与结构柔性的新范式。

Method: 采用深亚波长声波导的被动分布式架构：弹性膜帽封装的 Helmholtz 谐振器通过弹簧增强微管互连形成封闭网络，在宏观弯曲下保持声学传输不变；稀疏嵌入麦克风，结合快速连续小波变换和轻量级神经网络实现实时推理。

Result: 系统实现4mm最高空间分辨率，4麦克风64节点传感阵列准确率>99%，低频信号(<100 Hz)波形重建，推理时间5.5ms。成功演示指尖阵列、触觉手套、大面积皮肤等多种形态，可检测从单根头发接触到5mg微粒冲击、动脉脉搏波、羽毛触碰和手指接触等刺激。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一种可扩展、柔性、低成本的新一代人机界面触觉传感范式，有效解决了现有技术在规模扩展时面临的性能-柔性权衡问题。

Abstract: Artificial dynamic tactile sensing requires sensitivity, robustness, and compliance, yet existing technologies face trade-offs when scaling to large-area arrays, compounded by wiring complexity and cost. Here, we report a passive distributed paradigm using deep sub-wavelength acoustic waveguides that decouples performance from structural flexibility. Elastic-membrane-capped Helmholtz resonators interconnected by spring-reinforced microtubes form an enclosed network with invariant acoustic transmission under macroscopic bending. By sparsely embedding microphones, the system achieves real-time localization (4 mm highest spatial resolution; >99% accuracy in a 4 microphones 64-node sensing array) and waveform reconstruction of low-frequency signals (<100 Hz). Fast Continuous Wavelet Transform and a lightweight neural network enable inference within 5.5 ms. We demonstrate conformable prototypes-fingertip arrays, a tactile glove, and large-area skins-detecting stimuli from single-hair contact to 5-mg particle impacts, arterial pulse waves, feather touches, and finger contact. This establishes a scalable, flexible, low-cost paradigm for next-generation human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [308] [$μ_0$: A Scalable 3D Interaction-Trace World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13769)
*Seungjae Lee,Yoonkyo Jung,Jusuk Lee,Jonghun Shin,Amir Hossein Shahidzadeh,Yao-Chih Lee,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出μ₀，一种基于3D轨迹的可扩展世界模型。它通过预测交互点的平滑3D轨迹（而非像素或动作）实现跨本体、与动作无关的视觉运动表征。利用TraceExtract系统从多样化视频中自动提取3D监督信号进行预训练，预训练后的μ₀可冻结复用，与下游动作专家结合形成轨迹条件策略，在无动作监督的情况下达到与VLA模型竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型面临两难：像素空间视频模型虽提供广泛视觉先验但需耗费大量容量重建密集外观；直接动作模型虽高效但依赖具身特定标签，限制了跨本体可扩展性。因此需要一种既保留视觉先验可扩展性、又避免密集重建和动作标签依赖的紧凑运动表征。

Method: μ₀采用3D轨迹作为预测目标。1) TraceExtract系统：从视频中自动选择关键点、构建全局对齐的3D轨迹、用层次化语言描述关联运动片段。2) 模型架构：结合预训练的视觉-语言骨干网络与模块化的轨迹专家模块，每个查询通过B样条控制点表示并预测未来轨迹。训练数据来自多样化视频源，无需动作标签。

Result: μ₀在2D和3D轨迹预测任务上均优于基线方法（包括轨迹预测模型和基于token化的VLM方法）。冻结的μ₀与下游动作专家结合后，所形成的轨迹条件策略在无动作预训练的情况下，性能与使用动作监督的VLA模型（如π₀）相当。

Conclusion: 3D轨迹是一种可扩展且可迁移的跨本体操作表征。μ₀证明了在无动作标签条件下，通过学习紧凑的3D运动接口，能够实现与有监督VLA模型竞争的性能，为机器人学习的可扩展预训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: World models that capture how actions induce physical change enable scalable robot learning without reliance on embodiment-specific action labels. Pixel-space video models provide broad visual priors but expend model capacity on dense appearance reconstruction, while direct action models require embodiment-specific labels that hinder scalability. We present $μ_0$, a scalable world model based on 3D traces. Rather than predicting dense pixels or directly modeling actions, $μ_0$ forecasts smooth 3D trajectories for salient interaction points such as objects, tools, hands, and contact regions, yielding a compact, embodiment-agnostic motion interface. To enable training from diverse video sources, our TraceExtract system automatically extracts 3D supervision by selecting keypoints, constructing globally aligned traces, and associating motion segments with hierarchical language captions. This TraceExtract supervision pretrains $μ_0$ by combining a pretrained vision-language backbone with a modular trace expert, which represents each query via B-spline control points and predicts future traces. Experiments show that $μ_0$ outperforms baselines in both 2D and 3D trace prediction, including trace prediction models and tokenized VLM methods. Because $μ_0$ is frozen and reusable, it can be paired with action experts for downstream robot embodiments. Despite action-free pretraining, the resulting trace-conditioned policies achieve performance competitive with VLA models pretrained with action supervision, such as $π_0$. These results establish 3D traces as a scalable and transferable representation for cross-embodiment manipulation.

</details>


### [309] [FlowMo-WM: A World Model with Object Momentum and Hidden Ambient Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13817)
*Yitao Jiang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlowMo-WM，一种端到端可训练的视觉世界模型，通过将图像-动作历史分解为短历史潜状态（物体中心运动）和长历史上下文（隐藏漂移），在没有流场直接监督的情况下，提升水下车在隐藏水流等漂移环境中的长期预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型多在即时控制主导的运动场景中评估，而水下车等真实物体受惯性和隐藏环境漂移（如水流、风）影响，现有模型难以处理这类长期依赖的缓慢变化外生因素。

Method: FlowMo-WM将图像-动作历史分解为短历史潜状态（总结物体中心运动）和长历史上下文（总结缓慢变化的外生影响），采用零上下文残差过渡分离动作条件基础动力学与上下文相关漂移效应，在潜空间滚动预测。

Result: 在模拟水下环境（含隐藏流、扰动和随机车辆动力学）中，FlowMo-WM在长期滚动预测精度上优于代表性动作条件潜世界模型；上下文消融实验表明上下文对隐藏漂移下的稳定预测至关重要；冻结线性探针刻画了学习因子中编码的信息。

Conclusion: FlowMo-WM通过显式建模隐藏环境漂移的慢变上下文，有效提升了动作条件世界模型在惯性主导和非稳态环境中的长期预测能力。

Abstract: World models in robot learning predict future states from visual observations and actions, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of their controls. However, many action-conditioned models are evaluated in settings where motion is dominated by immediate control, whereas aquatic surface vehicles and other real-world objects continue moving under inertia and are displaced by hidden ambient drift, such as water currents or wind. We propose FlowMo-WM, an end-to-end trainable visual world model that infers object-centric motion state and a predictive long-history context associated with hidden drift from image-action histories without direct supervision of flow fields. FlowMo-WM factorizes image-action history into a short-history latent state, trained to summarize object-centric motion, and a longer-history context, trained to summarize slowly varying exogenous influences. A zero-context residual transition separates action-conditioned base dynamics from context-dependent drift effects during latent rollout. In simulated aquatic surface-vehicle environments with diverse hidden flows, disturbances, and randomized vehicle dynamics, FlowMo-WM improves long-horizon rollout accuracy over representative action-conditioned latent world models. Prediction-time context ablations, in which the inferred context is zeroed or shuffled during rollout, show that the ambient context is important for stable prediction under hidden drift, while frozen linear probes characterize information encoded in the learned factors.

</details>


### [310] [Multi-Agent Embodied Autonomous Driving: From V2X Information Exchange to Shared World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13840)
*Senkang Hu,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Zihan Fang,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述调研了380余篇文献，系统分析了自动驾驶从单车智能向多智能体具身系统转变过程中共享世界模型（SWMs）在V2X通信、协同感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶及闭环仿真验证等方面的研究进展，指出了当前评估集中于仿真和离线协议、缺乏实时安全保障等局限性，并提出了可验证共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐、安全协调行动等未来研究优先级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶正从独立车辆智能向多智能体具身系统转变，需要车辆之间共享感知、推断意图并在不确定性下协调行动。现有研究缺乏对这一转变过程中共享世界模型（SWMs）的系统性梳理，亟需整合跨领域的相关工作并明确关键研究空白。

Method: 综述超过380篇文献，从共享世界模型的角度，系统覆盖V2X通信、协同感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶、仿真与数据引擎等子领域，分析观测数据如何转化为对齐的状态表示、意图感知交互和协调的下游行动。

Result: 发现当前评估高度集中于仿真环境、标准基准和离线协议；基于基础模型的协调缺乏在开放交通中经过验证的实时安全保障；明确提出了多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的三大核心研究优先级。

Conclusion: 未来多智能体具身自动驾驶的核心挑战在于：可验证的共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与计划对齐，以及在通信、延迟和部署约束下的安全协调行动，这些构成了推动该领域发展的关键研究方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is shifting from isolated vehicle intelligence toward multi-agent embodied systems that share perception, infer intent, and coordinate action under uncertainty. This survey examines this transition through the lens of Shared World Models (SWMs): predictive cross-agent representations maintained across vehicles, infrastructure, and other traffic participants. We review more than 380 publications spanning vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, collaborative perception, inter-agent cognition, cooperative planning, end-to-end cooperative driving, and simulation and data engines for closed-loop validation. The organizing question is how exchanged observations become aligned state, intent-aware interaction, and coordinated downstream action. Across the surveyed literature, evaluation remains concentrated in simulation, curated benchmarks, and offline protocols. Foundation-model-based coordination also lacks verified real-time safety guarantees in open traffic. These gaps motivate key research priorities for multi-agent embodied autonomous driving (MAEAD): verifiable shared-state maintenance, robust intent and plan alignment, and safe coordinated action under communication, latency, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [311] [Efficient Domain-Adaptive Policy Learning via Kernel Representation with Application to Quadrotor Control under Non-Stationary Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13842)
*Hongyu Zhou,Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于核表示的高效域自适应策略学习算法，通过随机傅里叶特征建模未知扰动，在离线训练中通过可微仿真优化策略（仅需50秒），在线部署时通过最小二乘实时更新核参数，在四旋翼轨迹跟踪任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域自适应策略学习面临环境表示既要足够表达复杂的sim-to-real差距，又要支持快速在线适应的挑战，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 使用基于随机傅里叶特征的可微核近似建模未知扰动；离线阶段随机采样核系数和带宽参数生成多样扰动分布，通过可微仿真与解析梯度优化策略；在线部署阶段通过最小二乘估计实时更新核系数和带宽。

Result: 在Crazyflie四旋翼的高保真数值仿真和硬件实验中，在风扰、地面效应、载荷波动等多种扰动下实现了有效轨迹跟踪。

Conclusion: 该方法结合了离线可微仿真的高效训练与在线自适应能力，为域自适应策略学习提供了一种兼顾表达力和计算效率的方案。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for efficient domain-adaptive policy learning via kernel representations. Learning domain-adaptive policies is challenging since it requires an environment representation that is both sufficiently expressive to model complex sim-to-real gaps during offline training, and computationally efficient enough to support rapid online adaptation during deployment. For instance, a quadrotor may encounter time-varying, non-stationary disturbances, such as sudden gusts of wind, payload shifts, or transitions between distinct flight regimes with and without ground effects. To address these challenges, we model unknown disturbances using a differentiable kernel approximation based on random Fourier features. During the offline training phase, we randomly sample kernel coefficients and bandwidth parameters to generate a rich diversity of disturbance profiles. We then optimize the control policy via differentiable simulation with analytical gradients, a process that takes only 50 seconds of training time on an RTX 4090 GPU. During hardware deployment, the policy adapts to non-stationary environments in real time by updating both the kernel coefficients and bandwidth through online least-squares estimation. We evaluate our method on quadrotor trajectory tracking tasks across high-fidelity numerical simulations and hardware experiments using Crazyflie, subjected to various disturbances, including complex aerodynamic effects, wind, ground effects, and payload fluctuations.

</details>


### [312] [Output-Level Regularization Eliminates the Seed Lottery in Single-GPU VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13856)
*Jeffrin Sam,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 同一份微调代码运行13次，12次成功（91-94%），1次性能骤降至65.2%且无任何警告。这种现象被称为"种子彩票"，根源在于输出坍缩——动作预测器学习输出无关输入的近似相同结果。权重级正则化方法（L2、EWC）对此无效，而输出级正则化（VICReg、Dropout、减半学习率）可完全消除灾难性种子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现并解释VLA模型微调中存在的一个隐藏风险：相同数据、架构下，不同随机种子会导致个别训练运行性能大幅下降且无任何错误提示。

Method: 通过Jacobian零空间理论形式化输出坍缩现象，对比7种方法（含权重级L2/EWC和输出级VICReg/Dropout/减半学习率）在13个种子×3个LIBERO基准上的表现。

Result: 输出级正则化方法（VICReg n=12, Dropout n=4, 减半学习率 n=5）完全消除了灾难性种子（0/21坍缩 vs 基线1/13, F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001），而权重级方法无法解决该问题。

Conclusion: 最简修复方案是修改优化器配置中的一个数字（减半学习率），即可消除种子彩票现象。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action model (VLA-JEPA) on a single GPU should be simple: load a pretrained checkpoint, run training, deploy. There is a hidden danger. Run the same fine-tuning code thirteen times -- same data, same architecture, different random seed -- and twelve runs produce a robot succeeding 91--94% of the time, while one run silently degrades to 65.2%: a 29 pp gap with no error message, no warning, and no way to predict which seed will fail. We call this the seed lottery. We trace the cause to output collapse: the action predictor quietly learns to produce nearly identical outputs regardless of what the robot sees. Existing weight-level methods (L2, EWC) are structurally blind to this collapse -- they penalize weight changes, but collapse occurs in directions weights can move freely without affecting outputs, a gap we formalize via the Jacobian null-space. Across 7 methods x up to 13 seeds x 3 LIBERO benchmarks, three output-level regularizers -- VICReg (n=12 seeds), Dropout (n=4), and a halved learning rate (n=5) -- each eliminate every catastrophic seed (0/21 combined collapses vs. 1/13 Baseline; F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001), while weight-level methods (L2, EWC) preserve the lottery. The simplest fix is changing one number in your optimizer config.

</details>


### [313] [ContactWorld: What Matters in Vision-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13877)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Pokuang Zhou,Kaidi Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Temitope Amosa,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Jiuzhou Lei,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过ContactWorld基准系统研究了接触丰富操作中视觉-触觉世界模型的表示属性，发现空间结构化且时间连续的表示（如点云）能显著提升规划性能，触觉传感的有效性依赖于跨模态表示兼容性，且在长时域规划中触觉信息日益重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要世界模型从多模态感官观测中推理复杂接触动力学，但目前尚不清楚何种表示属性能够稳定支持长时域规划，因此有必要系统研究视觉-触觉世界模型的表示特性。

Method: 构建ContactWorld基准，包含12项接触丰富操作任务（插入、拆卸、旋拧、探索性交互等），系统对比不同观测表示（腕部视角、前视角、点云）及触觉力场表示在规划性能上的表现。

Result: 点云观测将平均规划成功率从腕部视角的20.7%和前视角的22.0%提升至32.1%；结合点云与触觉力场表示进一步将性能提升至36.1%，为所有评估任务中最强规划性能；触觉传感在长时域规划目标下日益重要。

Conclusion: 空间结构化且时间连续的表示、跨模态表示兼容性以及长时域鲁棒性是视觉-触觉世界模型用于接触丰富机器人操作的关键设计原则。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires world models to reason over complex contact dynamics from multimodal sensory observations. However, it remains unclear which representation properties fundamentally support stable long-horizon planning in contact-rich settings. In this paper, we present ContactWorld, a benchmark and systematic empirical study of vision-tactile world models spanning 12 contact-rich manipulation tasks, including insertion, disassembly, screwing, and exploratory interaction. Across extensive experiments, we find that representations that are both spatially structured and temporally continuous consistently achieve the strongest planning performance. In particular, point-cloud observations improve average planning success rates from 20.7% with wrist-view observations and 22.0% with front-view observations to 32.1%. We further find that the effectiveness of tactile sensing depends critically on cross-modal representation compatibility rather than modality scaling alone. Combining point-cloud observations with tactile force-field representations, which preserve richer spatial structure and interaction dynamics, further improves performance to 36.1%, yielding the strongest overall planning performance across all evaluated tasks. Moreover, tactile sensing becomes increasingly important under long-horizon planning objectives, where compounding prediction errors and contact uncertainty accumulate over time. Together, these findings highlight the importance of representation structure, multimodal compatibility, and long-horizon robustness in vision-tactile world models for contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [314] [AnyGoal: Vision-Language Guided Multi-Agent Exploration for Training-Free Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13878)
*MoniJesu James,Marcelino Julio Fernando,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyGoal是一种免训练的多机器人架构，以VLM为核心驱动前沿探索，通过共享的2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM）融合多智能体评估，在GOAT-Bench上达到SOTA（双智能体52.4%子任务成功率），相比Modular GOAT提升27.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端导航策略在分布外场景下性能严重下降；模块化管线受限于闭集目标检测的召回率；3D快照记忆系统维护密集的视点相关表示，开销大。

Method: 提出AnyGoal架构：1) 以VLM为核心的 frontier-based 探索框架；2) 共享2D高斯贝叶斯价值图（BVM），维护每个像素的(μ, σ²)后验，通过深度锥掩码的精度加权融合VLM分数，子任务间不重置实现终身证据累积；3) 前沿排序由VLM软评分与BVM的贝叶斯UCB项的凸组合决定；4) 贪婪分配器带空间分离惩罚与承诺滞后，无需集中控制器即可在智能体间分配前沿。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench验证集未见划分（360 episodes, 2669子任务）上，双智能体系统实现52.4%子任务成功率、12.7% SPL，达到严格物理体制下SOTA，比Modular GOAT（24.9%）提升27.5个百分点；单智能体AnyGoal达41.9%，证明收益来自决策架构；四路消融实验表明开放词汇检测器将主导失败模式从探索转向目标验证。

Conclusion: AnyGoal通过免训练的VLM驱动多智能体协作架构，显著提升了跨场景导航的泛化能力，验证了基于贝叶斯价值图的终身证据累积和智能体协调机制的有效性。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation policies trained on large simulation corpora degrade sharply when transferred to out-of-distribution scenes, categories, or goal modalities. Modular pipelines such as Modular GOAT are bottlenecked by closed-set object detection recall, while 3D snapshot-memory systems (e.g. 3D-Mem) accumulate dense, view-dependent representations that are heavy to maintain. We present AnyGoal, a training-free multi-robot architecture that places a Vision-Language Model (VLM) at the core of frontier-based exploration and coordinates agents through a shared 2D Gaussian Bayesian Value Map (BVM). The BVM maintains a per-pixel (mu, sigma^2) posterior over goal relevance, updated via precision-weighted fusion of VLM scores through a depth-cone mask, and is never reset between subtasks, yielding lifelong evidence accumulation. Frontiers are ranked by a convex blend of a VLM-as-judge softmax and a Bayesian UCB term on the BVM. A greedy allocator with spatial-separation penalty and commitment hysteresis distributes frontiers across agents without a centralized controller. On the full GOAT-Bench val unseen split (360 episodes, 2,669 subtasks), our dual-agent system achieves 52.4% Subtask SR at 12.7% SPL--state of the art under the strict physical regime (discrete 0.25 m steps, no teleportation, 42 deg HFOV) and a +27.5 pp improvement over Modular GOAT (24.9%). Single-agent AnyGoal achieves 41.9% Subtask SR, showing gains arise from the decision architecture. A four-way perception ablation shows that open-vocabulary detectors shift the dominant failure mode from exploration to goal verification.

</details>


### [315] [Guided Diffusion with Distilled Vision-Language Reliability for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13883)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可靠性感知的扩散规划器(RADP)，通过场景级可靠性热图引导无人机轨迹生成，解决端到端生成模型在玻璃、镜面等不可靠区域规划失败的问题，在仿真和实物实验中大幅降低障碍物碰撞率(40.3%→9.6%)并提升区域可靠性(0.588→0.925)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自主无人机导航的感知-建图-规划分离流程存在错误传播、延迟累积和环境重调参问题；端到端生成模型虽消除了模块接口，但在训练数据中未见过的不可靠观测区域(如玻璃、镜面、过曝光表面)上无法识别，将其当作有效证据进行规划，导致安全隐患。

Method: 1) 轻量级网络从视觉-语言模型(VLM)蒸馏开放词汇推理能力，在实时规划预算内生成场景级可靠性热图；2) 扩散规划器以观测和热图为条件生成轨迹；3) 去噪过程中使用可微两阶段ESDF代价函数，将深度物理障碍与高不可靠区域的虚拟障碍统一处理，无需重训即可泛化到未见环境。

Result: 仿真和真实四旋翼实验中：障碍物违反率从40.3%降至9.6%；遍历区域平均可靠性从0.588提升至0.925；消融可靠性项后平均可靠性从0.898降至0.783；蒸馏使框架比完整VLM快2倍。

Conclusion: 可靠性感知的扩散规划器通过显式建模观测可靠性，有效解决了端到端导航模型在不可靠区域的规划失败问题，在安全性上显著优于扩散基线，且蒸馏方案兼顾了实时性要求。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV navigation is conventionally solved by pipelines that separate perception, mapping, and planning into distinct stages, which propagates errors, accumulates latency, and requires environment-specific retuning. End-to-end generative models remove these interfaces by mapping raw observations directly to trajectories, but inherit a subtle failure mode: trained on clean data, they cannot recognise when an observation is unreliable, and treat degraded regions such as glass, mirrors, and overexposed surfaces as valid evidence for planning. We present a reliability-aware diffusion planner for 3D UAV navigation. It conditions trajectory generation on the observation together with a scene-level reliability heatmap that marks where perception cannot be trusted, produced by a lightweight network that distils the open-vocabulary reasoning of a vision-language model within the real-time planning budget. To generalise to unseen environments without retraining, we steer the denoising process with a differentiable two-stage ESDF cost that treats physical obstacles from depth and virtual obstacles from highly unreliable regions on equal footing. In simulation and on a real quadrotor, our planner produces markedly safer trajectories than a state-of-the-art diffusion baseline, reducing the obstacle-violation rate from 40.3% to 9.6% and raising the mean reliability of traversed regions from 0.588 to 0.925. Ablating the reliability term alone drops mean reliability from 0.898 to 0.783, confirming it as the decisive component, while distillation runs the framework up to 2 times faster than the full vision-language model.

</details>


### [316] [PhysVLA: Towards Physically-Grounded VLA for Embodied Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13886)
*Namai Chandra,Shriram Damodaran,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLA 是一个即插即用的推理时框架，通过相位感知有限状态机和选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门对冻结的 VLA 模型进行物理一致性校正，在不重新训练的情况下将绝对成功率提升高达 17%，轨迹效率提升 15%，并成功迁移到物理机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型仅拟合行为演示数据，未显式强制执行刚体动力学或接触约束等物理原理，导致存在关键物理鸿沟：标准时间平滑在提升轨迹质量的同时引入了短期记忆无法解决的额外失败。

Method: PhysVLA 由两部分组成：(i) 相位感知有限状态机，将离散任务阶段（接近、抓取、运输、放置）进行结构化；(ii) 选择性欧拉-拉格朗日门，仅在动力学检测器发现运动学不一致时激活，对预测动作进行物理校正。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Spatial（7-DoF Franka Panda）上，PhysVLA 在 OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、Force-VLA、Generalist-VLA 四个骨干网络上实现绝对成功率提升高达 17%、稳定性提升 19%、轨迹效率提升 15%；在 Robosuite Lift 跨模拟器测试中轨迹抖动鲁棒性提升最高 10 倍；在真实 Agilex Piper 机械臂上成功率提升达 50%。

Conclusion: 物理感知可以作为可组合的、与骨干网络无关的运行时模块，以即插即用方式提升任何冻结 VLA 模型的成功率和稳定性，且无需重新训练或权重访问。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at mapping visual inputs and natural language instructions directly to robotic control policies. However, because they are trained primarily to fit behavioural demonstration data, they do not explicitly enforce fundamental physical principles such as rigid-body dynamics or contact constraints. This exposes a critical physics gap: standard temporal smoothing applied on top of single-step or chunked VLAs trades trajectory quality for added failures that short-term memory cannot resolve. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysVLA (Physics-VLA), a plug-and-play, inference-time framework designed to wrap any frozen VLA backbone without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, with less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. PhysVLA intercepts the predicted control action, captures only the simulator or system state, and applies a dual-layered correction: (i) a phase-aware finite-state machine that structures discrete task segments (approach, grasp, transport, and place), and (ii) a selective Euler-Lagrange gate that activates only when a dynamics oracle detects kinodynamic inconsistency. Evaluated across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on LIBERO-Spatial with a 7-DoF Franka Panda, the framework delivers absolute success rate increases of up to 17% and stability increases of up to 19% with no per-task regressions, improves trajectory efficiency by up to 15% across all four backbones, and shows up to a 10x improvement in trajectory jerk robustness on a Robosuite Lift cross-simulator sweep. We further validate the framework on a real Agilex Piper arm with a pick-and-place task, confirming that PhysVLA transfers to physical hardware without retraining, with success-rate improvements of up to 50%, establishing physical awareness as a composable, backbone-agnostic runtime module.

</details>


### [317] [Learning Dynamic Swing-Up of an Inverted Pendulum using Remote Magnetic Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13915)
*Viacheslav Sydora,Jasan Zughaibi,Denis von Arx,Quentin Boehler,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次在临床级Navion电磁导航系统上实现了磁驱动倒立摆的摆起控制，通过结合轨迹优化、时变LQR状态反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC），在六次迭代内成功完成摆起。实验表明ILC校正量接近高保真磁场模型校准预测的扭矩差异，证明了学习与自适应方法在处理电磁驱动不确定性中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电磁导航系统多采用准静态控制，动态方法虽已展现出优势，但远离平衡状态的轨迹跟踪问题尚未得到解决。本文旨在填补这一空白，验证动态控制策略在临床级eMNS上的可行性。

Method: 采用组合控制策略：1）考虑eMNS内部动力学的轨迹优化生成参考轨迹；2）时变LQR状态反馈提供闭环稳定；3）迭代学习控制（ILC）利用历史试验数据和系统动态模型逐步前馈指令修正。实验在Navion eMNS平台上进行磁驱动倒立摆摆起任务。

Result: 单纯LQR控制无法完成摆起，而加入ILC后在六次迭代内成功实现倒立摆摆起。ILC学习到的校正量与高保真磁场模型校准预测的扭矩偏差高度吻合。

Conclusion: 学习与自适应方法是应对电磁驱动中不确定性（如患者特异性生理运动模式、磁场模型校准误差）的有效工具，所提方法可推广至导管、导丝等其他磁驱动器械的控制。

Abstract: Electromagnetic Navigation Systems (eMNS) have gained considerable attention for minimally invasive surgery and targeted drug delivery. While most of the literature relies on quasi-static control of these systems, recent work has demonstrated the benefits of dynamic approaches. However, trajectory tracking far from equilibrium states remains largely unaddressed. We close this gap by demonstrating the first swing-up of a magnetically actuated inverted pendulum using the clinically-ready Navion eMNS. Although the inverted pendulum is not clinically relevant in itself, the proposed method utilizes torques and forces as control objectives, making it applicable to other magnetically actuated devices such as catheters and guidewires. Our approach combines trajectory optimization that accounts for internal eMNS dynamics with time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) state feedback and Iterative Learning Control (ILC), which leverages previous trial data and the system's dynamic model to progressively refine the feedforward command. While LQR alone fails due to the complex phenomena of magnetic actuation, ILC enables successful swing-up within six iterations. Furthermore, post-experimental analysis reveals that the learned ILC correction closely matches the torque discrepancy predicted by high-fidelity magnetic field model calibration, suggesting learning and adaptation as a promising tool to deal with uncertainties in electromagnetic actuation arising, e.g., from patient-specific physiological motion patterns and field model calibration inaccuracies.

</details>


### [318] [An Attention-based Model for Robust Forecasting with Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13970)
*Zhitian Zhang,Wenjie Zi,Yunduz Rakhmangulova,Saghar Irandoust,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CVAE和Transformer的多模态模型，能够在训练和推理阶段处理缺失模态数据，在人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人系统常面临传感器数据不完整的问题，而现有大多数多模态模型假设训练和推理时所有模态均可用，限制了其在机器人感知和决策中的适用性。

Method: 将模型构建为条件变分自编码器（CVAE），采用基于Transformer的架构，利用注意力机制学习统一的固定维度表示，即使部分模态缺失也能正常工作。

Result: 在五个人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测数据集上进行了评估，实验表明该模型能从缺失数据中有效学习，且优于先前多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 该模型能够在缺失模态下进行训练和推理，学习到所有模态的鲁棒表示，为机器人多模态学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning with missing modalities is a fundamental challenge in multimodal robot learning, as real-world robotic systems often operate in environments with incomplete sensor data. Attention-based models are appealing for processing multimodal data because they can handle multiple modalities with a single backbone network. However, most multimodal models assume that all modalities are available during both training and inference, limiting their applicability in robotic perception and decision-making. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal model designed to handle missing modalities during both training and inference. The model is formulated as a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and incorporates a transformer-based architecture that leverages attention mechanisms to learn a unified, fixed-dimensional representation, even when some modalities are missing. We show that our proposed model can be trained with missing modalities while approximating a robust representation of all modalities. We evaluate our approach on five multimodal datasets across two robot learning tasks: human trajectory prediction and robot manipulation forecasting. Experimental results demonstrate that our model effectively learns from incomplete data and is superior to prior multimodal fusion approaches.

</details>


### [319] [SplatlessDF: Continuous Distance Field Mapping with Non-Splatting Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13990)
*Monisha Mushtary Uttsha,Lan Wu,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SplatlessDF 提出基于高斯元（从空间而非光度视角）的连续距离场映射框架，支持距离与梯度查询，并可联合 2DGS 实现距离场与渲染统一建模，适用于机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯溅射方法主要面向光度重建与渲染，缺乏专为机器人导航任务设计的连续距离场表示，且缺乏基于高斯原语的统一距离场与表面建模框架。

Method: 从空间视角直接参数化各向异性高斯元素，优化得到可微距离场；支持独立距离场建模（standalone）及与2D高斯溅射联合建模（joint DF-rendering）两种设置。

Result: 独立公式提供高效准确的距离与梯度查询；联合公式在改进渲染几何的同时连续建模距离场，验证了GS风格表示在导航映射中的潜力。

Conclusion: 高斯溅射表示不仅可以用于表面建模与渲染，也适用于面向机器人导航的映射表示。

Abstract: Recent Gaussian splatting (GS) methods have shown that scenes can be represented efficiently with optimisable Gaussians for high-quality reconstruction and rendering. In this paper, building on this principle, we introduce SplatlessDF, a continuous distance field (DF) mapping framework that uses anisotropic Gaussian elements from a spatial rather than photometric perspective. SplatlessDF directly parameterises the Gaussians and optimises to recover a differentiable DF, enabling distances and gradients to be queried in the spatial domain for downstream robotic tasks such as navigation. Furthermore, SplatlessDF can be coupled with 2D Gaussian splatting (2DGS), providing a unified framework based solely on Gaussian primitives that can learn continuous DF and surface models and supports photometric rendering. We consider two settings: a standalone DF-only formulation and a joint DF-rendering formulation coupled with 2DGS. Experiments show that the standalone formulation provides efficient and accurate distance and gradient queries, while the joint formulation improves rendering geometry and simultaneously models a continuous DF. These results highlight the potential of GS-style representations not only for surface modelling and rendering but also for mapping representations suited to robotic navigation.

</details>


### [320] [From Attacks to Curricula: Learnability-Guided Adversarial Training for Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14032)
*Yuewen Mei,Tong Nie,Jie Sun,Haotian Shi,Wei Ma,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AlignADV，一种可学习性引导的闭环对抗训练框架，通过偏好对齐将对抗场景转化为可解决且与能力匹配的课程，结合行为指纹与多模态能力预测模型实现动态课程采样，在Waymo数据集上训练步骤减少40.6%，同时降低碰撞率并提升路线完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练框架存在两个问题：1）碰撞驱动的生成器常合成不可解的极端场景，反而降低学习效果；2）启发式采样器忽略驾驶策略的演进能力，导致采样效率低、收敛慢。需要将对抗场景从攻击导向转向可学习性导向。

Method: 1）将对抗场景生成建模为偏好对齐问题，使用直接偏好优化(DPO)引导生成器生成关键但可解决的场景；2）引入行为指纹捕获演进策略的内在特征，构建多模态能力预测模型，无需昂贵闭环仿真即可估计策略性能；3）结合可解决性对齐场景与能力预测，设计动态课程采样机制，优先采样针对当前策略弱点的场景。

Result: 在Waymo开放运动数据集上，AlignADV相比基线方法训练步骤减少40.6%，在正常和对抗交通条件下均降低了碰撞率并提升了路线完成度。

Conclusion: 该工作标志着从攻击导向的场景生成向可学习性引导的策略改进的转变，为更安全高效的自动驾驶训练提供了原则性方向。

Abstract: Closed-loop adversarial training improves autonomous driving safety by exposing policies to rare safety-critical scenarios. Standard pipelines first generate adversarial scenarios and then sample them for policy optimization. However, most existing frameworks remain attack-oriented: collision-driven generators often synthesize unsolvable extreme situations, which can degrade learning, while heuristic samplers ignore the evolving capability of the driving policy, causing sample inefficiency and delayed convergence. We propose AlignADV, a learnability-guided closed-loop adversarial training framework that converts adversarial scenarios into resolvable and capability-aligned curricula. First, we reformulate adversarial scenario generation as a preference alignment problem and employ direct preference optimization to guide the generator toward critical yet resolvable scenarios. Second, we introduce behavioral fingerprints to capture the intrinsic characteristics of the evolving policy and construct a multi-modal capability prediction model that estimates policy performance without expensive closed-loop simulations. By combining resolvability-aligned scenarios with capability predictions, AlignADV develops a dynamic curriculum sampling mechanism that prioritizes scenarios targeting the current policy's vulnerabilities. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that AlignADV improves convergence efficiency and final performance, reducing training steps by up to 40.6 percent compared with baseline methods while lowering collision rate and improving route completion under both normal and adversarial traffic conditions. These results highlight a shift from attack-oriented scenario generation to learnability-guided policy improvement, offering a principled direction for safer and more efficient autonomous driving training. Project page: https://meiyuewen.github.io/AlignADV/.

</details>


### [321] [ReactSim-Bench: Benchmarking Reactive Behavior World Model Simulation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14058)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yanlun Peng,Jianing Zhang,Xianda Guo,Zehan Huang,Haoran Liu,Qifeng Li,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ReactSim-Bench基准，用于评估自动驾驶系统中行为世界模型模拟器的反应能力。通过解耦智能体与AV控制，使用不同于日志的AV行为测试智能体响应，并采用碰撞、地图和运动学可行性指标评估安全性及规则遵从性。实验涵盖2636个场景及多种模型架构，分析了重规划频率对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶行为仿真基准无法直接测量模拟器的反应能力（即智能体对偏离日志的AV行为的可行响应），往往通过联合控制AV与周围智能体来评估真实性，缺乏对反应能力的独立评测。

Method: 解耦智能体与AV的控制，构建pipeline生成偏离日志的AV行为（通过AV规划器模型生成候选行为，经规则和人工验证筛选）；采用碰撞、地图约束和运动学可行性三类指标评估反应响应的安全性和规则遵从性；构建2636个涵盖三类场景的测试集。

Result: 对基于Transformer、扩散模型和下一token预测等多种架构的SOTA模型进行了系统评估，并分析了重规划频率对性能的影响。

Conclusion: ReactSim-Bench填补了行为仿真反应能力评估的空白，提供了标准化测试框架和洞察，为未来研究指引方向。

Abstract: Reactive capability is a key property of data-driven behavior world model simulators for autonomous driving simulation systems. With this capability, simulated world agents can respond feasibly to autonomous vehicle (AV) behaviors that differ from the log. However, existing behavior simulation benchmarks do not directly measure reactive capability. They often let the simulator jointly control the AV and surrounding agents and evaluate realism through log similarity or open-loop prediction metrics. In this work, we introduce ReactSim-Bench for evaluating the reactive capability of behavior world model simulation in autonomous driving. We decouple the control of agents and the AV, using AV behaviors that differ from the log and require agents to respond as independent AV inputs. To obtain these AV behaviors, we construct a pipeline that uses an AV planner model to generate candidate behaviors and filters the data using rules and manual verification. Collision metrics, map-based metrics, and kinematic feasibility metrics are used to evaluate the safety and rule compliance of reactive responses. We construct 2,636 test scenarios with three categories and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art models across multiple architectures, including Transformer-based, diffusion-based, and next-token-prediction-based models. We further analyze how replan frequency affects performance and provide insights for future studies.

</details>


### [322] [Semidefinite Relaxations for Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14063)
*Bernhard Paus Graesdal,Alexandre Amice,Pablo A. Parrilo,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了碰撞避免运动规划的半定松弛方法，证明了凸松弛等价于在高维空间中求解全局最优的运动规划问题，并提出了对称性约简技术使半定松弛的规模与多项式次数线性相关、与环境维度无关。实验表明该方法比直接非线性规划方法快10-100倍，并可作为RRT规划器中的凸引导函数用于四旋翼最小加加速度规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞避免运动规划是一个经典难题，尽管半定松弛在机器人运动规划中的应用已有探索，但缺乏对其理论性质的深入分析。作者希望填补这一空白，为半定松弛在该问题上的有效性和局限性提供理论解释。

Method: 将问题精确建模为多项式曲线上的非凸优化问题，提出自然的半定松弛。通过几何解释证明凸松弛等价于在高维空间中求解全局最优的规划问题，从而给出松弛紧致的充要条件。同时利用对称性约简技术降低半定锥的规模。

Result: 半定松弛解法比SNOPT和IPOPT等直接非线性规划方法快10-100倍，求解时间方差显著更低，能可靠地找到原问题的局部最优路径。在C^4连续的四旋翼最小加加速度规划中作为RRT规划器的凸引导函数表现有效。

Conclusion: 本文首次为碰撞避免运动规划的半定松弛提供了理论分析，揭示了其几何意义和紧致条件，并通过对称性约简大幅提升了计算效率，展示了在实际机器人规划中的实用价值。

Abstract: We study semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. We focus on a point robot moving from start to goal through spherical obstacles in $\mathbb{R}^n$, subject to path continuity constraints and squared derivative costs; a setting that is conceptually simple yet captures the hardness of collision-free motion planning. We formulate this problem exactly as a nonconvex problem over polynomial curves, and present a natural semidefinite relaxation. We contribute two key theoretical insights; to our knowledge this is the first theoretical analysis of semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. First, we show that solving the convex relaxation is equivalent to solving, to global optimality, a related motion planning problem in a potentially higher-dimensional space. This geometric interpretation yields necessary and sufficient conditions for tightness, and a clear intuition for when the relaxation is loose. Second, we show that the relaxation admits a symmetry reduction that makes it significantly smaller than one might expect, with positive semidefinite cone sizes that scale linearly with the polynomial degree and are independent of the ambient dimension. The resulting relaxation is 10 to 100 times faster than direct nonlinear programming transcriptions solved with SNOPT and IPOPT, exhibits significantly lower variance in solve times, and reliably finds a locally optimal path for the original problem. We demonstrate its effectiveness as a convex steering function in an RRT planner for minimum-snap quadrotor planning with $C^4$ continuous trajectories.

</details>


### [323] [Development of a 3 in Sewer Pipe Inspection Robot with an Articulated Differential Mechanism using X-shaped Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14070)
*Shoya Umemura,Ryota Taniguchi,Atsushi Kakogawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进版3段式下水道管道检测机器人，通过连接推进单元提升牵引力和越障能力，并采用差动机构和基于电机电流检测的松弛线缆控制方法，实验验证了其越障性能的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一版机器人在管道检测中存在牵引力不足和越障能力差的问题，亟需改进以提升实用性能。

Method: 通过简单连接推进单元形成耦合结构，引入差动机构实现单线控制姿态变化以适应管径变化；并设计了基于驱动轮电机电流负载检测障碍物接触的控制方法，通过松弛线缆实现越障。

Result: 通过模拟管道实验，利用驱动轮电流波形进行负载对比，验证了所提控制方法显著改善了铰接式机器人的越障能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的改进结构和控制方法有效提升了管道检测机器人的牵引力和越障性能，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: This paper proposes, an improved version of the 3 in sewer pipe inspection robot equipped with an emergency evacuation mechanism. The low traction force and poor stepover capability, which were challenges of the first version, have been improved by simply connecting the propulsion units. The coupled propulsion units feature a differential mechanism capable of posture changes via a single wire, enabling adaptation to pipe diameter variations. To traverse obstacles like pipe joints, a control method was devised that detects obstacle contact through current load on the drive wheel motors and slackens the wire. This method was verified through simulated pipe experiments. Load comparisons were made using current waveforms applied to the drive wheels. Our proposed control method significantly improved the step-over capability of the new articulated robots.

</details>


### [324] [The N2D Haptic Glove: A Multi-Finger Glove for 2D Directional Force Feedback for Contact Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14083)
*Yao-Ting Huang,Jake Honma,Omar Hernandez,Logan Li,Kaitlin Calimbahin,Bryce Hackel,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出N2D Haptic Glove，一种多指可穿戴触觉手套，通过绞盘驱动传动实现平面屈伸指尖力反馈。实验证明，相比纯视觉和单轴力反馈，平面指尖力反馈显著降低接触力误差、提升操作一致性及用户体验。硬件与软件将完全开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉手套大多仅提供振动或单轴力反馈，缺失力的方向信息，导致用户在遥操作中依赖视觉推断接触力，容易产生过度按压、控制不一致和精度下降的问题。

Method: 设计了一种多指可穿戴设备N2D Haptic Glove，采用绞盘驱动传动机构，实现高透明度平面屈伸指尖力反馈。

Result: 通过台架验证和遥操作用户实验，与纯视觉及单轴力反馈基线相比，平面指尖力反馈显著降低了精确操作中的接触力误差，提升了试验间一致性，并在轴向探测任务中改善了用户体验。

Conclusion: N2D Haptic Glove及方向性指尖触觉设备是接触丰富型遥操作、沉浸式虚拟现实仿真和机器人模仿学习中有前景的交互模态。

Abstract: Humans rely on directional fingertip forces to probe and regulate contact during manipulation, yet most wearable haptic gloves render only vibration or single-axis force, leaving force direction ambiguous. Without directional cues, users must infer contact force from vision alone, often leading to over-pressing, inconsistent control, and reduced precision in robotic teleoperation. We present the N2D Haptic Glove, a multi-finger wearable device that renders planar flexion-extension fingertip forces using capstan-drive transmissions for high-transparency force feedback. Through benchtop validations and a user study involving haptic teleoperation of a robotic arm and hand, we demonstrate that compared to visual-only and single-axis haptic baselines, planar fingertip feedback significantly reduces contact force error during precise manipulation, improves trial-to-trial consistency, and enhances overall user experience in axial probing tasks. These findings establish the N2D Haptic Glove and directional finger-based haptics devices as a promising modality for contact-rich teleoperation, immersive virtual reality simulations, and robot learning from demonstrations. N2D Haptic Glove's hardware and software system will be fully open-sourced at \href{https://ucsdarclab.github.io/n2d-glove/}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [325] [Self-Improving VLA Policies: Selected Diffusion Noise for Spurious-Robust Action Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14084)
*Duc Minh Nguyen,Bao-Ngoc Dao,Tung M. Luu,Binh Gia Nguyen,Vinh Tong,Anji Liu,Vu N. Duong,Dung D. Le,Daniel Sonntag,Trung Le,Ngan Le,Jan Peter,An Thai Le,Minh Nhat Vu,Mathias Niepert,Khoa D. Doan,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SDN方法，通过测试时在扩散噪声空间中选取最大化与参考集分离且能产生更一致动作轨迹的噪声向量，无需重训练即可提升VLA策略的鲁棒性和成功率，在模拟和真实实验中分别提升+8%和+10%的成功率，并减少动作抖动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的VLA策略在机器人操作中表现出强泛化能力，但对虚假视觉关联和噪声动作生成敏感，导致在扰动下行为脆弱。现有方法缺乏简单有效的测试时增强手段。

Method: 提出Selected Diffusion Noise (SDN)，一种无训练、仅在测试时使用的噪声选择方法。通过从扩散噪声空间中动态采样与参考集最大分离的噪声向量来减少对虚假线索的依赖，同时选择能产生更连贯动作轨迹的候选噪声，实现双目标优化。

Result: 在Google Robot和Widow-X两个模拟基准以及两个真实机器人数据集上，针对pi_0、Groot-N1.5、Groot-N1.6多种VLA策略进行评估。SDN在模拟环境中成功率提升+8%，真实环境中提升+10%，且动作更平滑更稳定。

Conclusion: 扩散噪声选择可以作为测试时增强VLA策略的有效且通用机制，在不修改模型参数的情况下显著提升鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies enable strong generalization in robotic manipulation, but remain sensitive to spurious visual correlations and noisy action generation, leading to brittle behavior under perturbations.
