Results show our natural language approach significantly reduces user workload compared to traditional baselines. Additionally, occupational therapists confirmed the generated policies are safe and accurately reflect user preferences.

</details>


### [363] [Micro-Swarm Locomotion Optimization in Dynamic Flow using Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25025)
*Josef Berman,Oren Gal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合CFD-MO-MARL框架，将高保真不可压缩Navier-Stokes求解器与去中心化近端策略优化相结合，在振荡流中学习微机器人集群控制策略。使用PCGrad解决冲突目标，训练后策略在推进、能效和平滑度上均优于基线，并涌现出三种未编码的集群行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实时变流体环境中协调微型机器人集群是生物医学和环境应用的主要挑战，现有方法难以处理流体-智能体复杂交互和多目标冲突。

Method: 混合CFD-MO-MARL框架：耦合高保真不可压缩Navier-Stokes求解器与去中心化PPO，采用PCGrad处理冲突梯度，在脉动动脉波形2mm通道中训练16个磁驱动微机器人。

Result: 推进奖励6.5-7.0，能效0.63-0.65，平滑度0.97-0.99，主目标优于暴力基线8个奖励单位。未用PCGrad时能量和平滑度奖励崩溃。涌现三种行为：流体节流编队、周期同步棘轮机制、个体化最终接近策略。

Conclusion: 高保真流体-智能体交互可直接集成到多目标强化学习中，为生物医学导航、环境监测和微流控系统的微集群控制提供可扩展框架。

Abstract: Coordinating micro-robotic swarms in realistic, time-dependent fluid environments remains a major challenge for biomedical and environmental applications. We present a hybrid CFD-MO-MARL (Computational Fluid Dynamics-Multi Objective-Multi Agent Reinforcement Learning) framework that couples a high-fidelity incompressible Navier--Stokes solver with decentralized proximal policy optimization to learn swarm control policies in oscillatory flow. Sixteen magnetically actuated micro-robots were simulated to navigate a pulsatile arterial waveform within a 2 mm channel while jointly optimizing upstream progression, energy efficiency, and motion smoothness. Conflicting objectives are resolved using Projected Conflicting Gradient (PCGrad) surgery. Without PCGrad, energy and smoothness rewards collapse during training, demonstrating that gradient conflict resolution is essential for stable multi-objective learning. The converged policy achieves progress rewards of 6.5-7.0, energy efficiency of 0.63-0.65, and smoothness of 0.97-0.99, outperforming brute-force baselines by more than 8 reward units on the primary objective. Training reveals three emergent behaviors not encoded in the reward function: hydrodynamic throttling formations that reduce peak flow velocities, a cycle-synchronized ratchet mechanism that exploits flow reversals for upstream movement, and individualized final-approach strategies near the target boundary. These results demonstrate that physically realistic fluid--agent interactions can be integrated directly into multi-objective reinforcement learning, providing a scalable framework for micro-swarm control in biomedical navigation, environmental monitoring, and microfluidic systems.

</details>


### [364] [ParkourFormer: Integrating Predictive Supervision and Sequence Modeling into Parkour Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25782)
*Yanheng Mai,Wenhao Xu,Zirui Huang,Yifei Fu,Shengwei Dong,Xinjue Wang,Kailun Huang,Yanzhe Xie,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ParkourFormer 是一种基于Transformer的序列建模框架，通过显式建模未来本体感受状态来提升人形机器人在多变地形上的敏捷运动能力，在多样化地形上实现了93.85%的平均穿越成功率，相比基线方法提升高达47.12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习策略大多是反应式的，直接将观测映射到动作，未能显式建模未来身体状态，而在敏捷 locomotion 任务中，成功执行高度依赖于对即将到来的接触过渡和身体动力学的预判。

Method: 将人形机器人运动重新建模为未来条件决策问题；当前机器人状态通过交叉注意力查询历史传感器运动轨迹；轻量级预测头预测短时域未来本体感受状态（带监督信号训练）；将预测的未来状态与时序特征融合生成动作。

Result: 在包含楼梯、间隙、斜坡、粗糙地形和障碍穿越的多地形基准上，ParkourFormer 实现了93.85%的平均穿越成功率，相比 MLP、MoE-MLP 和原始 Transformer 基线提升高达47.12%，且使用单一统一策略。

Conclusion: 显式的未来状态建模显著提升了敏捷全身运动的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Humanoid parkour requires locomotion policies to coordinate whole-body dynamics across rapidly changing terrains such as stairs, gaps, slopes, and obstacles. Existing reinforcement learning policies are largely reactive, mapping observations directly to actions without explicitly modeling future body states. Such modeling becomes critical in agile locomotion tasks where successful motion execution depends strongly on anticipating upcoming contact transitions and body dynamics. We present ParkourFormer, a Transformer-based sequence modeling framework that reformulates humanoid locomotion as a future-conditioned decision-making problem. The current robot state queries historical sensorimotor trajectories through cross-attention, while a lightweight prediction head forecasts short-horizon future proprioceptive states. The predicted future states, trained with supervised signals, are fused with temporal features to generate actions, enabling the policy to jointly reason over motion history and anticipated future dynamics. We evaluate ParkourFormer on a diverse multi-terrain humanoid parkour benchmark including stairs, gaps, slopes, rough terrain, and obstacle traversal. Experiments in simulation and on a real humanoid robot show that ParkourFormer achieves a 93.85% average traversal success rate on highly challenging terrains, with improvements of up to 47.12% over strong MLP, MoE-based MLP, and vanilla Transformer baselines, while maintaining a single unified policy across all terrain types. These results demonstrate that explicit future-state modeling significantly improves robustness and generalization for agile whole-body locomotion.

</details>


### [365] [CoRe-MoE: Contrastive Reweighted Mixture of Experts for Multi-Terrain Humanoid Locomotion with Gait Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04718)
*Kailun Huang,Zikang Xie,Yanzhe Xie,Panpan Liao,Fanghai Zhang,Yanheng Mai,Wenhao Xu,Yunheng Wang,Renjing Xu,Haohui Huang,Chenguang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoRe-MoE框架，通过两阶段强化学习将步态生成与地形适应解耦，结合对比学习驱动的MoE架构实现双足机器人行走与奔跑的平滑过渡及多地形自适应，在仿真和Unitree G1实物机器人上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类可自然地在行走与奔跑间切换并适应复杂地形，但人形机器人难以在一个策略中同时实现步态过渡和多地形适应，主要挑战在于任务间的梯度干扰以及地形变化导致的分布偏移。

Method: 提出CoRe-MoE两阶段强化学习框架：第一阶段学习稳定 locomotion 策略以生成自然的行走/奔跑行为及平滑过渡；第二阶段引入地形感知的MoE分支，通过对比学习目标训练门控网络以学习结构化地形表征并促进专家专业化，最终通过加权融合基础步态策略与地形感知分支输出动作。

Result: 仿真实验表明所提方法在成功率、运动稳定性和多地形适应性上优于基线方法；零样本部署到Unitree G1人形机器人上，实现了在楼梯、斜坡、台阶、障碍物及非结构化户外地形上的稳健行走与奔跑，并保持精确的落脚点控制和动态稳定性。

Conclusion: CoRe-MoE通过解耦步态生成与地形适应，有效缓解了多任务间的梯度干扰，结合对比学习驱动的MoE实现了清晰专家分工，为人形机器人在复杂环境中的自然、稳定运动提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Humans primarily rely on walking and running to traverse complex terrains. Similarly, humanoid robots should be able to smoothly transition between walking and running while maintaining natural and stable locomotion. However, unifying gait transition and multi-terrain adaptation within a single policy remains challenging due to gradient interference between tasks and the distribution shift caused by terrain variations. Although Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures can mitigate multi-skill interference, direct joint training often fails to achieve clear expert specialization. To address these challenges, we propose CoRe-MoE, a two-stage reinforcement learning framework that decouples gait generation from terrain adaptation. In the first stage, a stable locomotion policy is learned to produce natural walking and running behaviors with smooth transitions. In the second stage, a terrain-aware MoE branch is introduced, and the gating network is trained with a contrastive objective to learn structured terrain representations and promote expert specialization. The final action is obtained through weighted fusion of the base gait policy and the terrain-aware branch, enabling the policy to preserve stable locomotion while adapting to complex terrains. Extensive simulation results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms baseline approaches in terms of success rate, locomotion stability, and multi-terrain adaptability. Furthermore, zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot validates the effectiveness of our framework, achieving robust walking and running across stairs, slopes, steps, obstacles, and unstructured outdoor terrains while maintaining accurate foothold control and dynamic stability.

</details>


### [366] [FAWAM: Force-Aware World Action Models for Closed-Loop Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08555)
*Haotian He,Zeyu Yan,Qipeng Liu,Ning Guo,Wenzhao Lian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAWAM，一个在感知、预测和闭环执行三个层面融合力信息的力感知世界动作模型，在多种接触丰富操作任务中相比视觉基线提升36.25%成功率，相比现有力感知基线提升21.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法大多仅将力信号作为额外观测模态，未能充分利用其在建模未来交互动力学或引导执行时反馈修正中的作用。

Method: FAWAM在三个层面融合力信息：1) 编码历史6轴力/力矩信号以调制动作生成；2) 联合预测未来动作和末端执行器力旋量，显式建模接触演化；3) 引入残差修正模块，以预测的力旋量轨迹作为执行时参考，基于实时力反馈在线优化动作。

Result: 在多种接触丰富操作任务中，FAWAM平均成功率比纯视觉基线提升36.25%，比现有力感知基线提升21.25%。

Conclusion: 所提出的力感知框架能有效提升鲁棒的接触丰富操作性能。

Abstract: Force signals provide critical interaction cues for contact-rich robotic manipulation. However, existing methods mostly use force as an additional observation modality, without fully exploiting its role in modeling future interaction dynamics or guiding execution-time feedback correction. In this paper, we propose FAWAM, a force-aware world action model that incorporates force information at three levels: perception, prediction, and closed-loop execution. FAWAM first encodes historical 6-axis force/torque signals to modulate action generation, then jointly predicts future actions and end-effector wrenches to explicitly model contact evolution. It further introduces a residual correction module that uses the predicted wrench trajectory as an execution-time reference to refine actions online based on real-time force feedback. Real-world experiments across multiple contact-rich tasks show that FAWAM improves the average success rate by 36.25% over vision-only baselines and 21.25% over existing force-aware baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of our force-aware framework for robust contact-rich manipulation.

</details>


### [367] [Benchmarking Vision-Language-Action Models on SO-101: Failure and Recovery Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08881)
*Yi Yu,Xinchuan Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于低成本SO-101机器人平台的标准真实世界基准，用于评估VLA和模仿学习策略。通过四个代表性操作任务和统一评估协议，研究发现预训练VLA策略整体优于模仿学习基线，但性能高度依赖具体任务，执行不稳定性是主要失败来源，不同架构的恢复能力差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的评估主要在高保真仿真或昂贵的机器人平台上进行，其在低成本真实机器人上的鲁棒性缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 在低成本SO-101平台上构建包含四个代表性操作任务的标准基准，采用统一评估协议，通过真实遥操作演示微调π₀.₅、SmolVLA、Wall-X和ACT等模型，并引入结构化失败分类、语义/执行级失败分解以及恢复感知评估指标。

Result: 更强预训练的VLA策略整体优于模仿学习基线，但性能高度任务依赖；执行不稳定性是主要失败来源；不同架构的恢复能力差异显著。

Conclusion: 真实低成本机器人部署条件下，超越二元任务成功率的失败与恢复分析至关重要，SO-101可作为评估具身AI系统的实用基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong generalization in robotic manipulation, yet existing evaluations are primarily conducted in simulation or on expensive robotic platforms, leaving their robustness on affordable real-world robots largely unexplored. We present a standardized real-world benchmark for evaluating representative VLA and imitation learning policies on the low-cost SO-101 robotic platform. The benchmark comprises four representative manipulation tasks together with unified evaluation protocols, enabling systematic comparison under embodiment uncertainty. Using real-world teleoperated demonstrations, we fine-tune and evaluate $π_{0.5}$, SmolVLA, Wall-X, and ACT directly on the physical platform. Beyond conventional task success rates, the benchmark incorporates a structured failure taxonomy, semantic- and execution-level failure decomposition, and recovery-aware evaluation metrics to characterize policy robustness. Experimental results show that stronger pretrained VLA policies generally outperform the imitation learning baseline, although performance remains highly task-dependent under low-cost robotic deployment conditions. Execution instability emerges as the dominant failure source, while recovery capability varies substantially across architectures. These results highlight the importance of failure and recovery analysis beyond binary task success and establish SO-101 as a practical benchmark for evaluating embodied AI systems under realistic low-cost robotic deployment conditions.

</details>


### [368] [Traceable Virtual Sea Trials in the Marine Robotics Unity Simulator for Manoeuvring Assessment of Unmanned Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12349)
*Paria Rezayan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在MARUS开源模拟器中扩展了标准化虚拟海试框架，支持USV的TC和ZZ机动自动执行、数据采集与处理，生成适用于系统辨识的高保真数据集，并通过案例验证了IMO合规性与可重复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理海试获取高保真操纵数据受成本、安全与环境干扰限制，亟需一种可重复、可审计的虚拟海试框架以支持USV水动力导数辨识与数字孪生研究。

Method: 基于MARUS模拟器扩展标准化虚拟海试框架，实现TC/ZZ机动自动执行与数据生成；建立指令-执行分离日志（差动推力转向），开发SI导向的数据调理与IMO/ITTC指标自动提取管线。

Result: TC测试中，左右舵旋回进距偏差约3.9%，战术直径偏差4.6-4.7%；ZZ测试中，±10°与±20°机动首摇角超调量均小于1°（满足IMO标准），峰值偏航角速率约4.1-5.8°/s。

Conclusion: 该框架提升了基于模拟器的机动试验可重复性与可审计性，为水动力导数辨识与数字孪生工作流提供了可直接使用的SI就绪数据集。

Abstract: Accurate identification of hydrodynamic derivatives is essential for precise control and autonomous navigation of Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs). However, acquiring high-fidelity manoeuvring data from physical sea trials is often constrained by cost, safety, and environmental disturbances. Standard manoeuvring trials, particularly Turning Circle (TC) and Zig-Zag (ZZ), remain fundamental to IMO and ITTC assessment procedures because they provide comparable performance metrics reflective of underlying hydrodynamic behaviour. This paper extends the open-source Marine Robotics Unity Simulator (MARUS) by introducing a standardised Virtual Sea Trial framework for automated execution and data generation of TC/ZZ manoeuvres. The framework provides traceable command-actuation logging, system-identification (SI)-focused data conditioning, and automated extraction of IMO/ITTC-aligned manoeuvring metrics. A key contribution is a dedicated TC/ZZ data acquisition and post-processing pipeline, improving the repeatability and auditability of simulator-based manoeuvres while producing SI-ready datasets for hydrodynamic-derivative identification and digital-twin workflows. The framework also provides explicit command-execution separation for differential-thrust steering, where manoeuvre inputs are recorded as ordered rudder-equivalent commands and realised actuation is logged as an execution-level proxy derived from applied thrust. Case study results demonstrate repeatable and IMO-compliant manoeuvre behaviour. For TC tests, the normalised advance differs by approximately 3.9% between port and starboard turns, while the tactical diameter differs by 4.6-4.7%. For ZZ tests, first and second overshoot excesses remain below 1 degree for both +/-10-degree and +/-20-degree manoeuvres, satisfying IMO criteria, while peak yaw rates range from approximately 4.1 to 5.8 degrees/second.

</details>


### [369] [EquiDexFlow: Contact-Grounded SE(3)-Equivariant Dexterous Grasp Generative Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12728)
*Clinton Enwerem,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EquiDexFlow是一个SE(3)-等变流匹配模型，能够联合预测抓取姿态、关节角度、 fingertip接触点、表面法线和接触力，保证接触放置和摩擦合规性，在仿真和真实机器人上均实现了优异的抓取性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的灵巧手抓取生成器通常将接触力作为后处理验证步骤，导致运动学可行的抓取姿态仍可能违反稳定物理抓取的条件，缺乏对力与接触一致性的端到端保证。

Method: 提出EquiDexFlow，一个SE(3)-等变流匹配模型。架构将接触点投影到物体表面，将力约束在Coulomb摩擦锥内，无需损失惩罚项。通过构造保证接触放置和摩擦合规性，并证明端到端SE(3)等变性。

Result: 在8,100个力闭合抓取数据上训练（81物体，16-DoF Allegro Hand），实现零摩擦违反、最佳综合分数和最低wrench残差。在200次旋转测试中，手腕残差低于0.04°，关节偏差为零。真实机器人上重定位到LEAP Hand后，所有六个测试物体的开环抓取均成功，非对称物体在规范姿态和120°共旋转下均成功。

Conclusion: 通过将接触力和几何约束联合建模并保证等变性，EquiDexFlow有效弥合了运动学规划与物理稳定抓取之间的鸿沟，在仿真和真实机器人上均验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Most learned dexterous grasp generators relegate contact forces to a downstream verification step, so a kinematically-plausible pose can still violate the conditions for a stable physical grasp. We address this with EquiDexFlow, an SE(3)-equivariant flow-matching model that jointly predicts wrist pose, joint angles, fingertip contacts, surface normals, and contact forces from an object point cloud. Our architecture projects contacts onto the object surface and forces into the Coulomb friction cone by construction, so placement and friction compliance hold without loss penalties. We prove end-to-end SE(3) equivariance and verify it empirically over 200 rotations, with wrist residuals below $0.04^\circ$ and exactly zero joint deviation. Trained on 8,100 force-closure grasps across 81 objects for the 16-DoF Allegro Hand, our model achieves zero friction violations, the best composite score, and the lowest wrench residual among all ablation variants. We retarget decoded fingertip contacts to a 16-DoF LEAP Hand via per-finger inverse kinematics, and our hardware-feasible refinement places every joint at least 5% inside its actuator envelope while preserving wrench balance. On the physical robot, retargeted EquiDexFlow-decoded grasps complete open-loop pick-and-hold trials on all six test objects, with every asymmetric object succeeding at both the canonical pose and a $120^\circ$ co-rotation. Videos, code, and checkpoints are available at https://equidexflow.github.io.

</details>


### [370] [Bounding Boxes as Goals: Language-Conditioned Grasping via Neuro-Symbolic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12910)
*Allison Andreyev,Landon Eum,Nestor Tiglao,Romel Gomez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GRASP是一个利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询转化为神经符号目标状态的框架，通过边界框检测实现物理世界接地，无需任务特定训练即可完成开放词汇桌面操作任务，在90次真实机器人试验中达到73.3%的整体成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM驱动的机器人任务与运动规划方法要么计算负担重，要么需要大量示范训练，缺乏轻量级且无需微调的开放词汇操作方案。

Method: 利用预训练VLM将自然语言查询翻译为神经符号目标状态，通过边界框检测管线实现物理世界接地，使机器人能够解释"顶层架子"等抽象空间概念并执行任务，无需额外微调。

Result: 在90次真实机器人试验中，三个难度级别上达到73.3%的整体成功率，无需任务特定训练。

Conclusion: GRASP展示了向开放词汇桌面操作迈进的可行路径，在保持计算轻量的同时实现了零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: For robotics to be effectively integrated into household or industrial environments, machines must adapt to natural-language prompts in real time. Although Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have enabled zero-shot generalization in robot task and motion planning (TAMP), current state-of-the-art approaches often remain computationally "heavyweight" or require extensive training on thousands of demonstrations. We present GRASP (Grounded Reasoning and Symbolic Planning), a framework designed as a step toward open-vocabulary tabletop manipulation. Our approach leverages a pretrained VLM to translate natural-language queries into neuro-symbolic goal states, grounded in the physical world via a bounding-box detection pipeline. Unlike methods that rely on fixed color lists or hard-coded coordinates, GRASP enables robots to interpret abstract spatial concepts such as "top shelf" and execute tasks without additional fine-tuning. We achieve 73.3% overall success across 90 real-robot trials at three difficulty levels, requiring no task-specific training.

</details>


### [371] [Improving Robotic Generalist Policies via Flow Reversal Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13675)
*Andy Tang,William Chen,Andrew Wagenmaker,Chelsea Finn,Sergey Levine*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)方法，通过将次优动作反向映射到流匹配通用策略的潜在噪声空间，再重映射到专家动作模式，实现对通用机器人策略的零样本控制优化与快速微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略虽具备丰富行为先验，但在面对新任务时，如何从策略中推理并调用合适的动作仍然困难，尤其是直接指令控制失败的情况下。

Method: 提出Flow Reversal Steering (FRS)：将次优但"合理"的动作通过流策略反向传递找到其潜在噪声，再将其映射到附近通用策略的专家动作模式，从而改善动作质量。

Result: FRS能将来自人类或视觉语言模型的粗略语义指导转化为良好的机器人动作，提升零样本控制效果；通过行为克隆训练辅助策略输出噪声（不到1分钟训练），可实现高达95%的绝对任务成功率提升；FRS还能引导强化学习在标准RL无法改进的任务上取得提升。

Conclusion: FRS是一种有效利用通用策略行为先验的方法，能够在零样本控制、快速微调以及强化学习引导等场景下显著提升机器人任务表现。

Abstract: Generalist policies can learn a wide range of skills from diverse robot datasets. In order to solve or improve on challenging new tasks, we need a way to infer and invoke the appropriate actions from the policy's rich behavioral prior, especially when directly commanding the policy fails. We focus on flow matching generalists and propose Flow Reversal Steering (FRS): a method that takes suboptimal but ``reasonable'' actions, finds their latent noises by passing them through the flow policy in reverse, and maps them to nearby generalist action modes. We evaluate FRS across many simulated and real-world manipulation settings. First, FRS can turn coarse semantic guidance from humans or vision-language models (VLMs) into corresponding good robot actions, improving zero-shot control. These gains can be distilled with behavioral cloning by training an auxiliary policy to output noises that the generalist maps to good actions -- showing up to 95% absolute task success rate boosts in under a minute of training. Finally, FRS enables policy improvement by bootstrapping reinforcement learning with semantic knowledge, improving on several tasks that standard RL fails to improve on.

</details>
